=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\BattleDebugInspectors.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    [CustomEditor(typeof(BattleSession))]
    public sealed class BattleSessionInspector : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        public override bool RequiresConstantRepaint() => Application.isPlaying;
        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            DrawDefaultInspector();
            var session = (BattleSession)target;
            if (GUILayout.Button("Validate Scene"))
            {
                var errors = session.ValidateConfiguration();
                if (errors.Count == 0) Debug.Log("Battle Scene is valid.", session);
                else Debug.LogError(string.Join("\n", errors), session);
            }
            if (Application.isPlaying && GUILayout.Button("Start / Restart Battle")) session.StartBattle();
            var simulation = session.Simulation;
            if (simulation == null) return;
            EditorGUILayout.Space();
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"{simulation.Outcome} | {simulation.ElapsedTime:F2}s | Lives {simulation.LivesRemaining}");
            EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Spawned {simulation.SpawnedCount}  Alive {simulation.AliveCount}  Dead {simulation.KilledCount}  Goal {simulation.ReachedGoalCount}");
            foreach (var tower in simulation.Towers)
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Tower {tower.Id}: HP {tower.Hp:F1}, cooldown {tower.CooldownRemaining:F2}, target {tower.TargetId?.ToString() ?? "-"}, {tower.Position}");
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"#{unit.MemberId} {unit.Role} {unit.LifeState} | HP {unit.Hp:F1}/{unit.MaxHp:F1} | {unit.Position} | Squad {unit.SquadId} Slot {unit.SlotId} Cell {unit.OccupiedCell}");
        }
    }

    [CustomEditor(typeof(BattleTower))]
    public sealed class BattleTowerInspector : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        public override bool RequiresConstantRepaint() => Application.isPlaying;
        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            DrawDefaultInspector();
            var state = ((BattleTower)target).RuntimeState;
            if (state != null) EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Cooldown {state.CooldownRemaining:F2} | Target {state.TargetId?.ToString() ?? "-"}");
        }
    }

    public static class BattleDebugLabels
    {
        [DrawGizmo(GizmoType.Selected | GizmoType.NonSelected)]
        private static void DrawLabels(BattleDebugGizmos view, GizmoType type)
        {
            if (!view.enabled || view.Session == null) return;
            var session = view.Session;
            if (session.Route != null)
            {
                for (var i = 0; i < session.Route.Cells.Count; i++)
                {
                    var cell = session.Route.Cells[i];
                    if (cell != null) Handles.Label(cell.TopCenter + Vector3.up * 0.1f, $"{i}: {cell.Kind}");
                }
            }
            foreach (var tower in session.Towers)
                if (tower != null) Handles.Label(tower.transform.position + Vector3.up, $"{tower.name} R={tower.Range:F1} CD={tower.CurrentCooldown:F1}");
            if (session.Simulation == null) return;
            foreach (var unit in session.Simulation.Units)
                if (unit.IsAlive) Handles.Label(unit.Position + Vector3.up * 0.6f, $"#{unit.Id} HP {unit.Hp:F0}");
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\BattleGridInspector.cs === 
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    [CustomEditor(typeof(BattleGrid))]
    public sealed class BattleGridInspector : UnityEditor.Editor
    {
        private Vector3Int macro;
        public override bool RequiresConstantRepaint() => Application.isPlaying;
        public override void OnInspectorGUI()
        {
            DrawDefaultInspector();
            var grid = (BattleGrid)target;
            macro = EditorGUILayout.Vector3IntField("Macro (X, layer, Z)", macro);
            var errors = new System.Collections.Generic.List<string>();
            grid.RebuildIndex(errors);
            var session = Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleSession>(FindObjectsSortMode.None).FirstOrDefault(value => value.Grid == grid);
            var cells = grid.GetMacroCells(macro);
            var occupancy = session?.Simulation == null ? null : grid.GetMacroOccupancy(macro, session.Simulation);
            for (var i = 0; i < cells.Length; i++)
            {
                EditorGUILayout.ObjectField($"Small ({i % 3},{i / 3})", cells[i], typeof(BattleCell), true);
                if (occupancy != null)
                    EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Towers: {string.Join(",", occupancy[i].TowerIds)}  Units: {string.Join(",", occupancy[i].UnitIds)}");
            }
            foreach (var error in errors) EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(error, MessageType.Error);
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    public static class GameMenuScenes
    {
        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Start Game")]
        public static void OpenStart() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open End Game")]
        public static void OpenEnd() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.EndScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Upgrade Scene")]
        public static void OpenUpgrade() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 1 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelOne() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 2 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelTwo() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 3 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelThree() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelThreeScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 4 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelFour() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelFourScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 5 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelFive() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelFiveScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 6 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelSix() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelSixScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Open Level 7 Grid Battle")]
        public static void OpenLevelSeven() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.LevelSevenScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Test/Open Test Start Scene")]
        public static void OpenTestStart() => Open(BattleSceneFlow.TestStartScene);

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Generate Levels 5, 6 & 7 + Rebalance Economy")]
        public static void GenerateExpansionLevelsAndEconomy()
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning("Exit Play Mode before generating levels.");
                return;
            }
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo()) return;

            UpdateEconomyAsset();
            BuildAndSaveLevel(5, BattleSceneFlow.LevelFiveScene);
            BuildAndSaveLevel(6, BattleSceneFlow.LevelSixScene);
            BuildAndSaveLevel(7, BattleSceneFlow.LevelSevenScene);
            EnsureBuildSettingsScenes();

            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            AssetDatabase.Refresh();
            Debug.Log("Successfully generated Level 5, Level 6, and Level 7 (with AOE towers) and rebalanced RebuildEconomy.asset!");
        }

        private static void UpdateEconomyAsset()
        {
            var economy = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameEconomySettings>("Assets/Rebuild/Resources/RebuildEconomy.asset");
            if (economy == null) return;
            var so = new SerializedObject(economy);
            so.FindProperty("startingGold").intValue = 35;
            so.FindProperty("baseSummonCost").intValue = 30;
            so.FindProperty("summonCostIncreasePerPull").intValue = 15;
            var baseBounty = so.FindProperty("baseTowerBounty");
            if (baseBounty != null) baseBounty.intValue = 25;
            var aoeBounty = so.FindProperty("aoeTowerBounty");
            if (aoeBounty != null) aoeBounty.intValue = 45;

            var rewards = so.FindProperty("levelRewards");
            int[] stageGolds = { 45, 65, 90, 120, 155, 195, 240 };
            rewards.arraySize = stageGolds.Length;
            for (var i = 0; i < stageGolds.Length; i++)
            {
                var elem = rewards.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i);
                elem.FindPropertyRelative("level").intValue = i + 1;
                elem.FindPropertyRelative("gold").intValue = stageGolds[i];
            }
            so.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            EditorUtility.SetDirty(economy);
        }

        private static void BuildAndSaveLevel(int levelNumber, string destinationPath)
        {
            var draft = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<MapDraft>();
            draft.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
            try
            {
                var dirTower = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(MapBuilderUtility.Kit + "Towers/Tower_Attack_1x1.prefab");
                var sqTower = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(MapBuilderUtility.Kit + "Towers/Tower_Attack_1x1_Square.prefab");

                draft.ResetGrid(24, 18);
                draft.levelNumber = levelNumber;
                for (var i = 0; i < draft.tiles.Length; i++) draft.tiles[i] = (int)CellKind.Ground;

                if (levelNumber == 5)
                {
                    // S-Bend Canyon with 2 AOE Towers + 3 Directional Towers
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(1, 4), new Vector2Int(10, 4), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(10, 4), new Vector2Int(10, 13), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(10, 13), new Vector2Int(22, 13), CellKind.Road);
                    draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(1, 4), (int)CellKind.Spawn, 3);
                    draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(22, 13), (int)CellKind.Goal, 3);

                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(1, 4));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(10, 4));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(10, 13));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(22, 13));

                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(6, 7), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 50);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(7, 1), dirTower, AttackArea.PositiveZ, isAoe: false, bounty: 30);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(13, 9), dirTower, AttackArea.NegativeX, isAoe: false, bounty: 35);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(16, 16), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 55);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(19, 10), dirTower, AttackArea.PositiveZ, isAoe: false, bounty: 35);
                }
                else if (levelNumber == 6)
                {
                    // Zig-Zag Bastion with 3 AOE Towers + 3 Directional Towers
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(1, 13), new Vector2Int(7, 13), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(7, 13), new Vector2Int(7, 4), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(7, 4), new Vector2Int(16, 4), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(16, 4), new Vector2Int(16, 13), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(16, 13), new Vector2Int(22, 13), CellKind.Road);
                    draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(1, 13), (int)CellKind.Spawn, 3);
                    draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(22, 13), (int)CellKind.Goal, 3);

                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(1, 13));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(7, 13));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(7, 4));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(16, 4));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(16, 13));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(22, 13));

                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(4, 10), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 55);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(10, 10), dirTower, AttackArea.NegativeX, isAoe: false, bounty: 35);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(11, 7), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 60);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(13, 1), dirTower, AttackArea.PositiveZ, isAoe: false, bounty: 35);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(19, 7), dirTower, AttackArea.NegativeX, isAoe: false, bounty: 40);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(19, 10), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 60);
                }
                else
                {
                    // Stage 7 Citadel Finale with 4 AOE Towers + 4 Directional Towers
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(1, 4), new Vector2Int(7, 4), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(7, 4), new Vector2Int(7, 13), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(7, 13), new Vector2Int(16, 13), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(16, 13), new Vector2Int(16, 4), CellKind.Road);
                    PaintMacroLine(draft, new Vector2Int(16, 4), new Vector2Int(22, 4), CellKind.Road);
                    draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(1, 4), (int)CellKind.Spawn, 3);
                    draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(22, 4), (int)CellKind.Goal, 3);

                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(1, 4));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(7, 4));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(7, 13));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(16, 13));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(16, 4));
                    draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(22, 4));

                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(4, 7), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 65);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(4, 1), dirTower, AttackArea.PositiveZ, isAoe: false, bounty: 40);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(10, 9), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 65);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(11, 16), dirTower, AttackArea.NegativeZ, isAoe: false, bounty: 45);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(13, 10), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 70);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(13, 7), dirTower, AttackArea.PositiveX, isAoe: false, bounty: 45);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(19, 7), sqTower, AttackArea.Square, isAoe: true, bounty: 75);
                    PlacePlatformAndTower(draft, new Vector2Int(19, 1), dirTower, AttackArea.PositiveZ, isAoe: false, bounty: 45);
                }

                var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
                MapBuilderUtility.Populate(draft, scene);
                EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, destinationPath);
            }
            finally
            {
                Object.DestroyImmediate(draft);
            }
        }

        private static void PaintMacroLine(MapDraft draft, Vector2Int from, Vector2Int to, CellKind kind)
        {
            var step = new Vector2Int(System.Math.Sign(to.x - from.x), System.Math.Sign(to.y - from.y));
            for (var p = from; ; p += step * 3)
            {
                draft.Paint(p, (int)kind, 3);
                if (p == to) break;
            }
        }

        private static void PlacePlatformAndTower(MapDraft draft, Vector2Int cell, GameObject prefab, AttackArea area, bool isAoe, int bounty)
        {
            draft.Paint(cell, (int)CellKind.TowerPlatform, 1);
            draft.PlaceTower(cell, prefab, area, isAoe, bounty);
        }

        private static void EnsureBuildSettingsScenes()
        {
            string[] required =
            {
                BattleSceneFlow.StartScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.LevelThreeScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.LevelFourScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.LevelFiveScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.LevelSixScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.LevelSevenScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene,
                BattleSceneFlow.EndScene
            };
            var list = new List<EditorBuildSettingsScene>(EditorBuildSettings.scenes);
            foreach (var path in required)
            {
                if (!File.Exists(path)) continue;
                var idx = list.FindIndex(s => s.path == path);
                if (idx >= 0) list[idx].enabled = true;
                else list.Add(new EditorBuildSettingsScene(path, true));
            }
            EditorBuildSettings.scenes = list.ToArray();
        }

        private static void Open(string path)
        {
            if (Application.isPlaying)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning("Exit Play Mode before opening a scene.");
                return;
            }
            if (EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo())
                EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(path);
        }
    }
}
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\WebGLBuild.cs === 
using System.Linq;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor
{
    // Builds the GitHub Pages player into docs/beta. CLI: -executeMethod Team5.Rebuild.Editor.WebGLBuild.BuildBeta
    public static class WebGLBuild
    {
        public const string OutputPath = "docs/beta";

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Build WebGL Beta (docs/beta)")]
        public static void BuildBeta()
        {
            var scenes = EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Where(scene => scene.enabled).Select(scene => scene.path).ToArray();
            var report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(new BuildPlayerOptions
            {
                scenes = scenes,
                locationPathName = OutputPath,
                target = BuildTarget.WebGL,
                options = BuildOptions.None
            });
            var summary = report.summary;
            Debug.Log($"WebGL build {summary.result}: {summary.totalErrors} errors, {summary.totalSize / (1024 * 1024)} MB, {summary.totalTime}");
            if (Application.isBatchMode) EditorApplication.Exit(summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded ? 0 : 1);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\LevelAuthoringWindow.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    public sealed class LevelAuthoringWindow : EditorWindow
    {
        private List<LevelValidationIssue> issues;
        private Vector2 scroll;
        private int number = 1;
        private BattleSession selectedSession;

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Level Authoring (Stage 1)")]
        public static void Open() => GetWindow<LevelAuthoringWindow>("Level Authoring");
        private void OnEnable() => Undo.undoRedoPerformed += Refresh;
        private void OnDisable() => Undo.undoRedoPerformed -= Refresh;
        private void OnHierarchyChange() => Refresh();
        private void Refresh() { issues = null; selectedSession = null; Repaint(); }

        private void OnGUI()
        {
            scroll = EditorGUILayout.BeginScrollView(scroll);
            EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Stage 1: scene template, safe copies, basic validation and Undo. Map brushes are not included.", MessageType.Info);
            if (GUILayout.Button("Open 2D Map Builder (custom map size)")) MapBuilderWindow.Open();
            using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode))
            {
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Level Template", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
                using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(true))
                    EditorGUILayout.ObjectField("Template", AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<SceneAsset>(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath), typeof(SceneAsset), false);
                if (GUILayout.Button("Open LevelTemplate"))
                {
                    if (EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo())
                        EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath);
                }
                if (GUILayout.Button("Create Level from Template…")) CreateCopy();
                EditorGUILayout.Space();
                var scene = SceneManager.GetActiveScene();
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Active Scene", scene.name);
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Asset Path", string.IsNullOrEmpty(scene.path) ? "Not saved yet" : scene.path);
                var sessions = scene.IsValid() && scene.isLoaded ? LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(scene) : Array.Empty<BattleSession>();
                var session = sessions.Length == 1 ? sessions[0] : null;
                if (session != selectedSession) { selectedSession = session; number = session != null ? session.DisplayedLevelNumber : 1; }
                var template = LevelTemplateUtility.IsTemplate(scene.path);
                if (template) EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("The template is read-only in this tool. Create a copy to edit level settings. You can still validate it or use Unity Play.", MessageType.Info);
                using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(template || session == null))
                {
                    number = EditorGUILayout.IntField("Displayed Level Number", number);
                    if (GUILayout.Button("Apply Level Number (Undo)"))
                    {
                        try { LevelEditScope.SetDisplayedLevelNumber(session, number); }
                        catch (Exception error) { EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Level settings", error.Message, "OK"); }
                    }
                }
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y undo or redo a scene edit. Save the scene with Ctrl+S.", EditorStyles.wordWrappedMiniLabel);
                if (GUILayout.Button("Validate Active Level")) issues = LevelValidation.Validate(scene);
                if (issues != null)
                {
                    if (!issues.Any(issue => issue.IsError)) EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Basic validation passed. This does not register the scene with a game flow.", MessageType.Info);
                    foreach (var issue in issues)
                    {
                        EditorGUILayout.HelpBox(issue.Message, issue.IsError ? MessageType.Error : MessageType.Warning);
                        if (issue.Context != null && GUILayout.Button("Select: " + issue.Context.name))
                        { Selection.activeObject = issue.Context; EditorGUIUtility.PingObject(issue.Context); }
                    }
                }
                EditorGUILayout.Space();
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Future Test Target Interface", EditorStyles.boldLabel);
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Stage 1 defines GUID-based scene references and provider/selection contracts only. Set as Test Level is not connected yet. The existing test flow is unchanged.", MessageType.Info);
                EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Current legacy target", BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene);
            }
            EditorGUILayout.EndScrollView();
        }

        private void CreateCopy()
        {
            var path = EditorUtility.SaveFilePanelInProject("Create Level from Template", "NewLevel", "unity", "Choose a NEW scene name. Existing files and TestLevel are protected.", "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path)) return;
            try
            {
                var asset = LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(path);
                Selection.activeObject = asset;
                EditorGUIUtility.PingObject(asset);
                if (EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Level created", "Created " + AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(asset) + ".\nThe test target and build list were not changed. Open the new scene?", "Open", "Keep current scene") &&
                    EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo())
                    EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(asset));
            }
            catch (Exception error) { EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Level not created", error.Message, "OK"); }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\LevelEditScope.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    // One user action = one Undo group. Scene-only; never records shared project assets.
    public sealed class LevelEditScope : IDisposable
    {
        private readonly Scene scene;
        private readonly int group;
        private readonly string label;
        private readonly HashSet<Object> recorded = new HashSet<Object>();
        private bool committed, disposed;

        public LevelEditScope(Scene scene, string label)
        {
            if (EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode || !scene.IsValid() || !scene.isLoaded)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Level edits require a loaded scene in Edit Mode.");
            if (LevelTemplateUtility.IsTemplate(scene.path))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Create a copy before editing the template.");
            var stage = PrefabStageUtility.GetCurrentPrefabStage();
            if (stage != null && stage.scene == scene)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Edit a level scene, not a prefab asset.");
            this.scene = scene;
            this.label = label;
            Undo.IncrementCurrentGroup();
            group = Undo.GetCurrentGroup();
            Undo.SetCurrentGroupName(label);
        }

        private void Check(Object target)
        {
            if (disposed || committed) throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(LevelEditScope));
            var go = target as GameObject ?? (target as Component)?.gameObject;
            if (go == null || EditorUtility.IsPersistent(target) || go.scene != scene)
                throw new ArgumentException("Only objects belonging to this level scene can be edited.");
        }

        public void Record(Object target)
        {
            Check(target);
            if (recorded.Add(target)) Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(target, label);
        }

        public void RegisterCreated(GameObject target)
        {
            Check(target);
            Undo.RegisterCreatedObjectUndo(target, label);
        }

        public void Destroy(GameObject target)
        {
            Check(target);
            Undo.DestroyObjectImmediate(target);
        }

        public void Commit()
        {
            if (disposed || committed) throw new ObjectDisposedException(nameof(LevelEditScope));
            foreach (var target in recorded)
                if (target != null && PrefabUtility.IsPartOfPrefabInstance(target))
                    PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(target);
            EditorSceneManager.MarkSceneDirty(scene);
            Undo.CollapseUndoOperations(group);
            committed = true;
        }

        public void Dispose()
        {
            if (disposed) return;
            if (!committed) Undo.RevertAllDownToGroup(group);
            disposed = true;
        }

        public static void SetDisplayedLevelNumber(BattleSession session, int number)
        {
            if (session == null || number < 1) throw new ArgumentException("A session and positive level number are required.");
            if (session.DisplayedLevelNumber == number) return;
            using (var edit = new LevelEditScope(session.gameObject.scene, "Set Level Number"))
            {
                edit.Record(session);
                var serialized = new SerializedObject(session);
                serialized.FindProperty("displayedLevelNumber").intValue = number;
                serialized.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
                edit.Commit();
            }
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\LevelTemplateUtility.cs === 
using System;
using System.IO;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    public static class LevelTemplateUtility
    {
        public const string TemplateScenePath = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/LevelTemplate.unity";
        public const string ReservedTestScenePath = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/TestLevel.unity";

        public static bool IsTemplate(string path) => string.Equals(path, TemplateScenePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        // Creates an asset only. Does not open/save scenes, change the build list or select a test target.
        public static SceneAsset CreateLevelFromTemplate(string destination)
        {
            if (EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Exit Play Mode before creating a level.");
            var path = NormalizeDestination(destination);
            if (AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<SceneAsset>(TemplateScenePath) == null)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("LevelTemplate.unity is missing.");
            if (!AssetDatabase.CopyAsset(TemplateScenePath, path))
                throw new IOException("Unity could not copy the template to " + path);
            AssetDatabase.ImportAsset(path, ImportAssetOptions.ForceSynchronousImport);
            return AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<SceneAsset>(path)
                ?? throw new IOException("The copied scene could not be imported: " + path);
        }

        public static string NormalizeDestination(string destination)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destination) || Path.IsPathRooted(destination))
                throw new ArgumentException("Choose a project-relative .unity path inside Assets.");
            var project = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(Application.dataPath, ".."));
            var full = Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(project, destination));
            var assets = Path.GetFullPath(Application.dataPath) + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
            if (!full.StartsWith(assets, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                !string.Equals(Path.GetExtension(full), ".unity", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                throw new ArgumentException("The new scene must be a .unity file inside Assets.");
            var path = full.Substring(project.Length + 1).Replace('\\', '/');
            if (IsTemplate(path) || string.Equals(path, ReservedTestScenePath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("LevelTemplate and TestLevel are protected destinations. Choose a new scene name.");
            if (!Directory.Exists(Path.GetDirectoryName(full)))
                throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Choose an existing project folder.");
            if (File.Exists(full) || File.Exists(full + ".meta") || Directory.Exists(full) ||
                AssetDatabase.LoadMainAssetAtPath(path) != null)
                throw new IOException("The destination already exists. Existing scenes are never overwritten.");
            return path;
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\LevelTestTarget.cs === 
using System;
using UnityEditor;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    // Contract only in stage 1. No active-target storage and no runtime-flow migration.
    // This identifies a Scene asset; it never stores terrain, route or tower layout data.
    public readonly struct LevelSceneReference
    {
        public string SceneGuid { get; }
        private LevelSceneReference(string guid) => SceneGuid = guid;

        public static bool TryFromAsset(SceneAsset scene, out LevelSceneReference reference)
        {
            var path = scene != null ? AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(scene) : "";
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path) || !path.EndsWith(".unity", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            { reference = default; return false; }
            reference = new LevelSceneReference(AssetDatabase.AssetPathToGUID(path));
            return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(reference.SceneGuid);
        }

        // Resolve on demand so renaming/moving the asset does not leave a stale path.
        public bool TryResolvePath(out string scenePath)
        {
            scenePath = string.IsNullOrEmpty(SceneGuid) ? "" : AssetDatabase.GUIDToAssetPath(SceneGuid);
            return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(scenePath) && AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<SceneAsset>(scenePath) != null;
        }
    }

    public interface ILevelTestTargetProvider
    {
        bool TryGetTestTarget(out LevelSceneReference target);
    }

    public interface ILevelTestTargetSelection : ILevelTestTargetProvider
    {
        // Future explicit selection action. Must not copy, rename or overwrite scene contents.
        // A later runtime adapter will receive a build-loadable path, never a UnityEditor type.
        void SetTestTarget(LevelSceneReference target);
    }
}
