=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\LevelValidation.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    public readonly struct LevelValidationIssue
    {
        public string Message { get; }
        public Object Context { get; }
        public bool IsError { get; }
        public LevelValidationIssue(string message, Object context = null, bool isError = true)
        { Message = message; Context = context; IsError = isError; }
    }

    public static class LevelValidation
    {
        public static T[] Components<T>(Scene scene) where T : Component =>
            scene.GetRootGameObjects().SelectMany(root => root.GetComponentsInChildren<T>(true)).ToArray();

        // Reads the scene and temporary lookup indices. Does not save or repair anything.
        public static List<LevelValidationIssue> Validate(Scene scene)
        {
            var issues = new List<LevelValidationIssue>();
            if (!scene.IsValid() || !scene.isLoaded)
            { issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("Open a level scene first.")); return issues; }
            var sessions = Components<BattleSession>(scene);
            if (sessions.Length != 1)
            { issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("The scene must contain exactly one BattleSession.")); return issues; }
            var session = sessions[0];
            if (!session.isActiveAndEnabled) issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("BattleSession must be active and enabled.", session));
            try
            {
                issues.AddRange(session.ValidateConfiguration().Select(message => new LevelValidationIssue(message, session)));
                if (!issues.Any(issue => issue.IsError))
                    _ = new BattleSimulation(session.CaptureDefinition()); // Constructor also checks full formation clearance.
            }
            catch (Exception error) when (error is ArgumentException || error is InvalidOperationException)
            { issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue(error.Message, session)); }
            if (session.Grid != null)
            {
                var owned = new HashSet<BattleCell>(session.Grid.SceneCells);
                foreach (var cell in Components<BattleCell>(scene))
                    if (!owned.Contains(cell)) issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("BattleCell is outside the session's GameplayGrid.", cell));
            }
            var towers = new HashSet<BattleTower>(session.Towers);
            foreach (var tower in Components<BattleTower>(scene))
                if (!towers.Contains(tower)) issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("Tower is not registered in BattleSession → Towers.", tower));
            var cameras = Components<Camera>(scene).Where(camera => camera.isActiveAndEnabled && camera.CompareTag("MainCamera")).ToArray();
            if (cameras.Length != 1)
                issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("Expected one enabled MainCamera for unit inspection.", null, false));
            var presentations = Components<BattlePresentation>(scene);
            if (presentations.Length == 0)
                issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("No BattlePresentation: simulation can run, but units will not be displayed.", session, false));
            foreach (var view in presentations)
                if (view.Session != session || view.UnitPrefab == null)
                    issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("Presentation needs this session and a UnitView prefab.", view));
            foreach (var hud in Components<LegacyBattleHud>(scene))
                if (hud.Session != session) issues.Add(new LevelValidationIssue("HUD is bound to another session.", hud));
            return issues;
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\MapBuilderUtility.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    public static class MapBuilderUtility
    {
        public const string Kit = "Assets/Rebuild/Prefabs/LevelBuilding/";
        private static void RequireEditMode()
        {
            if (EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode) throw new InvalidOperationException("Exit Play Mode first.");
        }

        public static BattleSession Populate(MapDraft draft, Scene scene)
        {
            RequireEditMode();
            if (!scene.IsValid() || !scene.isLoaded || scene.rootCount != 0)
                throw new ArgumentException("Generation requires a new empty scene.");
            if (draft == null || draft.tiles.Length != draft.width * draft.length)
                throw new ArgumentException("Create a grid first.");
            var rig = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(Kit + "Systems/BattleRig_3D.prefab");
            if (rig == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("BattleRig_3D prefab is missing.");
            var host = (GameObject)PrefabUtility.InstantiatePrefab(rig, scene);
            host.name = "Map Builder Level";
            var session = host.GetComponentInChildren<BattleSession>(true);
            var cells = new Dictionary<Vector2Int, BattleCell>();
            var prefabs = Enum.GetValues(typeof(CellKind)).Cast<CellKind>().ToDictionary(kind => kind,
                kind => AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(Kit + "Tiles/" + kind + "_Cell.prefab"));
            for (var z = 0; z < draft.length; z++)
                for (var x = 0; x < draft.width; x++)
                {
                    var p = new Vector2Int(x, z); var value = draft.Tile(p);
                    if (value == -1) continue;
                    if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(CellKind), value) || prefabs[(CellKind)value] == null)
                        throw new ArgumentException($"Unknown tile at ({x}, {z}).");
                    var go = (GameObject)PrefabUtility.InstantiatePrefab(prefabs[(CellKind)value], scene);
                    go.transform.SetParent(session.Grid.transform, false);
                    go.transform.position = draft.Position(p);
                    go.name = $"{(CellKind)value} ({x}, {z})";
                    PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(go.transform);
                    cells.Add(p, go.GetComponent<BattleCell>());
                }
            var route = new SerializedObject(session.Route);
            var entries = route.FindProperty("cells"); entries.arraySize = draft.route.Count;
            for (var i = 0; i < draft.route.Count; i++)
            {
                if (!cells.TryGetValue(draft.route[i], out var cell)) throw new ArgumentException("Route contains an empty cell.");
                entries.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i).objectReferenceValue = cell;
            }
            route.FindProperty("requiredWidth").intValue = 3;
            route.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            var settings = new SerializedObject(session);
            var towers = settings.FindProperty("towers"); towers.arraySize = draft.towers.Count;
            for (var i = 0; i < draft.towers.Count; i++)
            {
                var stamp = draft.towers[i];
                if (stamp.prefab == null || !PrefabUtility.IsPartOfPrefabAsset(stamp.prefab)) throw new ArgumentException("Tower needs a prefab asset.");
                var go = (GameObject)PrefabUtility.InstantiatePrefab(stamp.prefab, scene);
                go.transform.SetParent(host.transform, false);
                go.transform.position = draft.Position(stamp.cell) + Vector3.up * .25f;
                PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(go.transform);
                var tower = go.GetComponent<BattleTower>();
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stamp.configuration)) JsonUtility.FromJsonOverwrite(stamp.configuration, tower);
                var serialized = new SerializedObject(tower);
                serialized.FindProperty("attackArea").enumValueIndex = (int)stamp.area;
                var isAoeProp = serialized.FindProperty("isAoe");
                if (isAoeProp != null) isAoeProp.boolValue = stamp.isAoe;
                var bountyProp = serialized.FindProperty("goldBounty");
                if (bountyProp != null) bountyProp.intValue = stamp.goldBounty > 0 ? stamp.goldBounty : (stamp.isAoe ? 40 : 25);
                serialized.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
                towers.GetArrayElementAtIndex(i).objectReferenceValue = tower;
            }
            settings.FindProperty("autoStart").boolValue = true;
            settings.FindProperty("useSceneRosterOnly").boolValue = false;
            settings.FindProperty("displayedLevelNumber").intValue = Mathf.Max(1, draft.levelNumber);
            settings.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            FrameGeneratedCamera(host, draft);
            foreach (var component in host.GetComponentsInChildren<MonoBehaviour>(true))
                PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(component);
            return session;
        }

        private static void FrameGeneratedCamera(GameObject host, MapDraft draft)
        {
            var camera = host.GetComponentInChildren<Camera>(true);
            if (camera == null) return;
            var center = draft.Position(Vector2Int.zero) + new Vector3((draft.width - 1) * .5f, 1.5f, (draft.length - 1) * .5f);
            var halfSize = new Vector3(draft.width * .5f, 2, draft.length * .5f);
            var inverse = Quaternion.Inverse(camera.transform.rotation);
            var vertical = Mathf.Tan(camera.fieldOfView * .5f * Mathf.Deg2Rad);
            var horizontal = vertical * (16f / 9);
            var distance = 10f; var ortho = 1f;
            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
                for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
                    for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
                    {
                        var corner = inverse * Vector3.Scale(halfSize, new Vector3(x, y, z));
                        distance = Mathf.Max(distance, Mathf.Abs(corner.x) / horizontal - corner.z, Mathf.Abs(corner.y) / vertical - corner.z);
                        ortho = Mathf.Max(ortho, Mathf.Abs(corner.y), Mathf.Abs(corner.x) / (16f / 9));
                    }
            distance = distance * 1.15f + 2;
            camera.transform.position = center - camera.transform.forward * distance;
            if (camera.orthographic) camera.orthographicSize = ortho * 1.15f;
            camera.farClipPlane = Mathf.Max(camera.farClipPlane, distance + halfSize.magnitude + 10);
            PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(camera.transform);
            PrefabUtility.RecordPrefabInstancePropertyModifications(camera);
        }

        public static List<string> Validate(MapDraft draft)
        {
            RequireEditMode();
            var preview = EditorSceneManager.NewPreviewScene();
            try
            {
                Populate(draft, preview);
                return LevelValidation.Validate(preview).Select(issue => (issue.IsError ? "ERROR: " : "WARNING: ") + issue.Message).ToList();
            }
            catch (Exception error) { return new List<string> { "ERROR: " + error.Message }; }
            finally { EditorSceneManager.ClosePreviewScene(preview); }
        }

        public static Scene Generate(MapDraft draft)
        {
            RequireEditMode();
            if (Validate(draft).Any(message => message.StartsWith("ERROR:"))) throw new InvalidOperationException("Fix map validation errors before generation.");
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo()) throw new OperationCanceledException("Generation cancelled.");
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            try
            {
                Populate(draft, scene);
                foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects()) Undo.RegisterCreatedObjectUndo(root, "Generate Map");
                SceneManager.SetActiveScene(scene);
                EditorSceneManager.MarkSceneDirty(scene);
                return scene;
            }
            catch { EditorSceneManager.CloseScene(scene, true); throw; }
        }

        public static void SaveNew(Scene scene, string destination)
        {
            RequireEditMode();
            var path = LevelTemplateUtility.NormalizeDestination(destination);
            if (!scene.IsValid() || !scene.isLoaded || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(scene.path))
                throw new ArgumentException("Save New only accepts the unsaved generated scene.");
            if (!EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, path)) throw new InvalidOperationException("Scene could not be saved.");
        }

        public static MapDraft Read(Scene scene)
        {
            RequireEditMode();
            var cells = LevelValidation.Components<BattleCell>(scene);
            var sessions = LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(scene);
            if (cells.Length == 0 || sessions.Length != 1) throw new ArgumentException("Open a scene with one session and a nonempty grid.");
            if (cells.Select(c => c.Occupancy.y).Distinct().Count() != 1) throw new ArgumentException("Only single-layer maps can be read by this version.");
            var errors = new List<string>(); sessions[0].Grid.RebuildIndex(errors);
            if (errors.Count != 0) throw new ArgumentException(errors[0]);
            var result = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<MapDraft>();
            result.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
            try
            {
                var minX = cells.Min(c => c.Occupancy.x); var minZ = cells.Min(c => c.Occupancy.z);
                result.ResetGrid(Math.Max(3, cells.Max(c => c.Occupancy.x) - minX + 1), Math.Max(3, cells.Max(c => c.Occupancy.z) - minZ + 1));
                result.originX = minX; result.originZ = minZ; result.layer = cells[0].Occupancy.y;
                result.levelNumber = sessions[0].DisplayedLevelNumber;
                foreach (var cell in cells) result.tiles[(cell.Occupancy.z - minZ) * result.width + cell.Occupancy.x - minX] = (int)cell.Kind;
                foreach (var cell in sessions[0].Route.Cells)
                {
                    if (cell == null) throw new ArgumentException("Repair missing route references before importing.");
                    result.route.Add(new Vector2Int(cell.Occupancy.x - minX, cell.Occupancy.z - minZ));
                }
                foreach (var tower in sessions[0].Towers)
                {
                    var source = PrefabUtility.GetCorrespondingObjectFromSource(tower.gameObject);
                    if (source == null || source.GetComponent<BattleTower>() == null)
                        throw new ArgumentException("Import requires towers created from tower prefabs; custom scene towers are not converted automatically.");
                    var serialized = new SerializedObject(tower);
                    result.towers.Add(new MapTowerStamp
                    {
                        cell = new Vector2Int(Mathf.RoundToInt(tower.transform.position.x) - minX, Mathf.RoundToInt(tower.transform.position.z) - minZ),
                        prefab = source,
                        area = (Team5.Rebuild.Simulation.AttackArea)serialized.FindProperty("attackArea").enumValueIndex,
                        isAoe = tower.IsAoe,
                        goldBounty = tower.GoldBounty,
                        configuration = JsonUtility.ToJson(tower)
                    });
                }
                return result;
            }
            catch { UnityEngine.Object.DestroyImmediate(result); throw; }
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\MapBuilderWindow.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    public sealed class MapBuilderWindow : EditorWindow
    {
        [SerializeField] private MapDraft draft;
        private int width = 24, length = 18, brushSize = 3, tool;
        private float zoom = 26;
        private Vector2 gridScroll, panelScroll;
        [SerializeField] private float footerHeight = 140;
        private Rect splitterRect, gridViewport;
        private float splitStartY, splitStartHeight;
        private float workspaceTop = 280;
        private bool resizingSplitter;
        private GameObject towerPrefab;
        private AttackArea area = AttackArea.NegativeX;
        private bool towerIsAoe;
        private int towerGoldBounty = 25;
        private List<string> messages = new List<string>();
        private bool painting;
        private Vector2Int lastPaint;
        private Vector2Int hover = new Vector2Int(-1, -1);
        private Scene generated;
        private string generatedSnapshot;
        private static readonly string[] Tools = { "Ground", "Road", "Obstacle", "TowerPlatform", "Spawn", "Goal", "Erase tile", "Route", "Place tower", "Remove tower" };
        private static readonly Color[] Colors = { new Color(.28f, .48f, .31f), new Color(.64f, .53f, .32f), new Color(.25f, .26f, .29f), new Color(.18f, .42f, .85f), new Color(.85f, .22f, .25f), new Color(.12f, .73f, .86f) };

        [MenuItem("Team5/Rebuild/Map Builder (2D Grid)")]
        public static void Open() => GetWindow<MapBuilderWindow>("Map Builder");

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (draft == null)
            {
                draft = CreateInstance<MapDraft>(); draft.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
                draft.ResetGrid(width, length);
            }
            Undo.undoRedoPerformed += OnUndo;
            minSize = new Vector2(650, 600);
            wantsMouseMove = true;
            saveChangesMessage = "This grid is a temporary draft. Save it as a Unity Scene before closing, or discard it.";
        }
        private void OnDisable() => Undo.undoRedoPerformed -= OnUndo;
        private void OnDestroy() { if (draft != null) DestroyImmediate(draft); }
        private void OnUndo() { painting = false; messages.Clear(); hasUnsavedChanges = true; Repaint(); }
        private void Changed() { messages.Clear(); hasUnsavedChanges = true; Repaint(); }
        private void Mutate(string label, Action action)
        {
            Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(draft, label);
            try { action(); Changed(); }
            catch (Exception error) { messages = new List<string> { error.Message }; }
        }
        private bool CanReplaceDraft() => !hasUnsavedChanges || EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Replace temporary draft?", "Your unsaved grid edits will be replaced. Saved scenes are unaffected.", "Replace draft", "Cancel");

        private void OnGUI()
        {
            using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(EditorApplication.isPlayingOrWillChangePlaymode))
            {
                HandleSplitterInput();
                using (new GUILayout.AreaScope(new Rect(0, 0, position.width, position.height)))
                using (new EditorGUILayout.VerticalScope())
                {
                    EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Draw a single-layer map, mark the route, then generate a standalone scene. Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Y undo grid edits. Grid drafts exist only in this window.", MessageType.Info);
                    using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                    {
                        width = EditorGUILayout.IntField("Width (X)", width);
                        length = EditorGUILayout.IntField("Length (Z)", length);
                        if (GUILayout.Button("New empty grid") && CanReplaceDraft()) Mutate("New Grid", () => draft.ResetGrid(width, length));
                    }
                    using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                    {
                        EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"Grid: {draft.width} x {draft.length} | Origin: {draft.originX}, {draft.originZ} | Layer: {draft.layer}");
                        var number = Mathf.Max(1, EditorGUILayout.IntField("Level number", draft.levelNumber, GUILayout.Width(210)));
                        if (number != draft.levelNumber) Mutate("Set Draft Level Number", () => draft.levelNumber = number);
                    }
                    tool = GUILayout.SelectionGrid(tool, Tools, 5);
                    using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                    {
                        brushSize = EditorGUILayout.Popup("Brush", brushSize == 3 ? 1 : 0, new[] { "1 x 1", "3 x 3" }) == 1 ? 3 : 1;
                        zoom = EditorGUILayout.Slider("Zoom", zoom, 16, 48);
                        if (GUILayout.Button("Fill grid") && tool < 4 && EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Fill entire grid?", "Replace all tile types? Route and towers remain and will be validated.", "Fill", "Cancel"))
                            Mutate("Fill Grid", () => { for (var i = 0; i < draft.tiles.Length; i++) draft.tiles[i] = tool; });
                    }
                    if (tool == 4 || tool == 5) EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Spawn/Goal mark a 3x3 macro region. They do not create nine independent spawn events. Start/end the route on its center.", MessageType.None);
                    if (tool == 7)
                    {
                        EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Click road centerline points in travel order. Straight segments are filled automatically. Add corners explicitly; paint a full 3-cell-wide corridor first.", MessageType.None);
                        using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                        {
                            if (GUILayout.Button("Undo last route cell") && draft.route.Count > 0) Mutate("Trim Route", () => draft.route.RemoveAt(draft.route.Count - 1));
                            if (GUILayout.Button("Clear route")) Mutate("Clear Route", () => draft.route.Clear());
                        }
                    }
                    if (tool == 8)
                    {
                        towerPrefab = (GameObject)EditorGUILayout.ObjectField("Tower prefab", towerPrefab, typeof(GameObject), false);
                        area = (AttackArea)EditorGUILayout.EnumPopup("Attack direction / area", area);
                        using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                        {
                            towerIsAoe = EditorGUILayout.Toggle("AOE Multi-Target Tower", towerIsAoe);
                            towerGoldBounty = Mathf.Max(0, EditorGUILayout.IntField("Destroy Gold Bounty", towerGoldBounty));
                        }
                        using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                        {
                            if (GUILayout.Button("Directional 1x1"))
                            {
                                towerPrefab = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(MapBuilderUtility.Kit + "Towers/Tower_Attack_1x1.prefab");
                                area = AttackArea.NegativeX;
                                towerIsAoe = false;
                                towerGoldBounty = 25;
                            }
                            if (GUILayout.Button("Square 1x1"))
                            {
                                towerPrefab = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(MapBuilderUtility.Kit + "Towers/Tower_Attack_1x1_Square.prefab");
                                area = AttackArea.Square;
                                towerIsAoe = false;
                                towerGoldBounty = 30;
                            }
                            if (GUILayout.Button("AOE Tower 1x1 (New)"))
                            {
                                towerPrefab = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(MapBuilderUtility.Kit + "Towers/Tower_Attack_1x1_Square.prefab");
                                area = AttackArea.Square;
                                towerIsAoe = true;
                                towerGoldBounty = 45;
                            }
                        }
                        EditorGUILayout.LabelField("Hover: tinted cells show attack coverage. Orange = AOE Tower, Yellow = Single-Target.");
                    }
                    var maxPanelHeight = Mathf.Max(100, position.height - workspaceTop - 128);
                    EditorGUI.BeginChangeCheck();
                    var panelHeight = EditorGUILayout.Slider("Bottom panel height", footerHeight, 100, maxPanelHeight);
                    if (EditorGUI.EndChangeCheck()) { footerHeight = panelHeight; Repaint(); }
                    var headerEnd = GUILayoutUtility.GetRect(0, 0, GUILayout.ExpandWidth(true));
                    if (Event.current.type == EventType.Repaint) workspaceTop = headerEnd.yMax + 2;
                }
                DrawWorkspace();
            }
        }

        private void DrawWorkspace()
        {
            var top = Mathf.Clamp(workspaceTop, 0, Mathf.Max(0, position.height - 228));
            var maxFooter = Mathf.Max(100, position.height - top - 128);
            footerHeight = Mathf.Clamp(footerHeight, 100, maxFooter);
            var dividerY = position.height - footerHeight - 8;
            gridViewport = new Rect(0, top, position.width, Mathf.Max(120, dividerY - top));
            splitterRect = new Rect(0, dividerY, position.width, 8);

            using (new GUILayout.AreaScope(gridViewport)) DrawGrid();

            EditorGUI.DrawRect(splitterRect, new Color(.12f, .12f, .12f));
            EditorGUI.DrawRect(new Rect(splitterRect.center.x - 24, splitterRect.y + 3, 48, 2), new Color(.5f, .5f, .5f));
            EditorGUIUtility.AddCursorRect(splitterRect, MouseCursor.ResizeVertical);
            GUI.Label(splitterRect, new GUIContent("", "Drag to resize the grid and information panels. Double-click to reset."));

            using (new GUILayout.AreaScope(new Rect(0, dividerY + 8, position.width, footerHeight)))
            using (new EditorGUILayout.VerticalScope(GUILayout.Height(footerHeight)))
            {
                using (new EditorGUILayout.HorizontalScope())
                {
                    if (GUILayout.Button("Read layout from active scene…") && CanReplaceDraft())
                    {
                        try
                        {
                            var read = MapBuilderUtility.Read(SceneManager.GetActiveScene());
                            Mutate("Read Scene Layout", () => EditorUtility.CopySerialized(read, draft));
                            DestroyImmediate(read); width = draft.width; length = draft.length;
                        }
                        catch (Exception error) { messages = new List<string> { error.Message }; }
                    }
                    if (GUILayout.Button("Validate map")) messages = MapBuilderUtility.Validate(draft);
                    if (GUILayout.Button("Generate & preview…")) Generate();
                    using (new EditorGUI.DisabledScope(!generated.IsValid() || !generated.isLoaded || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(generated.path) || generatedSnapshot != EditorJsonUtility.ToJson(draft)))
                        if (GUILayout.Button("Save new level…")) SaveGenerated();
                }
                EditorGUILayout.HelpBox("Read layout imports tiles, route and prefab towers only. Generated copies use the standard system rig. Existing scene files and game-flow targets are never overwritten or reassigned.", MessageType.None);
                panelScroll = EditorGUILayout.BeginScrollView(panelScroll, GUILayout.ExpandHeight(true));
                foreach (var message in messages.Take(60)) EditorGUILayout.LabelField(message, EditorStyles.wordWrappedLabel);
                if (messages.Count > 60) EditorGUILayout.LabelField($"… {messages.Count - 60} more issues. Fix the first errors and validate again.");
                EditorGUILayout.EndScrollView();
            }
        }

        private void HandleSplitterInput()
        {
            var id = GUIUtility.GetControlID("MapBuilderSplitter".GetHashCode(), FocusType.Passive);
            var e = Event.current;
            if (!GUI.enabled) return;
            if (e.type == EventType.MouseDown && e.button == 0 && splitterRect.Contains(e.mousePosition))
            {
                painting = false;
                if (e.clickCount == 2)
                {
                    footerHeight = 140;
                    resizingSplitter = false;
                }
                else
                {
                    resizingSplitter = true;
                    GUIUtility.hotControl = id;
                    splitStartY = e.mousePosition.y;
                    splitStartHeight = footerHeight;
                }
                Repaint(); e.Use();
            }
            else if (resizingSplitter && e.type == EventType.MouseDrag)
            {
                footerHeight = Mathf.Clamp(splitStartHeight - (e.mousePosition.y - splitStartY),
                    100, Mathf.Max(100, position.height - workspaceTop - 128));
                Repaint(); e.Use();
            }
            else if (resizingSplitter && e.rawType == EventType.MouseUp)
            {
                resizingSplitter = false;
                if (GUIUtility.hotControl == id) GUIUtility.hotControl = 0;
                if (e.type != EventType.Used) e.Use();
                Repaint();
            }
        }

        private void DrawGrid()
        {
            var pointerInViewport = !resizingSplitter &&
                new Rect(0, 0, gridViewport.width, gridViewport.height).Contains(Event.current.mousePosition);
            gridScroll = EditorGUILayout.BeginScrollView(gridScroll, GUILayout.Height(gridViewport.height), GUILayout.ExpandWidth(true));
            var grid = GUILayoutUtility.GetRect(draft.width * zoom + 28, draft.length * zoom + 28, GUILayout.ExpandWidth(false));
            var e = Event.current;
            hover = new Vector2Int(Mathf.FloorToInt((e.mousePosition.x - grid.x - 28) / zoom), draft.length - 1 - Mathf.FloorToInt((e.mousePosition.y - grid.y - 22) / zoom));
            var preview = towerPrefab != null ? towerPrefab.GetComponent<BattleTower>() : null;
            var attackWidth = preview != null ? new SerializedObject(preview).FindProperty("attackWidth").floatValue : 0;
            var routeLabels = draft.route.Select((point, index) => new { point, index }).GroupBy(entry => entry.point).ToDictionary(group => group.Key, group => group.First().index);
            var towerLabels = draft.towers.GroupBy(tower => tower.cell).ToDictionary(group => group.Key, group => group.First());
            for (var x = 0; x < draft.width; x++) GUI.Label(new Rect(grid.x + 28 + x * zoom, grid.y, zoom, 20), x.ToString(), EditorStyles.miniLabel);
            for (var z = 0; z < draft.length; z++)
            {
                GUI.Label(new Rect(grid.x, grid.y + 22 + (draft.length - 1 - z) * zoom, 28, zoom), z.ToString(), EditorStyles.miniLabel);
                for (var x = 0; x < draft.width; x++)
                {
                    var p = new Vector2Int(x, z);
                    var rect = new Rect(grid.x + 28 + x * zoom, grid.y + 22 + (draft.length - 1 - z) * zoom, zoom - 1, zoom - 1);
                    var value = draft.Tile(p);
                    EditorGUI.DrawRect(rect, value < 0 ? new Color(.17f, .17f, .18f) : Colors[value]);
                    if (tool == 8 && preview != null && draft.Inside(hover) && SquadCombatRules.Contains(new Vector3(x - hover.x, 0, z - hover.y), area, preview.Range,
                        attackWidth))
                    {
                        EditorGUI.DrawRect(rect, towerIsAoe ? new Color(1f, 0.35f, 0.1f, 0.35f) : new Color(1f, .8f, .15f, .23f));
                    }
                    towerLabels.TryGetValue(p, out var tower);
                    if (routeLabels.TryGetValue(p, out var routeIndex)) GUI.Label(rect, routeIndex.ToString(), EditorStyles.whiteMiniLabel);
                    if (tower != null)
                    {
                        var symbol = tower.isAoe ? "✹" : tower.area == AttackArea.Square ? "▣" : tower.area == AttackArea.NegativeX ? "←" : tower.area == AttackArea.PositiveX ? "→" : tower.area == AttackArea.PositiveZ ? "↑" : "↓";
                        GUI.Label(rect, symbol, EditorStyles.whiteBoldLabel);
                    }
                }
            }
            if (e.type == EventType.MouseUp) painting = false;
            if (pointerInViewport && GUIUtility.hotControl == 0 && (e.type == EventType.MouseDown || e.type == EventType.MouseDrag) && e.button == 0 && draft.Inside(hover))
            {
                if (e.type == EventType.MouseDown)
                {
                    Undo.IncrementCurrentGroup(); Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(draft, "Draw Map");
                    painting = true; lastPaint = hover;
                }
                try
                {
                    if (painting && tool <= 6)
                    {
                        var count = Mathf.Max(Mathf.Abs(hover.x - lastPaint.x), Mathf.Abs(hover.y - lastPaint.y));
                        for (var i = 0; i <= count; i++)
                        {
                            var p = count == 0 ? hover : Vector2Int.RoundToInt(Vector2.Lerp(lastPaint, hover, (float)i / count));
                            draft.Paint(p, tool == 6 ? -1 : tool, tool == 4 || tool == 5 ? 3 : brushSize);
                        }
                        lastPaint = hover; Changed();
                    }
                    else if (e.type == EventType.MouseDown)
                    {
                        if (tool == 7) draft.AppendRoute(hover);
                        if (tool == 8) draft.PlaceTower(hover, towerPrefab, area, towerIsAoe, towerGoldBounty);
                        if (tool == 9) draft.towers.RemoveAll(t => Mathf.Abs(t.cell.x - hover.x) <= t.prefab.GetComponent<BattleTower>().FootprintSize / 2 && Mathf.Abs(t.cell.y - hover.y) <= t.prefab.GetComponent<BattleTower>().FootprintSize / 2);
                        Changed();
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception error) { messages = new List<string> { error.Message }; }
                e.Use();
            }
            EditorGUILayout.EndScrollView();
            if (e.type == EventType.MouseMove) Repaint();
        }

        private bool Generate()
        {
            try
            {
                messages = MapBuilderUtility.Validate(draft);
                if (messages.Any(m => m.StartsWith("ERROR:"))) return false;
                generated = MapBuilderUtility.Generate(draft);
                generatedSnapshot = EditorJsonUtility.ToJson(draft);
                messages = new List<string> { "Generated a new unsaved level. Save it before Play. After further grid edits, generate again." };
                return true;
            }
            catch (Exception error) { messages = new List<string> { error.Message }; return false; }
        }
        private void SaveGenerated()
        {
            var path = EditorUtility.SaveFilePanelInProject("Save new level", "NewMap", "unity", "Choose a NEW scene name. Existing scenes will not be overwritten.", "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes");
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(path)) return;
            try
            {
                MapBuilderUtility.SaveNew(generated, path);
                hasUnsavedChanges = false;
                messages = new List<string> { "Saved: " + path + ". No test/campaign entry was changed." };
            }
            catch (Exception error) { messages = new List<string> { error.Message }; }
        }
        public override void SaveChanges()
        {
            if (!Generate()) return;
            if (generated.IsValid() && generated.isLoaded && string.IsNullOrEmpty(generated.path)) SaveGenerated();
            if (!hasUnsavedChanges) base.SaveChanges();
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Editor\LevelAuthoring\MapDraft.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring
{
    [Serializable]
    public sealed class MapTowerStamp
    {
        public Vector2Int cell;
        public GameObject prefab;
        public AttackArea area;
        public bool isAoe;
        public int goldBounty = 25;
        public string configuration;
    }

    public sealed class MapDraft : ScriptableObject
    {
        public int width = 24, length = 18, originX, originZ, layer, levelNumber = 1;
        public int[] tiles = Array.Empty<int>();
        public List<Vector2Int> route = new List<Vector2Int>();
        public List<MapTowerStamp> towers = new List<MapTowerStamp>();
        public bool Inside(Vector2Int p) => p.x >= 0 && p.y >= 0 && p.x < width && p.y < length;
        public int Tile(Vector2Int p) => Inside(p) ? tiles[p.y * width + p.x] : -1;
        public Vector3 Position(Vector2Int p) => new Vector3(originX + p.x, layer * .5f, originZ + p.y);

        public void ResetGrid(int x, int z)
        {
            if (x < 3 || z < 3 || x > 96 || z > 96) throw new ArgumentException("Width and length must be between 3 and 96.");
            width = x; length = z; originX = originZ = layer = 0;
            tiles = Enumerable.Repeat(-1, x * z).ToArray(); route.Clear(); towers.Clear();
        }

        public void Paint(Vector2Int p, int kind, int size)
        {
            if (!Inside(p)) return;
            var x0 = size == 3 ? Mathf.FloorToInt((p.x + originX) / 3f) * 3 - originX : p.x;
            var z0 = size == 3 ? Mathf.FloorToInt((p.y + originZ) / 3f) * 3 - originZ : p.y;
            if (x0 < 0 || z0 < 0 || x0 + size > width || z0 + size > length)
                throw new ArgumentException("The whole brush must fit inside the grid.");
            for (var z = z0; z < z0 + size; z++)
                for (var x = x0; x < x0 + size; x++) tiles[z * width + x] = kind;
        }

        public void AppendRoute(Vector2Int point)
        {
            if (!Inside(point)) throw new ArgumentException("Route point is outside the grid.");
            var addition = new List<Vector2Int>();
            if (route.Count == 0) addition.Add(point);
            else
            {
                var previous = route[route.Count - 1];
                if (previous == point) return;
                if (previous.x != point.x && previous.y != point.y)
                    throw new ArgumentException("Choose an orthogonal corner first; diagonal routes are not allowed.");
                var step = new Vector2Int(Math.Sign(point.x - previous.x), Math.Sign(point.y - previous.y));
                for (var p = previous + step; ; p += step) { addition.Add(p); if (p == point) break; }
            }
            foreach (var p in addition)
            {
                if (route.Contains(p)) throw new ArgumentException("Route cannot cross or repeat itself.");
                var tile = Tile(p);
                if (tile != (int)CellKind.Road && tile != (int)CellKind.Spawn && tile != (int)CellKind.Goal)
                    throw new ArgumentException($"Route ({p.x}, {p.y}) needs Road, Spawn or Goal.");
            }
            route.AddRange(addition);
        }

        public void PlaceTower(Vector2Int point, GameObject prefab, AttackArea area, bool isAoe = false, int goldBounty = 25)
        {
            var tower = prefab != null ? prefab.GetComponent<BattleTower>() : null;
            if (tower == null) throw new ArgumentException("Choose a tower prefab with BattleTower on its root.");
            var radius = tower.FootprintSize / 2;
            if (radius == 1 && (((point.x + originX) % 3 + 3) % 3 != 1 || ((point.y + originZ) % 3 + 3) % 3 != 1))
                throw new ArgumentException("A 3x3 tower must be placed at a macro center (3n+1, 3n+1).");
            for (var z = -radius; z <= radius; z++)
                for (var x = -radius; x <= radius; x++)
                {
                    var p = point + new Vector2Int(x, z);
                    if (Tile(p) != (int)CellKind.TowerPlatform) throw new ArgumentException("The whole tower footprint needs TowerPlatform tiles.");
                    if (towers.Any(t => Mathf.Abs(t.cell.x - p.x) <= t.prefab.GetComponent<BattleTower>().FootprintSize / 2 &&
                                        Mathf.Abs(t.cell.y - p.y) <= t.prefab.GetComponent<BattleTower>().FootprintSize / 2))
                        throw new ArgumentException("Tower footprints overlap.");
                }
            towers.Add(new MapTowerStamp
            {
                cell = point,
                prefab = prefab,
                area = area,
                isAoe = isAoe,
                goldBounty = goldBounty > 0 ? goldBounty : (isAoe ? 40 : 25)
            });
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Analytics\AnalyticsDispatcher.cs === 
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Networking;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Analytics
{
    // WebGL hands each event to Analytics.jslib, which batches and flushes on page exit.
    // Other players POST batches as text/plain so Apps Script needs no CORS preflight.
    public sealed class AnalyticsDispatcher : MonoBehaviour, IAnalyticsSink
    {
        private const int MaxTries = 2, MaxQueued = 1000, MaxBatch = 100;
        private readonly List<string> queue = new List<string>();
        private string url;
        private float interval, timer;
        private bool send, log, posting;

#if UNITY_WEBGL && !UNITY_EDITOR
        [DllImport("__Internal")] private static extern void AnalyticsInit(string url, int intervalMs);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] private static extern void AnalyticsEnqueue(string json);
        [DllImport("__Internal")] private static extern void AnalyticsSetContext(string json);
        private static readonly bool UsesPage = true;
#else
        private static void AnalyticsInit(string url, int intervalMs) { }
        private static void AnalyticsEnqueue(string json) { }
        private static void AnalyticsSetContext(string json) { }
        private static readonly bool UsesPage = false;
#endif

        internal static AnalyticsDispatcher Create(AnalyticsSettings settings, bool send, bool log)
        {
            var host = new GameObject("Analytics Dispatcher");
            DontDestroyOnLoad(host);
            var dispatcher = host.AddComponent<AnalyticsDispatcher>();
            dispatcher.url = settings.EndpointUrl;
            dispatcher.interval = settings.FlushIntervalSeconds;
            dispatcher.send = send;
            dispatcher.log = log;
            if (send && UsesPage) AnalyticsInit(dispatcher.url, Mathf.RoundToInt(dispatcher.interval * 1000));
            return dispatcher;
        }

        public void Emit(AnalyticsEvent value)
        {
            var json = value.ToJson();
            if (log) Debug.Log("[Analytics] " + json);
            if (!send) return;
            if (UsesPage)
            {
                AnalyticsEnqueue(json);
                AnalyticsSetContext(GameAnalytics.ContextJson());
            }
            else if (queue.Count < MaxQueued) queue.Add(json);
        }

        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            BattleAnalytics.LateTick();
            if (!send || UsesPage) return;
            timer += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            if (timer >= interval) Flush();
        }

        private void OnApplicationPause(bool paused) { if (paused) Flush(); }
        private void OnApplicationQuit() => Flush();

        private void Flush()
        {
            timer = 0;
            if (!send || UsesPage || posting || queue.Count == 0) return;
            var count = Mathf.Min(MaxBatch, queue.Count);
            var batch = queue.GetRange(0, count);
            queue.RemoveRange(0, count);
            StartCoroutine(Post(AnalyticsJson.Batch(batch)));
        }

        private IEnumerator Post(string body)
        {
            posting = true;
            for (var tries = 1; tries <= MaxTries; tries++)
            {
                using (var request = new UnityWebRequest(url, UnityWebRequest.kHttpVerbPOST))
                {
                    request.uploadHandler = new UploadHandlerRaw(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(body)) { contentType = "text/plain;charset=utf-8" };
                    request.downloadHandler = new DownloadHandlerBuffer();
                    request.SetRequestHeader("Content-Type", "text/plain;charset=utf-8");
                    request.timeout = 15;
                    yield return request.SendWebRequest();
                    // Any HTTP answer means Apps Script ran; retrying would duplicate rows.
                    if (request.responseCode != 0) break;
                }
            }
            posting = false;
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Analytics\AnalyticsJson.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Analytics
{
    // Flat JSON objects only: string, number, bool or null values.
    public static class AnalyticsJson
    {
        public static string Object(IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<string, object>> fields)
        {
            var builder = new StringBuilder(256);
            builder.Append('{');
            for (var i = 0; i < fields.Count; i++)
            {
                if (i > 0) builder.Append(',');
                AppendString(builder, fields[i].Key);
                builder.Append(':');
                AppendValue(builder, fields[i].Value);
            }
            return builder.Append('}').ToString();
        }

        public static string Batch(IReadOnlyList<string> events)
        {
            var builder = new StringBuilder(64 + events.Count * 256);
            builder.Append("{\"events\":[");
            for (var i = 0; i < events.Count; i++)
            {
                if (i > 0) builder.Append(',');
                builder.Append(events[i]);
            }
            return builder.Append("]}").ToString();
        }

        public static void AppendValue(StringBuilder builder, object value)
        {
            switch (value)
            {
                case null: builder.Append("null"); break;
                case bool flag: builder.Append(flag ? "true" : "false"); break;
                case string text: AppendString(builder, text); break;
                case float number: AppendNumber(builder, number); break;
                case double number: AppendNumber(builder, number); break;
                case decimal number: builder.Append(number.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)); break;
                case int _: case long _: case short _: case byte _: case uint _: case ulong _: case ushort _: case sbyte _:
                    builder.Append(Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)); break;
                default: AppendString(builder, Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)); break;
            }
        }

        public static void AppendNumber(StringBuilder builder, double number)
        {
            if (double.IsNaN(number) || double.IsInfinity(number)) { builder.Append("null"); return; }
            var rounded = Math.Round(number, 3, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero);
            builder.Append(rounded == 0 ? "0" : rounded.ToString("0.###", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
        }

        public static void AppendString(StringBuilder builder, string text)
        {
            builder.Append('"');
            foreach (var c in text ?? "")
            {
                switch (c)
                {
                    case '"': builder.Append("\\\""); break;
                    case '\\': builder.Append("\\\\"); break;
                    case '\n': builder.Append("\\n"); break;
                    case '\r': builder.Append("\\r"); break;
                    case '\t': builder.Append("\\t"); break;
                    case '\b': builder.Append("\\b"); break;
                    case '\f': builder.Append("\\f"); break;
                    default:
                        // U+2028/U+2029 are valid JSON but break older JS string literals.
                        if (c < ' ' || c == '\u2028' || c == '\u2029') builder.Append("\\u").Append(((int)c).ToString("x4"));
                        else builder.Append(c);
                        break;
                }
            }
            builder.Append('"');
        }
    }
}
.

using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Analytics
{
    // Loaded from Resources/RebuildAnalytics. An empty endpoint disables network sends.
    [CreateAssetMenu(menuName = "Team5/Rebuild/Analytics Settings")]
    public sealed class AnalyticsSettings : ScriptableObject
    {
        public const string ResourceName = "RebuildAnalytics";
        [SerializeField] private string endpointUrl = "";
        [SerializeField] private bool enabled = true;
        [SerializeField] private bool sendFromEditor;
        [SerializeField] private bool logToConsole = true;
        [SerializeField] private string buildLabel = "beta";
        [SerializeField, Min(1)] private float flushIntervalSeconds = 5;
        public string EndpointUrl => endpointUrl != null ? endpointUrl.Trim() : "";
        public bool Enabled => enabled;
        public bool SendFromEditor => sendFromEditor;
        public bool LogToConsole => logToConsole;
        public string BuildLabel => buildLabel ?? "";
        public float FlushIntervalSeconds => Mathf.Max(1, flushIntervalSeconds);
    }

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Analytics
{
    // Follows scene loads, battle sessions and run settlement. One tracker per attempt.
    public static class BattleAnalytics
    {
        private static BattleSession session;
        private static BattleAnalyticsTracker tracker;
        private static int handledScene;
        private static string currentScene = "";
        private static float sceneEnteredAt, runStartedAt;
        private static int runsInSession, runAttempts, runDefeats;

        public static BattleAnalyticsTracker Current => tracker != null && !tracker.IsFinished ? tracker : null;
        public static double SimSeconds => Current != null ? Current.Simulation.ElapsedTime : 0;
        public static float ProgressPct => Current != null ? Current.CurrentProgressPct : 0;

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void ResetStatics()
        {
            Uninstall();
            session = null;
            tracker = null;
            handledScene = 0;
            currentScene = "";
            sceneEnteredAt = runStartedAt = 0;
            runsInSession = runAttempts = runDefeats = 0;
        }

        internal static void Install()
        {
            Uninstall();
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded += OnSceneLoaded;
            BattleSceneFlow.RunStarted += OnRunStarted;
            BattleSceneFlow.LevelSettled += OnLevelSettled;
        }

        private static void Uninstall()
        {
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnSceneLoaded;
            BattleSceneFlow.RunStarted -= OnRunStarted;
            BattleSceneFlow.LevelSettled -= OnLevelSettled;
        }

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.AfterSceneLoad)]
        private static void BindInitialScene()
        {
            if (GameAnalytics.IsActive) OnSceneLoaded(SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), LoadSceneMode.Single);
        }

        // Members whose slot changes when the editor's source slots are applied.
        public static int MovedMembers(BattleSimulation simulation, IReadOnlyList<int> sourceSlots)
        {
            try
            {
                var moved = 0;
                for (var destination = 0; sourceSlots != null && destination < sourceSlots.Count; destination++)
                    if (sourceSlots[destination] != destination && simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(sourceSlots[destination]) != null) moved++;
                return moved;
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); return 0; }
        }

        internal static void Observe(AnalyticsEvent value)
        {
            var current = Current;
            if (current == null) return;
            switch (value.Name)
            {
                case "formation_edit": if ((value["result"] as string) == "applied") current.formationEdits++; break;
                case "pause_toggle": if (value["paused"] is bool paused && paused) current.pauses++; break;
                case "speed_change": current.speedChanges++; break;
                case "unit_inspect": current.inspections++; break;
            }
        }

        internal static void LateTick()
        {
            try
            {
                var current = Current;
                if (current == null) return;
                if (session != null) current.Sample(session.PlaybackSpeed);
                // Test runs and skipped settlements never raise LevelSettled.
                if (current.Simulation.Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running) FinishCurrent(0, false);
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private static void OnSceneLoaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
        {
            if (mode != LoadSceneMode.Single || BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(scene) || scene.handle == handledScene) return;
            handledScene = scene.handle;
            try
            {
                CloseCurrent();
                PrepAnalytics.Leave();
                TutorialAnalytics.Reset();
                Unbind();
                var previous = currentScene;
                var previousSeconds = Time.realtimeSinceStartup - sceneEnteredAt;
                currentScene = scene.name;
                sceneEnteredAt = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
                GameAnalytics.SetScene(scene.name);
                foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
                {
                    session = root.GetComponentInChildren<BattleSession>();
                    if (session != null) break;
                }
                // The flow may restore the session's failed attempts after this handler, so use the flow's count here.
                var run = BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive;
                GameAnalytics.SetLevelContext(session != null ? session.DisplayedLevelNumber : run ? BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber : 0,
                    run ? BattleSession.DefaultLivesPerLevel - BattleSceneFlow.CurrentLevelLivesRemaining + 1 : session != null ? 1 : 0);
                GameAnalytics.Track("scene_enter", ("previous_scene", previous), ("previous_scene_s", previous.Length > 0 ? previousSeconds : 0));
                if (session == null) return;
                session.SimulationCreated += OnSimulationCreated;
                if (session.Simulation != null) OnSimulationCreated(session.Simulation);
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private static void OnSimulationCreated(BattleSimulation next)
        {
            try
            {
                if (next == null || session == null || (tracker != null && tracker.Simulation == next)) return;
                CloseCurrent();
                // StartBattle always builds a fresh simulation; only a tutorial rewind restores a clock past zero.
                var resumed = next.ElapsedTime > 0;
                var attempt = NextAttempt(session);
                GameAnalytics.SetLevelContext(session.DisplayedLevelNumber, attempt);
                if (resumed) GameAnalytics.Track("tutorial_rewind", ("checkpoint_sim_s", next.ElapsedTime), ("lives_left", session.AttemptsRemaining));
                runAttempts++;
                tracker = new BattleAnalyticsTracker(next, RouteLength(session), attempt, resumed);
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private static void OnRunStarted()
        {
            try
            {
                GameAnalytics.BeginRun();
                runsInSession++;
                runAttempts = runDefeats = 0;
                runStartedAt = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
                GameAnalytics.Track("run_start", ("run_number_in_session", runsInSession));
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        // Raised inside Settle, so rewards, lives and exhaustion are already final.
        private static void OnLevelSettled(BattleOutcome outcome, int level)
        {
            try
            {
                var current = Current;
                if (current != null && current.Simulation.Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running)
                {
                    if (session != null) current.Sample(session.PlaybackSpeed);
                    FinishCurrent(BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardGold, BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives);
                }
                if (outcome == BattleOutcome.Defeat) runDefeats++;
                if (outcome == BattleOutcome.Defeat && BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives)
                    GameAnalytics.Track("game_over", ("run_real_s", RunSeconds), ("total_attempts", runAttempts), ("total_defeats", runDefeats));
                // Settle leaves the next level unchanged only when the campaign is over.
                if (outcome == BattleOutcome.Victory && BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber == level)
                    GameAnalytics.Track("campaign_complete", ("run_real_s", RunSeconds), ("total_attempts", runAttempts), ("total_defeats", runDefeats));
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private static float RunSeconds => Time.realtimeSinceStartup - runStartedAt;

        private static int NextAttempt(BattleSession value) => value.AttemptsPerLevel - value.AttemptsRemaining + 1;

        private static void FinishCurrent(int goldReward, bool gameOver)
        {
            var current = Current;
            if (current == null) return;
            current.Finish(goldReward, session != null ? session.AttemptsRemaining : -1, gameOver);
        }

        private static void CloseCurrent()
        {
            var current = Current;
            tracker = null;
            if (current == null) return;
            if (session != null) current.Sample(session.PlaybackSpeed);
            if (current.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running) current.Abandon();
            else current.Finish(0, session != null ? session.AttemptsRemaining : -1, BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives);
        }

        private static void Unbind()
        {
            if (session != null) session.SimulationCreated -= OnSimulationCreated;
            session = null;
        }

        private static float RouteLength(BattleSession value)
        {
            if (value.Route == null) return 0;
            var points = value.Route.CaptureTopCenters();
            var length = 0f;
            for (var i = 1; i < points.Length; i++) length += Vector3.Distance(points[i - 1], points[i]);
            return length;
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Analytics\BattleAnalyticsTracker.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Analytics
{
    // One battle attempt. Observes simulation events only; never calls back into the simulation.
    public sealed class BattleAnalyticsTracker
    {
        private readonly BattleSimulation simulation;
        private readonly float routeLength;
        private readonly float startedAt;
        private readonly Dictionary<int, int> lastHitBy = new Dictionary<int, int>();
        private readonly Dictionary<int, float> towerDamage = new Dictionary<int, float>();
        private readonly Dictionary<int, int> towerKills = new Dictionary<int, int>();
        private readonly Dictionary<int, double> towerDestroyedAt = new Dictionary<int, double>();
        private readonly double[] speedSeconds = new double[4];
        private double sampledTime;
        private float damageTaken, damageToTowers, healingDone;
        private bool started;
        internal int formationEdits, pauses, speedChanges, inspections;

        public BattleSimulation Simulation => simulation;
        public int Attempt { get; }
        public bool Resumed { get; }
        public bool IsFinished { get; private set; }

        public BattleAnalyticsTracker(BattleSimulation simulation, float routeLength, int attempt, bool resumed)
        {
            this.simulation = simulation ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(simulation));
            this.routeLength = routeLength;
            Attempt = attempt;
            Resumed = resumed;
            startedAt = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            sampledTime = simulation.ElapsedTime;
            simulation.UnitSpawned += OnSpawned;
            simulation.UnitDamaged += OnDamaged;
            simulation.UnitDied += OnDied;
            simulation.TowerDamaged += OnTowerDamaged;
            simulation.TowerDestroyed += OnTowerDestroyed;
            simulation.UnitHealed += OnHealed;
            TryStart(false);
        }

        public float CurrentProgressPct
        {
            get
            {
                var best = -1f;
                foreach (var unit in simulation.Units) if (unit.IsAlive) best = Mathf.Max(best, ProgressPct(unit));
                return best >= 0 ? best : FurthestProgressPct;
            }
        }

        public float FurthestProgressPct
        {
            get
            {
                var best = 0f;
                foreach (var unit in simulation.Units) best = Mathf.Max(best, ProgressPct(unit));
                return best;
            }
        }

        public int Survivors => simulation.Units.Count(unit => unit.LifeState != UnitLifeState.Dead);

        public float ProgressPct(UnitState unit)
        {
            if (unit.LifeState == UnitLifeState.ReachedGoal) return 100;
            return routeLength > 0 ? Mathf.Clamp(unit.RouteProgress / routeLength * 100, 0, 100) : 0;
        }

        // Attributes simulated time to the playback speed active since the last sample.
        public void Sample(int playbackSpeed)
        {
            var delta = simulation.ElapsedTime - sampledTime;
            if (delta > 0) speedSeconds[Mathf.Clamp(playbackSpeed, 1, 3)] += delta;
            sampledTime = simulation.ElapsedTime;
        }

        public void Finish(int goldReward, int livesLeft, bool gameOver)
        {
            if (IsFinished) return;
            TryStart(true);
            Unsubscribe();
            var units = simulation.Units;
            var maxHp = units.Sum(unit => unit.MaxHp);
            var outcome = simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running ? "running" : simulation.Outcome.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();
            GameAnalytics.Track("level_end",
                ("outcome", outcome),
                ("resumed", Resumed),
                ("sim_s", simulation.ElapsedTime),
                ("real_s", Time.realtimeSinceStartup - startedAt),
                ("spawned", simulation.SpawnedCount),
                ("killed", simulation.KilledCount),
                ("reached_goal", simulation.ReachedGoalCount),
                ("survivors", Survivors),
                ("towers_destroyed", simulation.TowersDestroyedCount),
                ("tower_count", simulation.Towers.Count),
                ("furthest_progress_pct", FurthestProgressPct),
                ("squad_hp_left_pct", maxHp > 0 ? units.Sum(unit => unit.Hp) / maxHp * 100 : 0),
                ("damage_taken", damageTaken),
                ("damage_to_towers", damageToTowers),
                ("healing_done", healingDone),
                ("formation_edits", formationEdits),
                ("pauses", pauses),
                ("speed_changes", speedChanges),
                ("sim_s_at_1x", speedSeconds[1]),
                ("sim_s_at_2x", speedSeconds[2]),
                ("sim_s_at_3x", speedSeconds[3]),
                ("inspections", inspections),
                ("gold_reward", goldReward),
                ("lives_left", livesLeft),
                ("game_over", gameOver));
            foreach (var tower in simulation.Towers)
            {
                towerDamage.TryGetValue(tower.Id, out var damage);
                towerKills.TryGetValue(tower.Id, out var kills);
                var destroyed = towerDestroyedAt.TryGetValue(tower.Id, out var destroyedAt);
                GameAnalytics.Track("tower_stats",
                    ("outcome", outcome),
                    ("tower_id", tower.Id),
                    ("is_aoe", tower.Definition.IsAoe),
                    ("max_hp", tower.Definition.MaxHp),
                    ("damage_dealt", damage),
                    ("kills", kills),
                    ("destroyed", !tower.IsAlive),
                    ("destroyed_at_s", destroyed ? destroyedAt : -1),
                    ("hp_left_pct", tower.Hp / tower.Definition.MaxHp * 100));
            }
            IsFinished = true;
        }

        public void Abandon()
        {
            if (IsFinished) return;
            Unsubscribe();
            GameAnalytics.Track("level_abandon",
                ("resumed", Resumed),
                ("sim_s", simulation.ElapsedTime),
                ("real_s", Time.realtimeSinceStartup - startedAt),
                ("progress_pct", CurrentProgressPct),
                ("survivors", Survivors));
            IsFinished = true;
        }

        // The squad is known once every planned member has spawned (the first tick for prepared squads).
        private void TryStart(bool force)
        {
            if (started || (!force && simulation.PendingSpawnCount > 0)) return;
            started = true;
            var units = simulation.Units;
            GameAnalytics.Track("level_start",
                ("resumed", Resumed),
                ("squad_size", units.Count),
                ("roles", Roles(units)),
                ("slots", string.Join("|", units.OrderBy(unit => unit.SlotId).Select(unit => $"{unit.SlotId}:{unit.Role}"))),
                ("captain_present", units.Any(unit => unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain)),
                ("squad_total_hp", units.Sum(unit => unit.MaxHp)),
                ("avg_unit_level", units.Count > 0 ? units.Average(unit => unit.Definition.Level) : 0),
                ("max_rarity", MaxRarity(units.Select(unit => unit.Definition))),
                ("tower_count", simulation.Towers.Count));
        }

        public static string Roles(IEnumerable<UnitState> units) => Roles(units.Select(unit => unit.Definition));

        public static string Roles(IEnumerable<UnitDefinition> units) =>
            string.Join("|", units.GroupBy(unit => unit.Role).OrderBy(group => group.Key).Select(group => $"{group.Key}:{group.Count()}"));

        public static string MaxRarity(IEnumerable<UnitDefinition> units)
        {
            var list = units.ToList();
            return list.Count > 0 ? list.Max(unit => unit.Rarity).ToString() : "";
        }

        private void Unsubscribe()
        {
            simulation.UnitSpawned -= OnSpawned;
            simulation.UnitDamaged -= OnDamaged;
            simulation.UnitDied -= OnDied;
            simulation.TowerDamaged -= OnTowerDamaged;
            simulation.TowerDestroyed -= OnTowerDestroyed;
            simulation.UnitHealed -= OnHealed;
        }

        // Handlers run inside BattleSimulation.Step, so each one swallows its own failures.
        private void OnSpawned(UnitSpawnedEvent value)
        {
            try { TryStart(false); }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private void OnDamaged(DamageEvent value)
        {
            try
            {
                damageTaken += value.Amount;
                lastHitBy[value.UnitId] = value.TowerId;
                towerDamage.TryGetValue(value.TowerId, out var total);
                towerDamage[value.TowerId] = total + value.Amount;
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private void OnDied(UnitEndedEvent value)
        {
            try
            {
                var killer = lastHitBy.TryGetValue(value.UnitId, out var towerId) ? towerId : 0;
                if (killer != 0) { towerKills.TryGetValue(killer, out var kills); towerKills[killer] = kills + 1; }
                UnitState unit = null;
                foreach (var candidate in simulation.Units) if (candidate.Id == value.UnitId) { unit = candidate; break; }
                if (unit == null) return;
                GameAnalytics.Track("unit_death",
                    ("role", unit.Role.ToString()),
                    ("rarity", unit.Definition.Rarity.ToString()),
                    ("unit_level", unit.Definition.Level),
                    ("slot", unit.SlotId),
                    ("sim_s", simulation.ElapsedTime),
                    ("progress_pct", ProgressPct(unit)),
                    ("killer_tower_id", killer),
                    ("x", value.Position.x),
                    ("z", value.Position.z));
            }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private void OnTowerDamaged(TowerDamageEvent value)
        {
            try { damageToTowers += value.Amount; }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private void OnTowerDestroyed(TowerDestroyedEvent value)
        {
            try { towerDestroyedAt[value.TowerId] = simulation.ElapsedTime; }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }

        private void OnHealed(HealEvent value)
        {
            try { healingDone += value.Amount; }
            catch (Exception error) { GameAnalytics.Fail(error); }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Analytics\GameAnalytics.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Analytics
{
    public interface IAnalyticsSink
    {
        void Emit(AnalyticsEvent value);
    }

    public sealed class AnalyticsEvent
    {
        private readonly List<KeyValuePair<string, object>> fields = new List<KeyValuePair<string, object>>();
        public string Name { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<KeyValuePair<string, object>> Fields => fields;
        public object this[string key] => TryGet(key, out var value) ? value : null;

        internal AnalyticsEvent(string name)
        {
            Name = name;
            fields.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, object>("event", name));
        }

        internal void Set(string key, object value)
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < fields.Count; i++)
                if (fields[i].Key == key) { fields[i] = new KeyValuePair<string, object>(key, value); return; }
            fields.Add(new KeyValuePair<string, object>(key, value));
        }

        public bool TryGet(string key, out object value)
        {
            foreach (var field in fields)
                if (field.Key == key) { value = field.Value; return true; }
            value = null;
            return false;
        }

        public string ToJson() => AnalyticsJson.Object(fields);
    }

    // Static facade. Every entry point swallows its own failures; analytics never changes gameplay.
    public static class GameAnalytics
    {
        private const string PlayerKey = "team5.analytics.player";
        private const string SessionCountKey = "team5.analytics.sessions";
        private static IAnalyticsSink sink;
        private static string playerId = "", sessionId = "", runId = "", build = "", sceneName = "";
        private static int level, attempt;
        private static long sequence;
        private static bool failureLogged;

        public static bool IsActive => sink != null;
        public static string RunId => runId;
        public static int Level => level;
        public static int Attempt => attempt;

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void ResetStatics()
        {
            sink = null;
            playerId = sessionId = runId = build = sceneName = "";
            level = attempt = 0;
            sequence = 0;
            failureLogged = false;
        }

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.BeforeSceneLoad)]
        private static void Bootstrap()
        {
            try
            {
                var settings = Resources.Load<AnalyticsSettings>(AnalyticsSettings.ResourceName);
                if (settings == null || !settings.Enabled) return;
                var send = settings.EndpointUrl.Length > 0 && (!Application.isEditor || settings.SendFromEditor);
                var log = Application.isEditor && settings.LogToConsole;
                if (!send && !log) return;
                var returning = PlayerPrefs.HasKey(PlayerKey);
                playerId = returning ? PlayerPrefs.GetString(PlayerKey) : Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
                var sessions = PlayerPrefs.GetInt(SessionCountKey, 0) + 1;
                PlayerPrefs.SetString(PlayerKey, playerId);
                PlayerPrefs.SetInt(SessionCountKey, sessions);
                PlayerPrefs.Save();
                sessionId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
                build = settings.BuildLabel.Length > 0 ? settings.BuildLabel + "-" + Application.version : Application.version;
                sceneName = SceneManager.GetActiveScene().name;
                sink = AnalyticsDispatcher.Create(settings, send, log);
                BattleAnalytics.Install();
                Track("session_start", ("returning_player", returning), ("session_count", sessions),
                    ("screen_w", Screen.width), ("screen_h", Screen.height));
            }
            catch (Exception error) { Fail(error); }
        }

        // Tests and tools inject a sink; null disables tracking.
        public static void SetSink(IAnalyticsSink value) => sink = value;

        public static void Track(string name, params (string key, object value)[] fields)
        {
            if (sink == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(name)) return;
            try
            {
                var value = new AnalyticsEvent(name);
                AddCommonFields(value);
                if (fields != null)
                    foreach (var (key, field) in fields)
                        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(key)) value.Set(key, field);
                BattleAnalytics.Observe(value);
                PrepAnalytics.Observe(value);
                sink.Emit(value);
            }
            catch (Exception error) { Fail(error); }
        }

        // Battle-scoped events also carry the tracked attempt's sim time and route progress.
        public static void TrackBattle(string name, params (string key, object value)[] fields)
        {
            if (sink == null) return;
            try
            {
                var all = new (string key, object value)[(fields?.Length ?? 0) + 2];
                all[0] = ("sim_s", BattleAnalytics.SimSeconds);
                all[1] = ("progress_pct", BattleAnalytics.ProgressPct);
                if (fields != null) Array.Copy(fields, 0, all, 2, fields.Length);
                Track(name, all);
            }
            catch (Exception error) { Fail(error); }
        }

        public static void SetLevelContext(int levelNumber, int attemptNumber)
        {
            level = Math.Max(0, levelNumber);
            attempt = Math.Max(0, attemptNumber);
        }

        internal static void BeginRun() => runId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
        internal static void SetScene(string name) => sceneName = name ?? "";

        // Last known context, handed to the WebGL page so it can describe a tab close.
        internal static string ContextJson()
        {
            var value = new AnalyticsEvent("context");
            AddContextFields(value);
            value.Set("seq", sequence);
            return value.ToJson();
        }

        private static void AddCommonFields(AnalyticsEvent value)
        {
            value.Set("ts", DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss.fff'Z'", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            AddContextFields(value);
            value.Set("seq", ++sequence);
        }

        private static void AddContextFields(AnalyticsEvent value)
        {
            value.Set("player_id", playerId);
            value.Set("session_id", sessionId);
            value.Set("run_id", runId);
            value.Set("build", build);
            value.Set("platform", Application.platform.ToString());
            value.Set("is_editor", Application.isEditor);
            value.Set("is_test_run", BattleSceneFlow.IsTestRun);
            value.Set("scene", sceneName.Length > 0 ? sceneName : SceneManager.GetActiveScene().name);
            value.Set("level", level);
            value.Set("attempt", attempt);
        }

        internal static void Fail(Exception error)
        {
            if (failureLogged) return;
            failureLogged = true;
            if (Application.isEditor) Debug.LogWarning("[Analytics] Dropped an event after an error; later errors stay silent.\n" + error);
        }
    }
}
