=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\TowerRangeView.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    public sealed class TowerRangeView : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private TowerState tower;
        private TowerDefinition previewDefinition;
        private Mesh mesh;
        private MeshRenderer fill;
        private LineRenderer border;
        private Material fillMaterial, borderMaterial;
        private Vector3[] corners;
        private bool visible = true;
        public Bounds DisplayBounds { get; private set; }
        public int TowerId => tower?.Id ?? 0;

        public void Bind(TowerState state)
        {
            tower = state;
            previewDefinition = null;
            EnsureGeometry();
            Refresh();
        }

        public void BindPreview(TowerDefinition definition)
        {
            tower = null;
            previewDefinition = definition;
            EnsureGeometry();
            Refresh();
        }

        private void EnsureGeometry()
        {
            if (mesh == null)
            {
                mesh = new Mesh { name = "Tower Range XZ Quad" };
                gameObject.AddComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh = mesh;
                fill = gameObject.AddComponent<MeshRenderer>();
                fillMaterial = CreateMaterial(new Color(0.25f, 0.55f, 1, 0.15f));
                borderMaterial = CreateMaterial(new Color(0.25f, 0.55f, 1, 0.7f));
                fill.sharedMaterial = fillMaterial;
                fill.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
                fill.receiveShadows = false;
                border = new GameObject("Border").AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
                border.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
                border.sharedMaterial = borderMaterial;
                border.loop = true;
                border.useWorldSpace = true;
                border.positionCount = 4;
                border.widthMultiplier = 0.035f;
                border.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
                border.receiveShadows = false;
            }
        }

        public void SetVisible(bool value) { visible = value; Refresh(); }
        private void LateUpdate() => Refresh();
        private void OnEnable() => Refresh();
        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (fill != null) fill.enabled = false;
            if (border != null) border.enabled = false;
        }

        private void Refresh()
        {
            var definition = tower != null ? tower.Definition : previewDefinition;
            if (definition == null || fill == null || border == null) return;
            fill.enabled = border.enabled = isActiveAndEnabled && visible && (tower == null || tower.IsAlive);

            // Tint AOE towers orange-red with a subtle pulse
            if (definition.IsAoe)
            {
                var pulse = 0.14f + 0.08f * Mathf.Sin(Time.time * 4f);
                fillMaterial.SetColor("_BaseColor", new Color(1f, 0.36f, 0.12f, pulse));
                borderMaterial.SetColor("_BaseColor", new Color(1f, 0.45f, 0.15f, 0.85f));
            }
            else
            {
                fillMaterial.SetColor("_BaseColor", new Color(0.25f, 0.55f, 1f, 0.15f));
                borderMaterial.SetColor("_BaseColor", new Color(0.25f, 0.55f, 1f, 0.7f));
            }

            var position = tower != null ? tower.Position : definition.Position;
            var bounds = SquadCombatRules.AreaBounds(position, definition.Area, definition.Range, definition.AttackWidth);
            transform.SetPositionAndRotation(bounds.center + Vector3.up * 0.035f, Quaternion.identity);
            transform.localScale = Vector3.one;
            if (DisplayBounds != bounds || mesh.vertexCount == 0)
            {
                var half = bounds.extents;
                corners = new[] { new Vector3(-half.x, 0, -half.z), new Vector3(-half.x, 0, half.z),
                    new Vector3(half.x, 0, half.z), new Vector3(half.x, 0, -half.z) };
                mesh.vertices = corners;
                mesh.triangles = new[] { 0, 1, 2, 0, 2, 3 };
                mesh.RecalculateBounds();
            }
            DisplayBounds = bounds;
            for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) border.SetPosition(i, transform.TransformPoint(corners[i]));
        }

        private static Material CreateMaterial(Color color)
        {
            var material = new Material(Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Unlit")) { name = "Tower Range (Runtime)", renderQueue = 3000 };
            material.SetColor("_BaseColor", color);
            material.SetFloat("_Surface", 1);
            material.SetFloat("_SrcBlend", (float)BlendMode.SrcAlpha);
            material.SetFloat("_DstBlend", (float)BlendMode.OneMinusSrcAlpha);
            material.SetFloat("_ZWrite", 0);
            material.SetFloat("_Cull", (float)CullMode.Off);
            material.SetOverrideTag("RenderType", "Transparent");
            material.EnableKeyword("_SURFACE_TYPE_TRANSPARENT");
            return material;
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (mesh != null) Destroy(mesh);
            if (fillMaterial != null) Destroy(fillMaterial);
            if (borderMaterial != null) Destroy(borderMaterial);
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\UnitDetailsText.cs === 
using System;
using System.Text;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    public static class UnitDetailsText
    {
        public static string Format(UnitState unit)
        {
            if (unit == null) return string.Empty;
            var stats = unit.Definition;
            var text = new StringBuilder();
            text.AppendLine($"Role: {unit.Role.ToString().ToLowerInvariant()} (Lv.{stats.Level} {stats.Rarity})");
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"HP: {unit.Hp:0.##} / {unit.MaxHp:0.##}"));
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Attack: {stats.Damage:0.##} ({stats.TowerDamageMultiplier:0.##}x vs Towers)"));
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Defense: {stats.Defense * 100:0.##}% damage reduction"));
            text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Move speed: {unit.Speed:0.##} units/s"));
            if (SquadCombatRules.CanAttackTowers(unit.Role))
            {
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Attack range: {stats.Range:0.##}"));
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Attack interval: {stats.AttackInterval:0.##} s"));
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Cooldown: {unit.AttackCooldown:0.##} s"));
            }
            if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Heal)
            {
                text.AppendLine(FormattableString.Invariant($"Healing: {stats.HealPerSecond * SquadCombatRules.HealInterval:0.##} HP / {SquadCombatRules.HealInterval:0.##} s"));
                text.AppendLine("Heal range: 8 neighboring slots");
            }
            text.AppendLine($"Member: #{unit.MemberId}");
            text.AppendLine($"Formation: row {unit.FormationSlot.y + 1}, column {unit.FormationSlot.x + 1}");
            text.Append($"Status: {(unit.LifeState == UnitLifeState.Dead ? "Dead" : unit.LifeState == UnitLifeState.ReachedGoal ? "Reached goal" : "Alive")}");
            return text.ToString();
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\UnitView.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Rendering;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation
{
    public sealed class UnitView : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const float LookScale = 2f;
        private const int HealRingSegments = 48;
        private const float HealNeighborRadius = 1.45f; // Covers all 8 neighboring slots in the 3x3 squad

        private UnitState state;
        private Vector3 previousPosition;
        private Vector3 currentPosition;
        private double sampleTime;
        private float sampleInterval;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock colors;

        // Animated healing range components
        private LineRenderer healPerimeterRing;
        private LineRenderer healWaveRing;
        private Material healPerimeterMat;
        private Material healWaveMat;
        private float healWavePhase;
        private float healBurstBoost;

        public int UnitId => state?.Id ?? 0;
        public UnitState State => state;

        public bool TryGetVisibleBounds(out Bounds bounds)
        {
            bounds = default;
            var found = false;
            foreach (var renderer in GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
            {
                if (!renderer.enabled || !renderer.gameObject.activeInHierarchy || renderer is LineRenderer) continue;
                if (!found) { bounds = renderer.bounds; found = true; }
                else bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
            }
            return found;
        }

        public Vector3 PreviousPosition => previousPosition;
        public Vector3 CurrentPosition => currentPosition;

        public void Bind(UnitState unit)
        {
            state = unit;
            if (colors == null) colors = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            previousPosition = currentPosition = unit.Position;
            sampleTime = Time.timeAsDouble;
            sampleInterval = Time.fixedDeltaTime;
            transform.position = currentPosition;
            ApplyAppearance(unit.Role);
            ConfigureHealingRangeAnimation(unit.Role == UnitRole.Heal);
        }

        public void TriggerHealBurst()
        {
            healWavePhase = 0f;
            healBurstBoost = 1f;
        }

        private void ConfigureHealingRangeAnimation(bool isHealer)
        {
            if (!isHealer)
            {
                if (healPerimeterRing != null) healPerimeterRing.gameObject.SetActive(false);
                if (healWaveRing != null) healWaveRing.gameObject.SetActive(false);
                return;
            }

            if (healPerimeterRing == null)
            {
                healPerimeterMat = CreateRingMaterial(new Color(0.25f, 0.95f, 0.50f, 0.45f));
                healWaveMat = CreateRingMaterial(new Color(0.35f, 1.00f, 0.60f, 0.75f));

                healPerimeterRing = CreateRing("Heal Range Perimeter", healPerimeterMat, 0.035f);
                healWaveRing = CreateRing("Heal Range Ripple", healWaveMat, 0.055f);
                SetCirclePositions(healPerimeterRing, HealNeighborRadius);
            }

            healPerimeterRing.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            healWaveRing.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        }

        private LineRenderer CreateRing(string ringName, Material mat, float width)
        {
            var go = new GameObject(ringName);
            go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            go.transform.localPosition = new Vector3(0f, 0.045f, 0f);
            var lr = go.AddComponent<LineRenderer>();
            lr.useWorldSpace = false;
            lr.loop = true;
            lr.positionCount = HealRingSegments;
            lr.widthMultiplier = width;
            lr.sharedMaterial = mat;
            lr.shadowCastingMode = ShadowCastingMode.Off;
            lr.receiveShadows = false;
            return lr;
        }

        private static void SetCirclePositions(LineRenderer lr, float radius)
        {
            var step = 2f * Mathf.PI / HealRingSegments;
            for (var i = 0; i < HealRingSegments; i++)
            {
                var angle = i * step;
                lr.SetPosition(i, new Vector3(Mathf.Cos(angle) * radius, 0f, Mathf.Sin(angle) * radius));
            }
        }

        private static Material CreateRingMaterial(Color color)
        {
            var shader = Shader.Find("Universal Render Pipeline/Unlit") ?? Shader.Find("Sprites/Default");
            var mat = new Material(shader) { renderQueue = 3000 };
            mat.SetColor("_BaseColor", color);
            mat.color = color;
            mat.SetFloat("_Surface", 1);
            mat.SetFloat("_SrcBlend", (float)BlendMode.SrcAlpha);
            mat.SetFloat("_DstBlend", (float)BlendMode.OneMinusSrcAlpha);
            mat.SetFloat("_ZWrite", 0);
            mat.SetFloat("_Cull", (float)CullMode.Off);
            mat.SetOverrideTag("RenderType", "Transparent");
            mat.EnableKeyword("_SURFACE_TYPE_TRANSPARENT");
            return mat;
        }

        private void UpdateHealingRangeAnimation()
        {
            if (state == null || !state.IsAlive || state.Role != UnitRole.Heal || healWaveRing == null) return;

            healBurstBoost = Mathf.MoveTowards(healBurstBoost, 0f, Time.deltaTime * 1.5f);
            healWavePhase = (healWavePhase + Time.deltaTime / SquadCombatRules.HealInterval) % 1f;

            var radius = Mathf.Lerp(0.25f, HealNeighborRadius, healWavePhase);
            SetCirclePositions(healWaveRing, radius);

            var waveAlpha = Mathf.Lerp(0.75f + 0.25f * healBurstBoost, 0.0f, healWavePhase * healWavePhase);
            var waveColor = new Color(0.35f, 1.00f, 0.60f, waveAlpha);
            healWaveMat.SetColor("_BaseColor", waveColor);
            healWaveMat.color = waveColor;

            var borderPulse = 0.30f + 0.15f * Mathf.Sin(Time.time * 3.5f) + 0.25f * healBurstBoost;
            var borderColor = new Color(0.25f, 0.95f, 0.50f, Mathf.Clamp01(borderPulse));
            healPerimeterMat.SetColor("_BaseColor", borderColor);
            healPerimeterMat.color = borderColor;
        }

        public void ApplyAppearance(UnitRole role)
        {
            if (colors == null) colors = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            var look = LegacyUnitLooks.For(role);
            var body = role == UnitRole.Test ? new Color(1, 0.85f, 0.2f) : look.Body;
            SetPart("Outline", look.Outline, true, new Vector3(look.OuterSize * LookScale, 0.26f, look.OuterSize * LookScale));
            SetPart("Body", body, true, new Vector3(look.InnerSize * LookScale, 0.008f, look.InnerSize * LookScale));
            var showMark = role != UnitRole.Test;
            SetPart("Mark Diamond", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Diamond,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.DiamondSize * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.DiamondSize * LookScale));
            SetPart("Mark Plate", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Plate,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.PlateSize * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.PlateSize * LookScale));
            SetPart("Mark Across", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Cross,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.CrossLength * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.CrossWidth * LookScale));
            SetPart("Mark Upright", look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Cross,
                new Vector3(SoldierLook.CrossWidth * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.CrossLength * LookScale));
            for (var i = 0; i < SoldierLook.CrownSegments.Length; i += 2)
            {
                var partName = "Mark Crown " + i;
                var part = transform.Find(partName);
                if (part == null && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Crown)
                {
                    var template = transform.Find("Mark Plate");
                    if (template == null) continue;
                    part = Instantiate(template, transform);
                    part.name = partName;
                }
                if (part == null) continue;
                var start = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i];
                var end = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i + 1];
                var delta = end - start;
                var center = (start + end) * (LookScale / 2);
                part.localPosition = new Vector3(center.x, 0.268f, center.y);
                part.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, -Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg, 0);
                SetPart(partName, look.MarkColor, showMark && look.Mark == SoldierMark.Crown,
                    new Vector3(delta.magnitude * LookScale, 0.004f, SoldierLook.CrownStroke * LookScale));
            }
        }

        private void SetPart(string partName, Color tint, bool visible, Vector3 scale)
        {
            var part = transform.Find(partName);
            if (part == null) return;
            part.gameObject.SetActive(visible);
            part.localScale = scale;
            colors.Clear();
            colors.SetColor("_BaseColor", tint);
            var renderer = part.GetComponent<Renderer>();
            if (renderer != null) renderer.SetPropertyBlock(colors);
        }

        public void SnapToState()
        {
            if (state == null) return;
            previousPosition = currentPosition = state.Position;
            transform.position = currentPosition;
            sampleTime = Time.timeAsDouble;
        }

        public void CaptureState()
        {
            previousPosition = currentPosition;
            currentPosition = state.Position;
            sampleTime = Time.timeAsDouble;
            sampleInterval = Time.fixedDeltaTime;
        }

        public void Present(float alpha)
        {
            transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(previousPosition, currentPosition, Mathf.Clamp01(alpha));
        }

        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            Present(sampleInterval > 0 ? (float)((Time.timeAsDouble - sampleTime) / sampleInterval) : 1);
            UpdateHealingRangeAnimation();
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (healPerimeterMat != null) Destroy(healPerimeterMat);
            if (healWaveMat != null) Destroy(healWaveMat);
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\CaptainGuide.cs === 
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class CaptainGuide : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const int CenterSlot = 4;
        private const int TrailDots = 10;
        private static readonly Vector2 EmblemOffset = new Vector2(0f, 45f);
        private static readonly Color Gold = new Color(1f, 0.84f, 0.25f);
        private static readonly Color ChipInk = new Color(0.1f, 0.08f, 0f);
        private static readonly Color PulseLow = new Color(1f, 0.64f, 0.08f);
        private static readonly Color PulseHigh = new Color(1f, 0.94f, 0.48f);

        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Seconds the ghost takes to glide into the center slot.")] private float glideSeconds = 1.4f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Seconds the ghost rests on the center slot before fading.")] private float holdSeconds = 0.5f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Seconds the ghost takes to fade out.")] private float fadeSeconds = 0.3f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Seconds between the fade and the next glide.")] private float idleSeconds = 0.6f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Seconds after the pointer is released before the ghost comes back.")] private float resumeDelay = 0.6f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Opacity of the ghost Captain.")] private float ghostAlpha = 0.78f;

        private readonly List<Image> trail = new List<Image>();
        private readonly Vector2[] cellPositions = new Vector2[9];
        private LegacyUpgradeScene owner;
        private RectTransform root;
        private CanvasGroup group;
        private RectTransform ghost;
        private CanvasGroup ghostGroup;
        private RectTransform pointer;
        private CanvasGroup pointerGroup;
        private Image centerCell;
        private Color centerColor;
        private Image cardFrame;
        private Color cardColor;
        private int cardIndex = -1;
        private float clock;
        private float resumeAt;
        private bool hidden, finished;

        public static CaptainGuide Attach(LegacyUpgradeScene owner, RectTransform root)
        {
            if (owner == null || root == null || owner.Preparation == null || owner.Grid == null) return null;
            if (owner.Preparation.DeployedCount > 0 || FindCaptain(owner.Preparation) == null) return null;
            var rect = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(root, "Captain Guide", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero);
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(rect);
            var guide = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<CaptainGuide>();
            guide.owner = owner;
            guide.root = root;
            guide.Build();
            return guide;
        }

        private void Build()
        {
            group = gameObject.AddComponent<CanvasGroup>();
            group.interactable = false;
            group.blocksRaycasts = false;
            var gridRect = (RectTransform)owner.Grid.transform;
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
            {
                var cell = gridRect.Find("Cell " + slot) as RectTransform;
                cellPositions[slot] = gridRect.anchoredPosition + (cell != null ? cell.anchoredPosition : Vector2.zero);
                if (slot == CenterSlot && cell != null) centerCell = cell.GetComponent<Image>();
            }
            if (centerCell != null) centerColor = centerCell.color;
            for (var i = 0; i < TrailDots; i++)
                trail.Add(LegacyUiKit.CreateCircle(transform, "Guide Trail", Vector2.zero, 6f, new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0f)));
            var icon = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(transform, Vector2.zero, 58f * 1.4f);
            icon.SetType(UnitRole.Captain);
            ghost = icon.Rect;
            ghostGroup = icon.gameObject.AddComponent<CanvasGroup>();
            ghostGroup.blocksRaycasts = false;
            BuildPointer();
            BuildChip("DRAG HERE", cellPositions[CenterSlot] + new Vector2(0f, 52f));
            ShowGhost(false);
        }

        private void BuildPointer()
        {
            pointer = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(transform, "Guide Pointer", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero);
            pointerGroup = pointer.gameObject.AddComponent<CanvasGroup>();
            pointerGroup.blocksRaycasts = false;
            Stroke("Tail Border", new Vector2(6f, -9f), new Vector2(7f, 14f), Color.black);
            Stroke("Tail", new Vector2(6f, -9f), new Vector2(3.5f, 11f), Color.white);
            Head("Head Border", 30f, Color.black);
            Head("Head", 22f, Color.white);
        }

        private void Stroke(string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, Color color)
        {
            var image = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(pointer, name, position, size, color);
            image.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 35f);
        }

        private void Head(string name, float size, Color color)
        {
            var image = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(pointer, name, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(size, size), color);
            image.sprite = NextMapShapes.Triangle;
            image.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 125f);
        }

        private void BuildChip(string text, Vector2 position)
        {
            var chip = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(transform, "Guide Chip", position, Vector2.zero);
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(chip, "Label", text, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(160f, 26f), 14, ChipInk, FontStyles.Bold, 3);
            var back = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(chip, "Back", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(label.GetPreferredValues(text).x + 22f, 28f), Gold, 14);
            back.transform.SetSiblingIndex(label.transform.GetSiblingIndex());
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (finished) return;
            var preparation = owner != null ? owner.Preparation : null;
            var captain = preparation != null ? FindCaptain(preparation) : null;
            if (captain == null || owner.Grid == null) { Finish(); return; }
            var slot = SlotOf(preparation, captain.MemberId);
            if (slot == CenterSlot) { Finish(); return; }
            var blocked = owner.IsOverlayOpen || BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives;
            SetHidden(blocked);
            if (blocked) return;
            if (transform.GetSiblingIndex() != root.childCount - 1) transform.SetAsLastSibling();
            var now = Time.unscaledTime;
            PulseCard(slot < 0 ? CardFrame(preparation, captain.MemberId) : null, now);
            var landed = false;
            if (PointerPressed()) resumeAt = now + resumeDelay;
            if (now < resumeAt)
            {
                ShowGhost(false);
                clock = 0f;
            }
            else
            {
                clock += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                var from = slot < 0 ? LegacyUpgradeScene.WarehouseCellPosition(cardIndex) + EmblemOffset : cellPositions[slot];
                landed = Animate(from, cellPositions[CenterSlot]);
            }
            if (centerCell != null)
                centerCell.color = Color.Lerp(centerColor, Gold, landed ? 0.65f : 0.15f + 0.35f * (Mathf.Sin(now * 6f) + 1f) / 2f);
        }

        private bool Animate(Vector2 from, Vector2 to)
        {
            var lap = glideSeconds + holdSeconds + fadeSeconds + idleSeconds;
            var t = Mathf.Repeat(clock, lap);
            var lift = Vector2.Distance(from, to) > 200f ? 140f : 50f;
            var control = new Vector2((from.x + to.x) / 2f, Mathf.Max(from.y, to.y) + lift);
            var progress = Ease(Mathf.Clamp01(t / glideSeconds));
            var shown = t < glideSeconds + holdSeconds + fadeSeconds;
            var fade = t < glideSeconds + holdSeconds ? 1f : Mathf.Clamp01(1f - (t - glideSeconds - holdSeconds) / fadeSeconds);
            var at = Curve(from, control, to, progress);
            ghost.anchoredPosition = at;
            ghostGroup.alpha = shown ? ghostAlpha * fade : 0f;
            pointer.anchoredPosition = at + new Vector2(15f, -17f);
            pointerGroup.alpha = t < glideSeconds ? 1f : 0f;
            for (var i = 0; i < trail.Count; i++)
            {
                trail[i].rectTransform.anchoredPosition = Curve(from, control, to, Mathf.Max(0f, progress - (i + 1) * 0.035f));
                trail[i].color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, shown ? 0.28f * (1f - (i + 1f) / (trail.Count + 1f)) * fade : 0f);
            }
            return t >= glideSeconds && t < glideSeconds + holdSeconds;
        }

        private void ShowGhost(bool value)
        {
            ghostGroup.alpha = value ? ghostAlpha : 0f;
            pointerGroup.alpha = value ? 1f : 0f;
            foreach (var dot in trail) dot.color = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0f);
        }

        private void SetHidden(bool value)
        {
            if (hidden == value) return;
            hidden = value;
            group.alpha = value ? 0f : 1f;
            if (!value) return;
            RestoreCard();
            if (centerCell != null) centerCell.color = centerColor;
        }

        private Image CardFrame(SquadPreparation preparation, int memberId)
        {
            var index = IndexOf(preparation, memberId);
            if (index == cardIndex && cardFrame != null) return cardFrame;
            cardIndex = index;
            var cell = index >= 0 ? root.Find("Warehouse Cell " + index) : null;
            return cell != null ? cell.GetComponent<Image>() : null;
        }

        private void PulseCard(Image frame, float now)
        {
            if (frame != cardFrame)
            {
                RestoreCard();
                cardFrame = frame;
                if (cardFrame != null) cardColor = cardFrame.color;
            }
            if (cardFrame != null) cardFrame.color = Color.Lerp(PulseLow, PulseHigh, (Mathf.Sin(now * 4f) + 1f) / 2f);
        }

        private void RestoreCard()
        {
            if (cardFrame != null) cardFrame.color = cardColor;
            cardFrame = null;
        }

        private void Finish()
        {
            if (finished) return;
            finished = true;
            RestoreCard();
            if (centerCell != null) centerCell.color = centerColor;
            Destroy(gameObject);
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            RestoreCard();
            if (centerCell != null) centerCell.color = centerColor;
        }

        private static bool PointerPressed()
        {
            var device = Pointer.current;
            return device != null && device.press.isPressed;
        }

        private static MemberSpawnDefinition FindCaptain(SquadPreparation preparation)
        {
            foreach (var member in preparation.Members)
                if (member.Unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain) return member;
            return null;
        }

        private static int IndexOf(SquadPreparation preparation, int memberId)
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < preparation.Members.Count; i++)
                if (preparation.Members[i].MemberId == memberId) return i;
            return -1;
        }

        private static int SlotOf(SquadPreparation preparation, int memberId)
        {
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                if (preparation.MemberAtSlot(slot) == memberId) return slot;
            return -1;
        }

        private static Vector2 Curve(Vector2 from, Vector2 control, Vector2 to, float t)
        {
            var u = 1f - t;
            return u * u * from + 2f * u * t * control + t * t * to;
        }

        private static float Ease(float t) => t < 0.5f ? 4f * t * t * t : 1f - Mathf.Pow(-2f * t + 2f, 3f) / 2f;
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyBattleHud.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Analytics;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem.UI;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Team5's authored HUD is preserved in the scene. This replaces only its owner bindings.
    public sealed class LegacyBattleHud : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private BattleSession session;
        [SerializeField] private Slider speedSlider;
        [SerializeField] private Button editButton;
        private TextMeshProUGUI levelLabel, livesLabel;
        private Canvas canvas;
        private TMP_FontAsset hudFont;
        private BattleCombatControls debugControls;
        private bool previousDebugPanel;
        private bool previousEditButtonActive;
        private GameObject ownedEventSystem;
        private LegacyFormationEditor editor;
        private BattleSimulation bound;
        private LegacyHealthBars healthBars;
        private Button pauseButton;
        private TMP_Text pauseLabel;
        private readonly Button[] squadButtons = new Button[3];
        private LegacyUnitIconUI squadIcon;
        private BattleUnitInspection inspection;
        private bool HasOpenEditor => editor != null && editor.gameObject.activeSelf;
        public Button SquadButton(int index) => squadButtons[index];
        public BattleSession Session => session;
        public Slider SpeedSlider => speedSlider;
        public Button EditButton => editButton;
        public Button PauseButton => pauseButton;
        public bool TutorialOwnsPause { get; set; }
        public bool TutorialAllowsSpeed { get; set; }
        public TMP_Text LevelLabel => levelLabel;
        public TMP_Text LivesLabel => livesLabel;
        public LegacyFormationEditor FormationEditor => editor;
        public bool CanEditFormation => isActiveAndEnabled && bound != null && bound.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running &&
            bound.AliveCount > 0 && bound.PendingSpawnCount == 0 && !HasOpenEditor;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene)) return;
            canvas = GetComponent<Canvas>(); canvas.enabled = true;
            if (session == null || speedSlider == null)
            { Debug.LogError("Legacy HUD needs its scene bindings.", this); return; }
            var authoredText = editButton != null ? editButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true) : GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true);
            hudFont = authoredText != null && authoredText.font != null ? authoredText.font : TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset;
            if (editButton != null)
            {
                previousEditButtonActive = editButton.gameObject.activeSelf;
                editButton.gameObject.SetActive(false); // Optional legacy entry; squad/skill buttons now handle editing.
            }
            debugControls = session.GetComponent<BattleCombatControls>();
            if (debugControls != null) { previousDebugPanel = debugControls.ShowDebugPanel; debugControls.ShowDebugPanel = false; }
            if (EventSystem.current == null)
            {
                ownedEventSystem = new GameObject("Battle UI EventSystem", typeof(EventSystem), typeof(InputSystemUIInputModule));
                ownedEventSystem.transform.SetParent(session.transform, false);
                ownedEventSystem.GetComponent<InputSystemUIInputModule>().AssignDefaultActions();
            }
            EnsureLevelLabel();
            EnsurePauseButton();
            EnsureSquadButtons();
            var barsRoot = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(transform, "World Health Bars", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero);
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(barsRoot);
            barsRoot.pivot = Vector2.zero;
            barsRoot.SetAsFirstSibling();
            healthBars = barsRoot.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyHealthBars>();
            session.SimulationCreated += OnSimulationCreated;
            OnSimulationCreated(session.Simulation);
            Refresh();
        }
        private void OnSimulationCreated(BattleSimulation next)
        {
            if (editor != null) { editor.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(editor.gameObject); editor = null; }
            bound = next;
            if (healthBars != null) healthBars.Bind(session, next);
            speedSlider.SetValueWithoutNotify(session.PlaybackSpeed - 1);
        }
        private GameObject recoveryPanel;
        private void Update()
        {
            Refresh();
            if (session != null && session.RecoveryRequired && recoveryPanel == null)
                recoveryPanel = LegacyLevelOneRecovery.Open(session);
        }
        private void Refresh()
        {
            if (session == null || levelLabel == null) return;
            levelLabel.text = BattleSceneFlow.IsTestRun ? "TEST LEVEL" : $"LEVEL {Mathf.Max(1, session.DisplayedLevelNumber)}";
            livesLabel.text = LegacyUiKit.LifeCounterText(session.AttemptsRemaining, session.AttemptsPerLevel);
            if (editButton != null) editButton.interactable = CanEditFormation;
            speedSlider.interactable = bound != null && bound.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && !HasOpenEditor && !session.IsUnitInspectionActive && (!TutorialOwnsPause || TutorialAllowsSpeed);
            pauseButton.interactable = bound != null && bound.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && !HasOpenEditor && !TutorialOwnsPause;
            pauseLabel.text = bound != null && bound.IsPaused ? "Resume" : "Pause";
            squadButtons[0].interactable = CanEditFormation || (inspection != null && inspection.IsSquadSelected);
            UnitState captain = null;
            if (bound != null)
                foreach (var unit in bound.Units)
                { if (captain == null) captain = unit; if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain) { captain = unit; break; } }
            squadIcon.SetType(captain?.Role ?? UnitRole.Test);
            squadButtons[0].targetGraphic.color = inspection != null && inspection.IsSquadSelected ? LegacyUiKit.NodeBlueColor : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor;
        }
        private void EnsureSquadButtons()
        {
            if (squadButtons[0] != null) { foreach (var button in squadButtons) button.gameObject.SetActive(true); return; }
            var scaler = canvas.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            var scale = scaler != null && scaler.uiScaleMode == CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize ? scaler.referenceResolution.x / 1280f : 1f;
            for (var i = 0; i < squadButtons.Length; i++)
            {
                var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(transform, "Squad Selection " + (i + 1), Vector2.zero,
                    Vector2.one * 56 * scale, LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 0);
                var rect = (RectTransform)button.transform;
                rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = rect.pivot = new Vector2(0, 1);
                rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(24, -124 - i * 68) * scale;
                button.interactable = false;
                squadButtons[i] = button;
                if (i == 0)
                {
                    squadIcon = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(rect, Vector2.zero, 110 * scale);
                    squadIcon.Rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(28, -28) * scale;
                    squadIcon.Rect.anchorMin = squadIcon.Rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0, 1);
                    button.onClick.AddListener(SelectSquad);
                }
            }
        }
        public void SelectSquad()
        {
            if (inspection == null)
                foreach (var view in FindObjectsByType<BattlePresentation>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                    if (view.Session == session) { inspection = view.GetComponent<BattleUnitInspection>(); break; }
            if (inspection == null) return;
            if (inspection.IsSquadSelected) inspection.CloseInspection();
            else inspection.SelectSquad(this);
            Refresh();
        }
        private void EnsurePauseButton()
        {
            if (pauseButton == null)
            {
                pauseButton = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(speedSlider.transform, "Pause Battle", Vector2.zero,
                    new Vector2(80, 22), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 3);
                var rect = (RectTransform)pauseButton.transform;
                rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 1);
                rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0, 30);
                pauseLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(rect, "Label", "Pause", Vector2.zero,
                    new Vector2(76, 20), 12, Color.white);
                pauseLabel.font = hudFont;
                pauseButton.onClick.AddListener(TogglePause);
            }
            pauseButton.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        }
        public void TogglePause()
        {
            if (bound == null || bound.Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running || HasOpenEditor || TutorialOwnsPause) return;
            bound.SetPaused(!bound.IsPaused);
            GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("pause_toggle", ("paused", bound.IsPaused));
            Refresh();
        }
        // Same method names as old persistent UnityEvents; only the receiver changes.
        public void SetSpeedFromSlider(float value)
        {
            var index = Mathf.RoundToInt(value);
            var previous = session.PlaybackSpeed;
            session.SetPlaybackSpeed(Mathf.Clamp(index + 1, 1, 3));
            if (session.PlaybackSpeed != previous)
                GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("speed_change", ("from", previous), ("to", session.PlaybackSpeed));
        }
        public void OpenSquadEditor()
        {
            if (!CanEditFormation) return;
            editor = LegacyFormationEditor.Open(this, bound); Refresh();
            GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("formation_open", ("source", "captain_skill"));
        }
        public LegacyFormationEditor OpenSquadEditorInPanel(RectTransform panel)
        {
            if (!CanEditFormation) return null;
            editor = LegacyFormationEditor.Open(this, bound, panel); Refresh();
            GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("formation_open", ("source", "squad_button"));
            return editor;
        }
        private void EnsureLevelLabel()
        {
            var existing = transform.Find("Level Label");
            if (existing != null)
            {
                levelLabel = existing.GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
                livesLabel = transform.Find("Death Count").GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>(); return;
            }
            // Unchanged layout/font calculation from OffenseWaveManager.EnsureLevelLabel.
            var scaler = canvas.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            var uiScale = scaler != null && scaler.uiScaleMode == CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize ? scaler.referenceResolution.x / 1280f : 1;
            levelLabel = CreateCornerLabel("Level Label", new Vector2(24, -24) * uiScale, new Vector2(240, 60) * uiScale, 36 * uiScale, FontStyles.Bold);
            livesLabel = CreateCornerLabel("Death Count", new Vector2(56, -76) * uiScale, new Vector2(208, 40) * uiScale, 24 * uiScale, FontStyles.Normal);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateHeartIcon(transform, new Vector2(24, -80) * uiScale, uiScale);
        }
        private TextMeshProUGUI CreateCornerLabel(string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, float fontSize, FontStyles style)
        {
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(transform, name, "", position, size, fontSize, Color.white, style);
            label.font = hudFont;
            label.rectTransform.anchorMin = label.rectTransform.anchorMax = label.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(0, 1);
            label.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = position;
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.TopLeft;
            return label;
        }
        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (recoveryPanel != null) { recoveryPanel.SetActive(false); Destroy(recoveryPanel); recoveryPanel = null; }
            if (inspection != null && inspection.IsSquadSelected) inspection.CloseImmediately();
            foreach (var button in squadButtons) if (button != null) button.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            if (pauseButton != null) pauseButton.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            if (editButton != null) editButton.gameObject.SetActive(previousEditButtonActive);
            if (session != null) session.SimulationCreated -= OnSimulationCreated;
            if (canvas != null) canvas.enabled = false;
            if (debugControls != null) debugControls.ShowDebugPanel = previousDebugPanel;
            if (editor != null) { editor.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(editor.gameObject); editor = null; }
            if (ownedEventSystem != null) { ownedEventSystem.SetActive(false); Destroy(ownedEventSystem); ownedEventSystem = null; }
            if (healthBars != null) { healthBars.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(healthBars.gameObject); healthBars = null; }
            bound = null;
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyClaimCardOverlay.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Analytics;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class LegacyClaimCardOverlay : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private LegacyUpgradeScene owner;

        public static LegacyClaimCardOverlay Open(LegacyUpgradeScene owner)
        {
            var offer = BattleSceneFlow.OpenSummonOffer();
            if (offer.Count == 0) return null;

            var host = new GameObject("Unit Summon Offer", typeof(RectTransform));
            var view = host.AddComponent<LegacyClaimCardOverlay>();
            view.owner = owner;

            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(host, 200, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0.5f);
            var backdrop = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(host.transform, "Backdrop", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0.02f, 0.03f, 0.05f, 0.96f));
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(backdrop.rectTransform);
            backdrop.raycastTarget = true;

            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(host.transform, "Title", "SUMMON NEW UNIT", new Vector2(0, 270), new Vector2(900, 50), 36, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            var shop = BattleSceneFlow.CardShop;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(host.transform, "BannerInfo", shop.GetBannerSummary(),
                new Vector2(0, 228), new Vector2(900, 30), 18, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            var balance = BattleSceneFlow.HasUnlimitedGold ? "UNLIMITED" : BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance.ToString();
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(host.transform, "Price", $"Cost: {shop.NextPrice} Gold  |  Balance: {balance}",
                new Vector2(0, 198), new Vector2(900, 32), 22, LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor);

            for (var i = 0; i < offer.Count; i++)
                view.BuildSummonCard(i, offer[i], new Vector2((i - (offer.Count - 1) * 0.5f) * 285, -10), shop);

            var cancel = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(host.transform, "Cancel", new Vector2(0, -255), new Vector2(240, 55), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 6);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(cancel.transform, "Label", "CANCEL", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(230, 50), 23, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            cancel.onClick.AddListener(() =>
            {
                GameAnalytics.Track("summon_cancel", ("price", shop.NextPrice), ("gold", BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance));
                view.Close();
            });
            GameAnalytics.Track("summon_offer", ("price", shop.NextPrice), ("gold", BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance),
                ("offer_0", PrepAnalytics.Describe(offer.Count > 0 ? offer[0] : null)),
                ("offer_1", PrepAnalytics.Describe(offer.Count > 1 ? offer[1] : null)),
                ("offer_2", PrepAnalytics.Describe(offer.Count > 2 ? offer[2] : null)));
            return view;
        }

        private void BuildSummonCard(int index, UnitDefinition rolledUnit, Vector2 position, ClaimCardShop shop)
        {
            var borderColor = LegacyUnitLooks.RarityColor(rolledUnit.Rarity);
            var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(transform, $"Summon Option {index}", position, new Vector2(260, 330), borderColor, 12);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(button.transform, "Face", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(252, 322), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 10);

            var introduction = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(button.transform, "Introduction", new Vector2(0, 20), new Vector2(200, 262));
            introduction.localScale = Vector3.one * 1.1f;
            LegacyUnitIntroductionUI.Build(introduction, rolledUnit, false);

            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(button.transform, "Cost", $"SUMMON ({shop.NextPrice}G)", new Vector2(0, -140),
                new Vector2(240, 35), 20, LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor, FontStyles.Bold);

            button.onClick.AddListener(() =>
            {
                var price = shop.NextPrice;
                var gold = BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance;
                if (!BattleSceneFlow.TrySummonUnit(index)) return;
                GameAnalytics.Track("summon_purchase", ("chosen_index", index), ("role", rolledUnit.Role.ToString()),
                    ("rarity", rolledUnit.Rarity.ToString()), ("unit_level", rolledUnit.Level), ("price", price),
                    ("gold_before", gold), ("gold_after", BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance));
                Close();
            });
        }

        public void Close()
        {
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
            if (owner != null) owner.OnClaimCardsClosed();
            Destroy(gameObject);
        }
    }
}.

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyFormationEditor.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Analytics;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Layout copied from Team5 BattleSquadEditor. Only its owner/data adapter changes.
    public sealed class LegacyFormationEditor : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private LegacyFormationGrid grid;
        private LegacyBattleHud owner;
        private TextMeshProUGUI error;
        private bool wasPaused;
        public LegacyFormationGrid Grid => grid;
        public static LegacyFormationEditor Open(LegacyBattleHud owner, BattleSimulation simulation, RectTransform inspectionPanel = null)
        {
            var host = new GameObject("Battle Squad Editor", typeof(RectTransform));
            host.transform.SetParent(inspectionPanel != null ? inspectionPanel : owner.Session.transform, false);
            var editor = host.AddComponent<LegacyFormationEditor>();
            editor.owner = owner; editor.simulation = simulation; editor.wasPaused = simulation.IsPaused;
            simulation.SetPaused(true); editor.Build(inspectionPanel != null); return editor;
        }
        private void Build(bool embedded)
        {
            var root = (RectTransform)transform;
            if (embedded) LegacyUiKit.Stretch(root);
            else
            {
                LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(gameObject, 800, new Vector2(1280, 720), 1);
                var backdrop = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Input Blocker", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0, 0, 0, 0.15f));
                LegacyUiKit.Stretch(backdrop.rectTransform); backdrop.raycastTarget = true;
            }
            var panel = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Panel", new Vector2(-20, -20), new Vector2(320, 410), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 12);
            if (embedded)
            {
                panel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = Vector2.zero;
            }
            else
            {
                panel.rectTransform.anchorMin = panel.rectTransform.anchorMax = panel.rectTransform.pivot = Vector2.one;
                panel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-20, -20);
            }
            panel.raycastTarget = true;
            var content = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(panel.transform, "Content", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(content, "Title", "EDIT SQUAD", new Vector2(0, 174), new Vector2(280, 30), 24, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(content, "Paused", "Battle paused", new Vector2(0, 145), new Vector2(280, 24), 16, LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor);
            grid = LegacyFormationGrid.Create(content, root, simulation);
            var done = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(content, "Apply And Resume", new Vector2(0, -170), new Vector2(260, 48), LegacyUiKit.NodeGreenColor, 8);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(done.transform, "Label", "APPLY & RESUME", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(250, 40), 20, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            done.onClick.AddListener(() => ApplyAndResume());
            error = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(content, "Error", "", new Vector2(0, -136), new Vector2(290, 20), 11, Color.red);
            if (EventSystem.current != null) EventSystem.current.SetSelectedGameObject(null);
        }
        public bool ApplyAndResume()
        {
            var sourceSlots = grid.CopySourceSlots();
            var moved = BattleAnalytics.MovedMembers(simulation, sourceSlots);
            if (owner.Session.Simulation != simulation || !simulation.TryApplyFormation(sourceSlots))
            {
                GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("formation_edit", ("result", "rejected"), ("members_moved", 0));
                error.text = "Formation cannot be applied."; return false;
            }
            GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("formation_edit", ("result", "applied"), ("members_moved", moved));
            simulation.SetPaused(wasPaused);
            gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(gameObject); return true;
        }
        public void CancelAndClose()
        {
            if (owner != null && owner.Session.Simulation == simulation) simulation.SetPaused(wasPaused);
            GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("formation_edit", ("result", "cancel"), ("members_moved", 0));
            gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(gameObject);
        }
        // Removing a view must not change the pause or battle state.
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyFormationGrid.cs === 
using System;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Battle editing uses a slot permutation; preparation delegates to the bounded deployment model.
    // Neither mode changes a live simulation's state directly.
    public sealed class LegacyFormationGrid : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private readonly Image[] cells = new Image[9];
        private readonly LegacyUnitIconUI[] icons = new LegacyUnitIconUI[9];
        private readonly int[] sourceSlots = new int[9];
        private Func<int, UnitRole> roleAtSource;
        private SquadPreparation preparation;
        private RectTransform dragLayer;
        private LegacyUnitIconUI ghost;
        private int dragFrom = -1, selected = -1;
        public int[] CopySourceSlots() => (int[])sourceSlots.Clone();

        public static LegacyFormationGrid Create(Transform parent, RectTransform dragLayer, BattleSimulation simulation)
            => Create(parent, dragLayer, slot => simulation.GetMemberAtSlot(slot)?.Role ?? UnitRole.Test);

        public static LegacyFormationGrid CreateForPreparation(Transform parent, RectTransform dragLayer, SquadPreparation preparation)
        {
            var grid = Create(parent, dragLayer, preparation.RoleAtSlot);
            grid.preparation = preparation;
            preparation.Changed += grid.Refresh;
            grid.transform.Find("Hint").GetComponent<TMPro.TMP_Text>().text = "Drag out to return unit";
            return grid;
        }
        public bool TryPlaceWarehouseMember(int memberId, PointerEventData data)
            => preparation != null && preparation.TryAssign(memberId, FindCell(data));

        public static LegacyFormationGrid Create(Transform parent, RectTransform dragLayer, Func<int, UnitRole> roleAtSource)
        {
            const float cell = 58, gap = 8, size = cell * 3 + gap * 2;
            var rect = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(parent, "Squad Grid", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * size);
            var grid = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyFormationGrid>();
            grid.roleAtSource = roleAtSource; grid.dragLayer = dragLayer;
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
            {
                grid.sourceSlots[slot] = slot;
                // Native X/Z slots: +Z is the top row. Member IDs are not UI indices.
                var position = new Vector2(slot % 3 - 1, slot / 3 - 1) * (cell + gap);
                var image = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(rect, "Cell " + slot, position, Vector2.one * cell, LegacyUiKit.CellColor, 8);
                image.raycastTarget = true;
                var outline = image.gameObject.AddComponent<Outline>();
                outline.effectColor = LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor; outline.effectDistance = new Vector2(1.5f, -1.5f);
                image.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyFormationCellUI>().Initialize(grid, slot);
                grid.cells[slot] = image;
                grid.icons[slot] = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(image.transform, Vector2.zero, cell * 1.4f);
            }
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(rect, "Top", "TOP", new Vector2(0, size / 2 + 22), new Vector2(size, 18), 12,
                LegacyUiKit.MutedColor, TMPro.FontStyles.Bold, 6);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(rect, "Hint", "Drag to swap", new Vector2(0, -size / 2 - 26), new Vector2(size + 120, 22), 14, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            grid.Refresh(); return grid;
        }
        private UnitRole RoleAt(int slot) => preparation != null ? preparation.RoleAtSlot(slot) : roleAtSource(sourceSlots[slot]);
        private void Refresh()
        {
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
            {
                icons[slot].SetType(RoleAt(slot));
                icons[slot].gameObject.SetActive(dragFrom != slot);
                cells[slot].GetComponent<Outline>().effectColor = selected == slot ? LegacyUiKit.DropColor : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor;
            }
        }
        public void HandleClick(int slot)
        {
            if (selected < 0) selected = slot;
            else { Swap(selected, slot); selected = -1; }
            Refresh();
        }
        private void Swap(int first, int second)
        {
            if (preparation != null) { preparation.TryMove(first, second); return; }
            var value = sourceSlots[first]; sourceSlots[first] = sourceSlots[second]; sourceSlots[second] = value;
        }
        public void HandleBeginDrag(int slot, PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            if (RoleAt(slot) == UnitRole.Test) return;
            dragFrom = slot; selected = -1;
            ghost = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(dragLayer, Vector2.zero, 58 * 1.4f * 1.15f);
            ghost.SetType(RoleAt(slot)); ghost.transform.SetAsLastSibling();
            MoveGhost(eventData); Refresh();
        }
        public void HandleDrag(PointerEventData eventData) { if (dragFrom >= 0) MoveGhost(eventData); }
        public void HandleEndDrag(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            if (dragFrom < 0) return;
            var target = FindCell(eventData); var from = dragFrom; dragFrom = -1;
            if (ghost != null) Destroy(ghost.gameObject);
            ghost = null;
            if (target >= 0 && target != from) Swap(from, target);
            else if (target < 0 && preparation != null) preparation.TryRemove(from);
            Refresh();
        }
        private void MoveGhost(PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (ghost != null && RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle(dragLayer, data.position, data.pressEventCamera, out var point))
                ghost.Rect.anchoredPosition = point;
        }
        private int FindCell(PointerEventData data)
        {
            // Use the UI raycaster's actual hovered cell first (also handles scaled Game Views).
            var hit = data.pointerCurrentRaycast.gameObject;
            if (hit != null)
                for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                    if (hit.transform == cells[slot].transform || hit.transform.IsChildOf(cells[slot].transform)) return slot;
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                if (RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint(cells[slot].rectTransform, data.position, data.pressEventCamera)) return slot;
            return -1;
        }
        private void OnDisable() { if (ghost != null) Destroy(ghost.gameObject); ghost = null; dragFrom = -1; }
        private void OnDestroy() { if (preparation != null) preparation.Changed -= Refresh; }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyHealthBars.cs === 
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Old HealthBar palette and left-aligned fill, adapted to an overlay for the 3D scene.
    public sealed class LegacyHealthBars : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private sealed class Bar { public RectTransform Root; public Image Fill; public float Width, Height, Inset; }
        private static readonly Color backgroundColor = new Color(0.055f, 0.067f, 0.082f, 0.92f);
        private static readonly Color fullColor = new Color(0.35f, 0.85f, 0.4f);
        private static readonly Color emptyColor = new Color(0.9f, 0.25f, 0.2f);
        private readonly Dictionary<int, Bar> unitBars = new Dictionary<int, Bar>();
        private readonly Dictionary<int, Bar> towerBars = new Dictionary<int, Bar>();
        private readonly HashSet<int> damaged = new HashSet<int>();
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private BattleSession session;
        private BattlePresentation presentation;
        public void Bind(BattleSession owner, BattleSimulation next)
        {
            if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged -= OnDamaged;
            foreach (Transform child in transform) { child.gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(child.gameObject); }
            unitBars.Clear(); towerBars.Clear(); damaged.Clear();
            session = owner; simulation = next;
            presentation = null;
            foreach (var view in Object.FindObjectsByType<BattlePresentation>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                if (view.Session == owner) { presentation = view; break; }
            if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged += OnDamaged;
        }
        private void OnDamaged(DamageEvent value) => damaged.Add(value.UnitId);
        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            var camera = Camera.main;
            if (simulation == null || camera == null) return;
            foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
            {
                if (!unitBars.TryGetValue(unit.Id, out var bar))
                { bar = Create("Unit HP " + unit.Id, 9, 1.5f, 0.3f); unitBars.Add(unit.Id, bar); }
                var position = unit.Position + Vector3.up * 0.9f;
                Transform model = null;
                if (presentation != null && presentation.TryGetView(unit.Id, out var view)) model = view.transform;
                Present(bar, position, unit.Hp / unit.MaxHp, unit.IsAlive && (damaged.Contains(unit.Id) || unit.Hp < unit.MaxHp), camera, model);
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < simulation.Towers.Count; i++)
            {
                var tower = simulation.Towers[i];
                if (!towerBars.TryGetValue(tower.Id, out var bar))
                { bar = Create("Tower HP " + tower.Id, 28, 3.5f, 0.6f); towerBars.Add(tower.Id, bar); }
                Present(bar, tower.Position + Vector3.up * 1.5f, tower.Hp / tower.Definition.MaxHp, tower.IsAlive, camera, session.Towers[i].transform);
            }
        }
        private Bar Create(string name, float width, float height, float inset)
        {
            var background = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, name, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(width, height), backgroundColor);
            var rect = background.rectTransform;
            rect.anchorMin = rect.anchorMax = Vector2.zero;
            var fill = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(rect, "Fill", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(width - inset * 2, height - inset * 2), fullColor);
            fill.rectTransform.anchorMin = fill.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(0, 0.5f);
            fill.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(0, 0.5f);
            fill.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(inset, 0);
            return new Bar { Root = rect, Fill = fill, Width = width, Height = height, Inset = inset };
        }
        private void Present(Bar bar, Vector3 position, float fraction, bool visible, Camera camera, Transform model)
        {
            var screen = camera.WorldToScreenPoint(position);
            if (model != null)
            {
                var renderers = model.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>();
                if (renderers.Length > 0)
                {
                    var bounds = renderers[0].bounds;
                    foreach (var renderer in renderers) bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
                    var top = float.MinValue;
                    for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
                    for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
                    for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
                        top = Mathf.Max(top, camera.WorldToScreenPoint(bounds.center + Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z))).y);
                    screen = camera.WorldToScreenPoint(bounds.center);
                    screen.y = top + (bar.Height / 2 + 1) * GetComponentInParent<Canvas>().scaleFactor;
                }
            }
            bar.Root.gameObject.SetActive(visible && screen.z > 0);
            if (!visible) return;
            if (RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle((RectTransform)transform, screen, null, out var local))
                bar.Root.anchoredPosition = local;
            fraction = Mathf.Clamp01(fraction);
            bar.Fill.rectTransform.sizeDelta = new Vector2((bar.Width - bar.Inset * 2) * fraction, bar.Height - bar.Inset * 2);
            bar.Fill.color = Color.Lerp(emptyColor, fullColor, fraction);
        }
        private void OnDisable() { if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged -= OnDamaged; }
    }
}
.

using Team5.Rebuild.Analytics;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Adapted from Team5 HowToPlay.ShowLevelOneDefeat and OutOfLivesScreen.
    public sealed class LegacyLevelOneRecovery : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private BattleSession session;
        public static GameObject Open(BattleSession session)
        {
            var tutorial = session.GetComponent<LevelOneTutorial>();
            if (tutorial != null) tutorial.SuspendForDefeat();
            var host = new GameObject("Level Recovery", typeof(RectTransform));
            // A nested Canvas inherits the HUD's reference scale and ignores its own scaler.
            // Parent under the non-UI session so this is a root Canvas with its own sizing.
            host.transform.SetParent(session.transform, false);
            var view = host.AddComponent<LegacyLevelOneRecovery>();
            view.session = session;
            view.Build();
            return host;
        }

        private void Build()
        {
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(gameObject, 2000, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0.5f);
            var backdrop = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Backdrop", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0.025f, 0.035f, 0.055f, 0.95f));
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(backdrop.rectTransform); backdrop.raycastTarget = true;
            var card = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Recovery Card", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(760, 400), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 12).transform;
            var exhausted = session.AttemptsRemaining == 0;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(card, "Title", exhausted ? "OUT OF LIVES" : "SQUAD DEFEATED", new Vector2(0, 140), new Vector2(700, 55), 34, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(card, "Description", exhausted
                ? $"You have used all {session.AttemptsPerLevel} lives.\nExit and restart the game to try again."
                : $"Lives: {session.AttemptsRemaining}/{session.AttemptsPerLevel}\nRestart, or return to the last tutorial hint.\nRewinding does not refund a lost life.",
                new Vector2(0, 45), new Vector2(700, 110), 23, LegacyUiKit.TextColor);
            if (!exhausted)
            {
                Button(card, "Restart", "RESTART LEVEL", new Vector2(-170, -50), BattleSceneFlow.RestartCurrentLevel);
                var rewind = Button(card, "Rewind", "BACK TO LAST HINT", new Vector2(170, -50), () => BattleSceneFlow.RewindLevelOne());
                rewind.interactable = session.HasTutorialCheckpoint;
            }
            Button(card, "Exit", "EXIT & RESTART GAME", new Vector2(0, -140), BattleSceneFlow.ReturnToStart);
        }

        private static Button Button(Transform parent, string name, string label, Vector2 position, UnityEngine.Events.UnityAction action)
        {
            var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(parent, name, position, new Vector2(320, 58), LegacyUiKit.NodeBlueColor, 6);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(button.transform, "Label", label, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(310, 54), 21, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            button.onClick.AddListener(() => GameAnalytics.Track("recovery_choice", ("choice", name.ToLowerInvariant())));
            button.onClick.AddListener(action);
            return button;
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (session != null && session.RecoveryRequired) return;
            gameObject.SetActive(false); Destroy(gameObject);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyLevelSettlement.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class LegacyLevelSettlement : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text missionHeading;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text rewardHeading;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text rewardAmount;
        [SerializeField] private TMP_Text rewardDescription;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            var victory = BattleSceneFlow.LastOutcome != BattleOutcome.Defeat;
            var level = Mathf.Max(1, BattleSceneFlow.CompletedLevelNumber);
            missionHeading.text = victory ? "MISSION COMPLETE" : "MISSION FAILED";
            rewardHeading.text = $"LEVEL {level} {(victory ? "COMPLETE" : "RETRY")}";

            if (victory)
            {
                var totalGold = BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardGold;
                var stageGold = BattleSceneFlow.LastStageClearGold;
                var towerGold = BattleSceneFlow.LastTowerBonusGold;
                var towersKilled = BattleSceneFlow.LastTowersDestroyedCount;

                if (!BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive)
                {
                    var settings = Resources.Load<GameEconomySettings>("RebuildEconomy");
                    stageGold = settings != null ? settings.GetStageClearGold(level) : level * 45;
                    totalGold = stageGold;
                }

                rewardAmount.text = totalGold > 0 ? $"+{totalGold:N0} GOLD" : "STAGE CLEARED";
                var bountyLine = towersKilled > 0
                    ? $"Stage Clear: +{stageGold}G  |  Towers Destroyed ({towersKilled}): +{towerGold}G\n"
                    : $"Stage Clear: +{stageGold}G  |  Destroy enemy towers with Attack units for bonus Gold!\n";
                rewardDescription.text = $"{bountyLine}Summon Tier {level} Unlocked! Higher levels & rarity odds available.";
            }
            else
            {
                rewardAmount.text = "REGROUP";
                rewardDescription.text = level == 1
                    ? "Try again with your Captain. Level 1 uses its fixed squad."
                    : "Summon stronger units or rearrange your squad on the Upgrade screen.";
            }
        }

        public void ContinueToUpgrade() => BattleSceneFlow.ContinueToUpgrade();
    }
}.
