using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Loads the actual upcoming Scene temporarily, without simulation or battle UI.
    public sealed class LegacyNextLevelPreview : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const int PreviewLayer = 31;
        private const float Left = -536f;
        private static readonly Vector2 CardSize = new Vector2(1136f, 652f);
        private static readonly Vector2 MapCenter = new Vector2(0f, 4f);
        private static readonly Vector2 MapSize = new Vector2(1072f, 480f);
        private static readonly Quaternion BoardRotation = Quaternion.Euler(90f, 0f, 0f);
        private static readonly Color DimColor = new Color(0.024f, 0.035f, 0.06f, 0.74f);
        private static readonly Color StartInk = new Color(0.196f, 0.196f, 0.196f);

        private readonly List<TowerRangeView> ranges = new List<TowerRangeView>();
        private readonly List<Camera> strippedCameras = new List<Camera>();
        private LegacyUpgradeScene owner;
        private string path;
        private Scene scene;
        private AsyncOperation load;
        private RectTransform overlayRoot;
        private RawImage image;
        private TMP_Text status;
        private TMP_Text facts;
        private Button startButton;
        private RenderTexture texture;
        private Camera previewCamera;
        private NextMapData data;
        private bool closed, loadRunning = true, finishing, ended, neutralized;

        public bool IsReady { get; private set; }
        public Scene LoadedScene => scene;

        public static LegacyNextLevelPreview Open(LegacyUpgradeScene owner)
        {
            if (!Application.CanStreamedLevelBeLoaded(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleScenePath) || !BattleSceneFlow.TryBeginLevelPreview()) return null;
            var host = new GameObject("Next Level Preview", typeof(RectTransform));
            var view = host.AddComponent<LegacyNextLevelPreview>();
            view.owner = owner;
            view.path = BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleScenePath;
            view.StripPreviewLayer();
            view.Build();
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded += view.OnPreviewSceneLoaded;
            view.StartCoroutine(view.LoadMap());
            return view;
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (startButton != null) startButton.interactable = !closed && owner != null && owner.CanStartWave;
        }

        private void Build()
        {
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(gameObject, 500, new Vector2(1280f, 720f), 0f);
            var dim = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Dim", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, DimColor);
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(dim.rectTransform);
            dim.raycastTarget = true;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Card Frame", Vector2.zero, CardSize + new Vector2(4f, 4f), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 17);
            var card = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Card", Vector2.zero, CardSize, LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 16).rectTransform;
            BuildHeader(card);
            LabeledButton(card, "Close", "X", new Vector2(514f, 282f), new Vector2(44f, 40f), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, Color.white, 22, true)
                .onClick.AddListener(Close);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(card, "Map Frame", MapCenter, MapSize + new Vector2(3f, 3f), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 11);
            var panel = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(card, "Map Panel", MapCenter, MapSize, LegacyUiKit.CellColor, 10);
            panel.gameObject.AddComponent<Mask>().showMaskGraphic = true;
            var map = new GameObject("Map", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(RawImage));
            map.transform.SetParent(panel.transform, false);
            image = map.GetComponent<RawImage>();
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(image.rectTransform);
            image.raycastTarget = false;
            image.enabled = false;
            overlayRoot = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(panel.transform, "Overlay", Vector2.zero, MapSize);
            overlayRoot.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            status = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel.transform, "Status", "Loading map...", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(700f, 60f), 24, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            NextMapOverlay.Legend(card, new Vector2(Left, -280f));
            LabeledButton(card, "Back", "BACK", new Vector2(247f, -280f), new Vector2(130f, 52f), LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, Color.white, 20, true)
                .onClick.AddListener(Close);
            startButton = LabeledButton(card, "Start Wave", "Start Wave", new Vector2(431f, -280f), new Vector2(210f, 52f), Color.white, StartInk, 26, false);
            startButton.onClick.AddListener(StartWave);
        }

        private void BuildHeader(RectTransform card)
        {
            var name = BattleSceneFlow.IsTestRun ? "TEST LEVEL" : "LEVEL " + Mathf.Max(1, BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber);
            var title = LeftText(card, "Title", name, Left, 282f, 32, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            var x = Left + title.GetPreferredValues(name).x + 26f;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateHeartIcon(card, Vector2.zero, 0.8f);
            var heart = (RectTransform)card.Find("Heart Icon");
            heart.anchorMin = heart.anchorMax = heart.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            heart.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(x, 282f);
            var lives = BattleSceneFlow.CurrentLevelLivesRemaining + "/" + BattleSession.DefaultLivesPerLevel;
            var livesLabel = LeftText(card, "Lives", lives, x + 14f, 281f, 20, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            facts = LeftText(card, "Facts", string.Empty, x + 14f + livesLabel.GetPreferredValues(lives).x + 22f, 281f, 18, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor, FontStyles.Normal);
        }

        private static TMP_Text LeftText(Transform parent, string name, string text, float left, float y, float size, Color color, FontStyles style)
        {
            const float width = 600f;
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(parent, name, text, new Vector2(left + width / 2f, y), new Vector2(width, 44f), size, color, style);
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Left;
            return label;
        }

        private static Button LabeledButton(Transform parent, string name, string text, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, Color fill, Color ink, float fontSize, bool bold)
        {
            var button = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(parent, name, position, size, fill, 8);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(button.transform, "Label", text, Vector2.zero, size, fontSize, ink, bold ? FontStyles.Bold : FontStyles.Normal);
            return button;
        }

        private void StartWave()
        {
            if (owner != null) owner.StartWave();
        }

        private void OnPreviewSceneLoaded(Scene loaded, LoadSceneMode mode)
        {
            if (loaded.path != path) return;
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnPreviewSceneLoaded;
            scene = loaded;
            Neutralize();
        }

        private void Neutralize()
        {
            if (neutralized || !scene.IsValid() || !scene.isLoaded) return;
            neutralized = true;
            foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
            {
                foreach (var behaviour in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Behaviour>(true)) behaviour.enabled = false;
                foreach (var collider in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Collider>(true)) collider.enabled = false;
                foreach (var child in root.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true)) child.gameObject.layer = PreviewLayer;
            }
        }

        private IEnumerator LoadMap()
        {
            try { load = SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(path, LoadSceneMode.Additive); }
            catch (System.Exception error) { status.text = "Unable to load map."; Debug.LogException(error, this); }
            if (load == null) { loadRunning = false; if (closed) yield return Finish(); yield break; }
            yield return load;
            if (!scene.IsValid()) scene = SceneManager.GetSceneByPath(path);
            Neutralize();
            if (closed) { loadRunning = false; yield return Finish(); yield break; }
            var prepared = false;
            try { prepared = PrepareMap(); }
            catch (System.Exception error) { Debug.LogException(error, this); }
            if (!prepared) { loadRunning = false; status.text = "Map preview unavailable"; yield break; }
            yield return null; // Let the camera render once before displaying its texture.
            loadRunning = false;
            if (closed) { yield return Finish(); yield break; }
            image.texture = texture;
            image.enabled = true;
            status.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            overlayRoot.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            IsReady = true;
        }

        private bool PrepareMap()
        {
            data = NextMapData.Capture(scene);
            if (!data.HasMap) return false;
            for (var i = 0; i < data.Towers.Count; i++)
            {
                var rangeObject = new GameObject("Preview Tower Range " + (i + 1));
                SceneManager.MoveGameObjectToScene(rangeObject, scene);
                var range = rangeObject.AddComponent<TowerRangeView>();
                range.BindPreview(data.Towers[i].Definition);
                foreach (var child in rangeObject.GetComponentsInChildren<Transform>(true)) child.gameObject.layer = PreviewLayer;
                ranges.Add(range);
            }
            var projection = NextMapProjection.Fit(BoardRotation, FitPoints(), MapSize, 8f, 30f, 8f);
            var height = Mathf.Clamp(Mathf.RoundToInt(MapSize.y * Screen.width / 1280f), 270, 1080);
            var width = Mathf.RoundToInt(height * MapSize.x / MapSize.y);
            texture = new RenderTexture(width, height, 24) { name = "Next Map", antiAliasing = 4 };
            texture.Create();
            var cameraObject = new GameObject("Preview Camera");
            SceneManager.MoveGameObjectToScene(cameraObject, scene);
            previewCamera = cameraObject.AddComponent<Camera>();
            previewCamera.targetTexture = texture;
            previewCamera.cullingMask = 1 << PreviewLayer;
            previewCamera.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
            previewCamera.backgroundColor = LegacyUiKit.CellColor;
            projection.Apply(previewCamera);
            var lightObject = new GameObject("Preview Light");
            SceneManager.MoveGameObjectToScene(lightObject, scene);
            var lightSource = lightObject.AddComponent<Light>();
            lightSource.type = LightType.Directional;
            lightSource.intensity = 1.2f;
            lightSource.cullingMask = 1 << PreviewLayer;
            lightSource.shadows = LightShadows.None;
            lightObject.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(50f, -30f, 0f);
            var count = data.Towers.Count;
            facts.text = count + (count == 1 ? " tower" : " towers") + "  |  road " + data.Route.Count + " tiles";
            overlayRoot.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            overlayRoot.gameObject.AddComponent<NextMapOverlay>().Build(data, projection, ranges);
            overlayRoot.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            return true;
        }

        private IEnumerable<Vector3> FitPoints()
        {
            foreach (var corner in Corners(data.Cells)) yield return corner;
            foreach (var tower in data.Towers)
            {
                if (tower.HasModel)
                    foreach (var corner in Corners(tower.Model)) yield return corner;
                var lane = tower.Lane;
                foreach (var corner in Corners(new Bounds(lane.center + Vector3.up * 0.035f, lane.size))) yield return corner;
            }
        }

        private static IEnumerable<Vector3> Corners(Bounds bounds)
        {
            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
                for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
                    for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
                        yield return bounds.center + Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z));
        }

        private void StripPreviewLayer()
        {
            foreach (var camera in FindObjectsByType<Camera>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
            {
                if ((camera.cullingMask & (1 << PreviewLayer)) == 0) continue;
                camera.cullingMask &= ~(1 << PreviewLayer);
                strippedCameras.Add(camera);
            }
        }

        private void RestoreLayer()
        {
            foreach (var camera in strippedCameras)
                if (camera != null) camera.cullingMask |= 1 << PreviewLayer;
            strippedCameras.Clear();
        }

        public void Close()
        {
            if (closed) return;
            closed = true;
            IsReady = false;
            if (!loadRunning) StartCoroutine(Finish());
        }

        private IEnumerator Finish()
        {
            if (finishing) yield break;
            finishing = true;
            if (previewCamera != null) previewCamera.enabled = false;
            image.enabled = false;
            overlayRoot.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            status.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            status.text = "Closing preview...";
            if (scene.IsValid() && scene.isLoaded) yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(scene);
            BattleSceneFlow.EndLevelPreview(path);
            ended = true;
            RestoreLayer();
            if (owner != null) owner.OnNextLevelPreviewClosed();
            Destroy(gameObject);
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnPreviewSceneLoaded;
            RestoreLayer();
            if (previewCamera != null) { previewCamera.enabled = false; previewCamera.targetTexture = null; }
            if (texture != null) { texture.Release(); Destroy(texture); }
            if (ended) return;
            if (scene.IsValid() && scene.isLoaded) SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(scene);
            if (load != null && !load.isDone)
            {
                var pendingPath = path;
                UnityEngine.Events.UnityAction<Scene, LoadSceneMode> cleanup = null;
                cleanup = (loaded, mode) =>
                {
                    if (loaded.path != pendingPath) return;
                    SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= cleanup;
                    foreach (var root in loaded.GetRootGameObjects()) root.SetActive(false);
                    SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(loaded);
                    BattleSceneFlow.EndLevelPreview(pendingPath);
                };
                SceneManager.sceneLoaded += cleanup;
            }
            else BattleSceneFlow.EndLevelPreview(path);
        }
    }
}
.

using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public static class LegacyUiKit
    {
        public static readonly Color PanelColor = new Color(0.11f, 0.14f, 0.21f);
        public static readonly Color PanelLineColor = new Color(0.3f, 0.36f, 0.46f);
        public static readonly Color CellColor = new Color(0.07f, 0.09f, 0.14f);
        public static readonly Color TextColor = new Color(0.92f, 0.94f, 0.97f);
        public static readonly Color MutedColor = new Color(0.62f, 0.67f, 0.75f);
        public static readonly Color HighlightColor = new Color(1f, 0.88f, 0.25f);
        public static readonly Color NodeGreenColor = new Color(0.18f, 0.65f, 0.22f);
        public static readonly Color NodeGreyColor = new Color(0.25f, 0.25f, 0.25f);
        public static readonly Color NodeBlueColor = new Color(0.15f, 0.35f, 0.7f);
        public static readonly Color DropColor = new Color(0.35f, 0.85f, 0.45f);
        public static readonly Color LockColor = new Color(0.86f, 0.88f, 0.92f);

        private const int RoundedTextureSize = 64;
        private const float RoundedTextureRadius = 16f;
        private const float RoundedPixelsPerUnit = 100f;

        private static Sprite rounded;
        private static Sprite circle;

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void ResetStatics()
        {
            rounded = null;
            circle = null;
        }

        public static string LifeCounterText(int remaining, int limit) =>
            $": {Mathf.Clamp(remaining, 0, limit)}/{limit}";

        public static void CreateHeartIcon(Transform parent, Vector2 topLeftPosition, float scale = 1f)
        {
            var root = CreateRect(parent, "Heart Icon", topLeftPosition, new Vector2(24f, 24f));
            root.anchorMin = root.anchorMax = root.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 1f);
            root.anchoredPosition = topLeftPosition;
            root.localScale = new Vector3(scale, scale, 1f);
            var red = new Color(1f, 0.2f, 0.3f, 1f);
            var tip = CreateImage(root, "Heart Tip", new Vector2(0f, -2f), new Vector2(14f, 14f), red);
            tip.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 45f);
            CreateCircle(root, "Heart Left", new Vector2(-5f, 3f), 14f, red);
            CreateCircle(root, "Heart Right", new Vector2(5f, 3f), 14f, red);
        }

        public static Canvas CreateCanvas(GameObject host, int sortingOrder, Vector2 referenceResolution, float matchWidthOrHeight)
        {
            var canvas = host.AddComponent<Canvas>();
            canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
            canvas.sortingOrder = sortingOrder;

            var scaler = host.AddComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
            scaler.referenceResolution = referenceResolution;
            scaler.screenMatchMode = CanvasScaler.ScreenMatchMode.MatchWidthOrHeight;
            scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = matchWidthOrHeight;

            host.AddComponent<GraphicRaycaster>();
            return canvas;
        }

        public static RectTransform CreateRect(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size)
        {
            var rect = (RectTransform)new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform)).transform;
            rect.SetParent(parent, false);
            rect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rect.anchoredPosition = position;
            rect.sizeDelta = size;
            return rect;
        }

        public static void Stretch(RectTransform rect)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            rect.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        }

        public static Image CreateImage(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, Color color, float radius = 0f)
        {
            var image = CreateRect(parent, name, position, size).gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
            image.color = color;
            image.raycastTarget = false;
            if (radius > 0f) Round(image, radius);
            return image;
        }

        public static Image CreateCircle(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, float diameter, Color color)
        {
            var image = CreateImage(parent, name, position, new Vector2(diameter, diameter), color);
            image.sprite = CircleSprite;
            return image;
        }

        public static void Round(Image image, float radius)
        {
            image.sprite = RoundedSprite;
            image.type = Image.Type.Sliced;
            image.pixelsPerUnitMultiplier = RoundedTextureRadius / radius;
        }

        public static TextMeshProUGUI CreateText(Transform parent, string name, string text, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, float fontSize,
            Color color, FontStyles style = FontStyles.Normal, float spacing = 0f)
        {
            var label = CreateRect(parent, name, position, size).gameObject.AddComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
            if (TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset != null) label.font = TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset;
            label.text = text;
            label.fontSize = fontSize;
            label.color = color;
            label.fontStyle = style;
            label.characterSpacing = spacing;
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
            label.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.NoWrap;
            label.richText = true;
            label.raycastTarget = false;
            return label;
        }

        public static Button MakeButton(Image image)
        {
            image.raycastTarget = true;
            var button = image.gameObject.AddComponent<Button>();
            button.targetGraphic = image;
            button.navigation = new Navigation { mode = Navigation.Mode.None };
            return button;
        }

        public static Button CreateButton(Transform parent, string name, Vector2 position, Vector2 size, Color color, float radius) =>
            MakeButton(CreateImage(parent, name, position, size, color, radius));

        private static Sprite RoundedSprite => rounded != null ? rounded : (rounded = CreateRoundedSprite());
        private static Sprite CircleSprite => circle != null ? circle : (circle = CreateCircleSprite());

        private static Sprite CreateRoundedSprite()
        {
            const int size = RoundedTextureSize;
            const float radius = RoundedTextureRadius;
            const float half = size * 0.5f;
            const float inner = half - radius;

            var texture = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false)
            {
                name = "RK Rounded Panel",
                wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp,
                filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear
            };

            var pixels = new Color32[size * size];
            for (var y = 0; y < size; y++)
                for (var x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    var dx = Mathf.Max(Mathf.Abs(x + 0.5f - half) - inner, 0f);
                    var dy = Mathf.Max(Mathf.Abs(y + 0.5f - half) - inner, 0f);
                    var alpha = Mathf.Clamp01(radius - Mathf.Sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) + 0.5f);
                    pixels[y * size + x] = new Color32(255, 255, 255, (byte)Mathf.RoundToInt(alpha * 255f));
                }
            texture.SetPixels32(pixels);
            texture.Apply(false, true);

            return Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0f, 0f, size, size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), RoundedPixelsPerUnit, 0,
                SpriteMeshType.FullRect, new Vector4(radius, radius, radius, radius));
        }

        private static Sprite CreateCircleSprite()
        {
            const int size = 64;
            var texture = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false)
            {
                name = "RK Circle",
                wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp,
                filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear
            };
            var center = Vector2.one * (size * 0.5f);
            var pixels = new Color32[size * size];
            for (var y = 0; y < size; y++)
                for (var x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    var distance = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(x + 0.5f, y + 0.5f), center);
                    var alpha = Mathf.Clamp01(size * 0.5f - distance + 0.5f);
                    pixels[y * size + x] = new Color32(255, 255, 255, (byte)Mathf.RoundToInt(alpha * 255));
                }
            texture.SetPixels32(pixels);
            texture.Apply(false, true);
            return Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, size, size), Vector2.one * 0.5f, size, 0, SpriteMeshType.FullRect);
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LegacyUnitIconUI.cs === 
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using TMPro;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public static class LegacyUnitIntroductionUI
    {
        public static TMP_Text Build(Transform panel, UnitDefinition stats, bool showAvailability = true)
        {
            var role = stats.Role;
            var rarityColor = LegacyUnitLooks.RarityColor(stats.Rarity);

            LegacyUiKit.CreateCircle(panel, "Emblem Ring", new Vector2(0, 75), 76, rarityColor);
            var face = LegacyUiKit.CreateCircle(panel, "Emblem", new Vector2(0, 75), 70, LegacyUiKit.PanelColor);
            LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(face.transform, Vector2.zero, 76 * 1.15f).SetType(role);

            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Name", $"{LegacyUnitLooks.Name(role)}  Lv.{stats.Level}", new Vector2(0, 18), new Vector2(200, 24), 17,
                LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold, 2);

            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Rarity", stats.Rarity.ToString().ToUpperInvariant(), new Vector2(0, -2), new Vector2(200, 18), 13,
                rarityColor, FontStyles.Bold, 2);

            var status = showAvailability
                ? LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Availability", "", new Vector2(0, -20), new Vector2(200, 20), 13, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor)
                : null;

            var description = role switch
            {
                UnitRole.Captain => "Leader / DEF 50%",
                UnitRole.Tank => "Heavy frontline shield",
                UnitRole.Heal => "Heals 8 neighbors",
                UnitRole.Sniper => "Long-range tower hunter",
                UnitRole.Vanguard => "Armored bruiser / ATK+DEF",
                UnitRole.Demolisher => "2.25x Siege DMG vs Towers",
                _ => "1.5x DMG vs Towers"
            };

            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Role", description, new Vector2(0, -38), new Vector2(200, 18), 12, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(panel, "Divider", new Vector2(0, -54), new Vector2(160, 2), rarityColor);

            var isAttacker = SquadCombatRules.CanAttackTowers(role);
            var key = isAttacker ? "ATK" : role == UnitRole.Tank ? "DEF" : "HEAL";
            var value = isAttacker ? $"{stats.Damage:0.##}"
                : role == UnitRole.Tank ? $"{stats.Defense * 100:0}%"
                : $"{stats.HealPerSecond:0.##}/s";

            BuildStat(panel, key, value, -76);
            BuildStat(panel, "HP", $"{stats.MaxHp:0.##}", -104);
            return status;
        }

        private static void BuildStat(Transform panel, string name, string value, float y)
        {
            var size = new Vector2(148, 24);
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Stat", name, new Vector2(0, y), size, 14, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor, FontStyles.Bold, 2);
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Left;
            var number = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Value", value, new Vector2(0, y), size, 18, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            number.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Right;
        }
    }

    public class LegacyUnitIconUI : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const float ShadowAlpha = 0.5f;
        private static readonly Vector2 shadowOffset = new Vector2(0.03f, -0.05f);

        private RectTransform rect;
        private float pixelsPerUnit;
        private UnitRole shown = UnitRole.Test;

        public RectTransform Rect => rect;

        public static LegacyUnitIconUI Create(Transform parent, Vector2 position, float pixelsPerUnit)
        {
            var rect = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(parent, "Unit Icon", position, Vector2.one * pixelsPerUnit * 0.5f);
            var icon = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<LegacyUnitIconUI>();
            icon.rect = rect;
            icon.pixelsPerUnit = pixelsPerUnit;
            return icon;
        }

        public void SetType(UnitRole type)
        {
            if (type == shown) return;
            shown = type;

            for (var i = transform.childCount - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                Destroy(transform.GetChild(i).gameObject);

            if (type == UnitRole.Test) return;

            var look = LegacyUnitLooks.For(type);
            var k = pixelsPerUnit;
            var outer = Vector2.one * look.OuterSize * k;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Shadow", shadowOffset * k, outer, new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, ShadowAlpha));
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Outline", Vector2.zero, outer, look.Outline);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Body", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * look.InnerSize * k, look.Body);

            switch (look.Mark)
            {
                case SoldierMark.Crown:
                    for (var i = 0; i < SoldierLook.CrownSegments.Length; i += 2)
                    {
                        var start = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i];
                        var end = SoldierLook.CrownSegments[i + 1];
                        var delta = end - start;
                        var line = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Crown " + i, (start + end) * (k / 2),
                            new Vector2(delta.magnitude, SoldierLook.CrownStroke) * k, look.MarkColor);
                        line.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
                    }
                    break;
                case SoldierMark.Diamond:
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * SoldierLook.DiamondSize * k, look.MarkColor)
                        .rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 45f);
                    break;
                case SoldierMark.Plate:
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark", Vector2.zero, Vector2.one * SoldierLook.PlateSize * k, look.MarkColor);
                    break;
                case SoldierMark.Cross:
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark Across", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(SoldierLook.CrossLength, SoldierLook.CrossWidth) * k, look.MarkColor);
                    LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(transform, "Mark Upright", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(SoldierLook.CrossWidth, SoldierLook.CrossLength) * k, look.MarkColor);
                    break;
            }
        }
    }
}.

using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public enum SoldierMark
    {
        None,
        Diamond,
        Plate,
        Cross,
        Crown
    }

    public readonly struct SoldierLook
    {
        public const float DiamondSize = 0.13f;
        public const float PlateSize = 0.16f;
        public const float CrossLength = 0.22f;
        public const float CrossWidth = 0.07f;
        public const float CrownStroke = 0.014f;

        public static readonly Vector2[] CrownSegments =
        {
            new Vector2(-0.10f, 0.065f), new Vector2(-0.065f, -0.055f),
            new Vector2(-0.065f, -0.055f), new Vector2(0.065f, -0.055f),
            new Vector2(0.065f, -0.055f), new Vector2(0.10f, 0.065f),
            new Vector2(0.10f, 0.065f), new Vector2(0.04f, 0.025f),
            new Vector2(0.04f, 0.025f), new Vector2(0, 0.09f),
            new Vector2(0, 0.09f), new Vector2(-0.04f, 0.025f),
            new Vector2(-0.04f, 0.025f), new Vector2(-0.10f, 0.065f),
            new Vector2(-0.07f, -0.09f), new Vector2(0.07f, -0.09f)
        };

        public SoldierLook(Color body, Color outline, float outerSize, float innerSize, SoldierMark mark, Color markColor)
        {
            Body = body;
            Outline = outline;
            OuterSize = outerSize;
            InnerSize = innerSize;
            Mark = mark;
            MarkColor = markColor;
        }

        public Color Body { get; }
        public Color Outline { get; }
        public float OuterSize { get; }
        public float InnerSize { get; }
        public SoldierMark Mark { get; }
        public Color MarkColor { get; }
    }

    public static class LegacyUnitLooks
    {
        private const float OuterSize = 0.4f;
        private const float InnerSize = 0.34f;
        private const float TankOuterSize = 0.44f;
        private const float TankInnerSize = 0.3f;
        private const float DiamondTint = 0.6f;

        private static readonly Color attackBody = new Color(0.95f, 0.35f, 0.3f);
        private static readonly Color attackOutline = new Color(0.35f, 0.08f, 0.08f);
        private static readonly Color tankBody = new Color(0.5f, 0.56f, 0.65f);
        private static readonly Color tankOutline = new Color(0.16f, 0.18f, 0.22f);
        private static readonly Color tankPlate = new Color(0.74f, 0.79f, 0.86f);
        private static readonly Color healBody = new Color(0.3f, 0.78f, 0.45f);
        private static readonly Color healOutline = new Color(0.07f, 0.26f, 0.12f);
        private static readonly Color healCross = new Color(0.96f, 1f, 0.97f);
        private static readonly Color sniperBody = new Color(0.22f, 0.78f, 0.88f);
        private static readonly Color sniperOutline = new Color(0.06f, 0.24f, 0.32f);
        private static readonly Color vanguardBody = new Color(0.38f, 0.48f, 0.82f);
        private static readonly Color vanguardOutline = new Color(0.12f, 0.16f, 0.34f);
        private static readonly Color demolisherBody = new Color(0.98f, 0.55f, 0.16f);
        private static readonly Color demolisherOutline = new Color(0.38f, 0.18f, 0.04f);

        public static SoldierLook For(UnitRole type) => type switch
        {
            UnitRole.Captain => new SoldierLook(new Color(0.72f, 0.56f, 0.28f), new Color(0.36f, 0.26f, 0.10f), OuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Crown, Color.white),
            UnitRole.Tank => new SoldierLook(tankBody, tankOutline, TankOuterSize, TankInnerSize, SoldierMark.Plate, tankPlate),
            UnitRole.Heal => new SoldierLook(healBody, healOutline, OuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Cross, healCross),
            UnitRole.Sniper => new SoldierLook(sniperBody, sniperOutline, OuterSize * 0.95f, InnerSize * 0.95f, SoldierMark.Diamond, Color.white),
            UnitRole.Vanguard => new SoldierLook(vanguardBody, vanguardOutline, TankOuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Plate, new Color(0.85f, 0.9f, 1f)),
            UnitRole.Demolisher => new SoldierLook(demolisherBody, demolisherOutline, TankOuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Diamond, new Color(1f, 0.92f, 0.4f)),
            _ => new SoldierLook(attackBody, attackOutline, OuterSize, InnerSize, SoldierMark.Diamond, Color.Lerp(attackBody, Color.white, DiamondTint))
        };

        public static string Name(UnitRole type) => type switch
        {
            UnitRole.Attack => "ATTACK",
            UnitRole.Tank => "TANK",
            UnitRole.Heal => "HEAL",
            UnitRole.Captain => "CAPTAIN",
            UnitRole.Sniper => "SNIPER",
            UnitRole.Vanguard => "VANGUARD",
            UnitRole.Demolisher => "DEMOLISHER",
            _ => string.Empty
        };

        public static Color RarityColor(UnitRarity rarity) => rarity switch
        {
            UnitRarity.Common => new Color(0.75f, 0.75f, 0.75f),    // Silver
            UnitRarity.Rare => new Color(0.20f, 0.55f, 0.90f),      // Blue
            UnitRarity.Epic => new Color(0.58f, 0.25f, 0.85f),      // Purple
            UnitRarity.Legendary => new Color(1.00f, 0.75f, 0.15f), // Gold/Orange
            _ => new Color(0.60f, 0.60f, 0.60f)
        };
    }
}.

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Analytics;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class LegacyUpgradeScene : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private SquadRoster roster;
        [SerializeField] private Button startGameButton;
        private GameObject screen;
        private TMP_Text countLabel, warehouseHint, levelLabel, goldLabel;
        private RectTransform levelHeart;
        private RectTransform screenRoot;
        private readonly List<GameObject> warehouseCells = new List<GameObject>();
        private MemberSpawnDefinition[] renderedMembers;
        private Button claimButton;
        private TMP_Text claimLabel, quotaLabel;
        private Image quotaFill;

        public Button PreviewNextLevelButton => previewButton;

        public SquadRoster Roster => roster;

        public TMP_Text LevelLabel => levelLabel;

        private void Start()

            goldLabel.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Right;
            goldLabel.rectTransform.anchorMin = goldLabel.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(1, 1);
            goldLabel.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(1, 1);
            goldLabel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-28, -20);
            RefreshGoldLabel();

            levelLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Level Label", "", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(360, 60),
                36, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);

            levelLabel.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Bottom;

            levelHeart = (RectTransform)levelLabel.transform.Find("Heart Icon");

            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(previewLabel.rectTransform);

            previewLabel.fontSizeMin = 14;

            var previewRect = (RectTransform)previewButton.transform;
