                24, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold, 3);
            for (var index = 0; index < SquadPreparation.WarehouseCapacity; index++)
                BuildWarehouseCell(root, index);
            renderedMembers = Preparation.Members.ToArray();

            var quota = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Card Quota", new Vector2(-165, -160), new Vector2(330, 10), LegacyUiKit.CellColor, 5);
            quotaFill = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(quota.transform, "Fill", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(330, 10), LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor, 5);
            quotaFill.rectTransform.anchorMin = quotaFill.rectTransform.anchorMax = quotaFill.rectTransform.pivot = new Vector2(0, 0.5f);

            quotaLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Card Quota Text", "", new Vector2(-165, -140), new Vector2(740, 26), 16, LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor);
            claimButton = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(root, "Claim Card Button", new Vector2(-165, -210), new Vector2(330, 58), LegacyUiKit.NodeGreenColor, 6);
            claimLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(claimButton.transform, "Label", "SUMMON UNIT", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(320, 54), 22, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            claimButton.onClick.AddListener(OpenClaimCards);

            warehouseHint = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Warehouse Hint", "Drag units to squad  |  Right-click unit to dismiss",
                new Vector2(-165, -115), new Vector2(780, 26), 16, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);

            Grid = LegacyFormationGrid.CreateForPreparation(root, root, Preparation);
            ((RectTransform)Grid.transform).anchoredPosition = new Vector2(465, 43);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(Grid.transform, "Title", "SQUAD", new Vector2(0, 151), new Vector2(310, 26),
                18, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold, 3);
            countLabel = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(Grid.transform, "Squad Count", "", new Vector2(0, 189), new Vector2(310, 30),
                26, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);

            Preparation.Changed += Refresh;
            startGameButton.onClick.AddListener(StartWave);
            Refresh();
            CaptainGuide.Attach(this, root);
            StartWaveNotice.Attach(this, root);
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            RefreshLevelLabel();
            RefreshGoldLabel();
            RefreshClaimCards();
            if (startGameButton != null && Preparation != null)
                startGameButton.interactable = CanStartWave && levelPreview == null;
            if (previewButton != null)
                previewButton.interactable = levelPreview == null && claimOverlay == null && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives && !startPending;
        }

        public bool CanStartWave =>
            Preparation != null && Preparation.CanStart && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives && claimOverlay == null && !startPending;

        public bool IsOverlayOpen => claimOverlay != null || levelPreview != null;

        public TMP_Text SquadCountLabel => countLabel;

        public static Vector2 WarehouseCellPosition(int index) =>
            new Vector2(-500 + index % SquadPreparation.WarehouseColumns * 134, 185 - index / SquadPreparation.WarehouseColumns * 180);

        public void OpenNextLevelPreview()
        {
            if (levelPreview != null || claimOverlay != null || BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives || startPending) return;
            levelPreview = LegacyNextLevelPreview.Open(this);
            if (levelPreview != null) GameAnalytics.Track("map_preview_open");
        }

        public void OnNextLevelPreviewClosed()
        {
            levelPreview = null;
            var start = startPending;
            startPending = false;
            Refresh();
            if (start && Preparation.CanStart && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives) BattleSceneFlow.StartPreparedBattle(Preparation);
        }

        public void OpenClaimCards()
        {
            if (claimOverlay != null || levelPreview != null || BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives) return;
            claimOverlay = LegacyClaimCardOverlay.Open(this);
            RefreshClaimCards();
        }

        public void OnClaimCardsClosed() { claimOverlay = null; Refresh(); RefreshClaimCards(); }

        private void RefreshClaimCards()
        {
            var shop = BattleSceneFlow.CardShop;
            if (claimButton == null || shop == null) return;
            claimButton.interactable = shop.CanClaim && claimOverlay == null && levelPreview == null && !BattleSceneFlow.IsOutOfLives;
            claimButton.image.color = claimButton.interactable ? LegacyUiKit.NodeGreenColor : LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor;
            claimLabel.text = Preparation.CanAddMember ? $"SUMMON UNIT ({shop.NextPrice} GOLD)" : "STORAGE FULL";
            quotaLabel.text = shop.GetBannerSummary();
            quotaFill.enabled = BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance > 0;
            quotaFill.rectTransform.sizeDelta = new Vector2(330 * Mathf.Clamp01((float)BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance / shop.NextPrice), 10);
        }

        private void RefreshWarehouseCards()
        {
            if (renderedMembers != null && Preparation.Members.SequenceEqual(renderedMembers)) return;
            foreach (var cell in warehouseCells) { cell.SetActive(false); Destroy(cell); }
            warehouseCells.Clear();
            cardGroups.Clear();
            cardStatuses.Clear();
            for (var index = 0; index < SquadPreparation.WarehouseCapacity; index++)
                BuildWarehouseCell(screenRoot, index);
            renderedMembers = Preparation.Members.ToArray();
        }

        private void RefreshGoldLabel()
        {
            if (goldLabel != null) goldLabel.text = BattleSceneFlow.HasUnlimitedGold ? "$ UNLIMITED" : $"$ {BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance:N0}";
        }

        private void RefreshLevelLabel()
        {
            if (levelLabel == null) return;
            var lives = $"{BattleSceneFlow.CurrentLevelLivesRemaining}/{BattleSession.DefaultLivesPerLevel}";
            var levelName = BattleSceneFlow.IsTestRun ? "TEST LEVEL" : $"LEVEL {Mathf.Max(1, BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber)}";
            levelLabel.text = $"{levelName}  <space=30>{lives}";
            var buttonRect = (RectTransform)startGameButton.transform;
            var top = buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.center.x, buttonRect.rect.yMax, 0));
            var point = levelLabel.rectTransform.parent.InverseTransformPoint(top);
            var labelBottom = point.y + 12;
            if (previewButton != null)
            {
                var parent = levelLabel.rectTransform.parent;
                var left = parent.InverseTransformPoint(buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.xMin, buttonRect.rect.center.y, 0)));
                var right = parent.InverseTransformPoint(buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.xMax, buttonRect.rect.center.y, 0)));
                var bottom = parent.InverseTransformPoint(buttonRect.TransformPoint(new Vector3(buttonRect.rect.center.x, buttonRect.rect.yMin, 0)));
                var rect = (RectTransform)previewButton.transform;
                rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(Vector3.Distance(left, right) * 0.85f, Vector3.Distance(point, bottom) * 0.75f);
                rect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(point.x, point.y + 12);
                labelBottom = rect.anchoredPosition.y + rect.sizeDelta.y + 12;
            }
            levelLabel.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(point.x, labelBottom);
            if (levelHeart != null)
            {
                levelLabel.ForceMeshUpdate();
                var info = levelLabel.textInfo;
                if (info.characterCount >= lives.Length)
                {
                    var first = info.characterInfo[info.characterCount - lives.Length];
                    levelHeart.localPosition = new Vector3(first.bottomLeft.x - 18,
                        (first.topRight.y + first.bottomLeft.y) / 2, 0);
                }
            }
        }

        private void BuildWarehouseCell(RectTransform root, int index)
        {
            var position = WarehouseCellPosition(index);
            var size = new Vector2(200, 262);
            var cell = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Warehouse Cell " + index, position, size + Vector2.one * 4, LegacyUiKit.PanelLineColor, 14).rectTransform;
            warehouseCells.Add(cell.gameObject);
            cell.localScale = Vector3.one * 0.6f;
            var panelImage = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(cell, "Introduction", Vector2.zero, size, LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 12);
            var panel = panelImage.rectTransform;
            if (index >= Preparation.Members.Count)
            {
                LegacyUiKit.CreateText(panel, "Empty", "EMPTY", Vector2.zero, size, 18, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor);
                return;
            }
            var member = Preparation.Members[index];
            cell.GetComponent<Image>().color = LegacyUnitLooks.RarityColor(member.Unit.Rarity);
            panelImage.raycastTarget = true;
            panel.gameObject.AddComponent<WarehouseUnitCardUI>().Initialize(this, root, member);
            cardGroups[member.MemberId] = cell.gameObject.AddComponent<CanvasGroup>();
            cardStatuses[member.MemberId] = LegacyUnitIntroductionUI.Build(panel, member.Unit);
        }

        public void StartWave()
        {
            if (Grid == null || !CanStartWave) return;
            if (levelPreview != null)
            {
                startPending = true;
                levelPreview.Close();
                return;
            }
            BattleSceneFlow.StartPreparedBattle(Preparation);
        }

        public bool TryPlaceMember(int memberId, PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (Grid.TryPlaceWarehouseMember(memberId, data)) return true;
            warehouseHint.text = Preparation.DeployedCount >= SquadPreparation.MaxDeployed
                ? "Squad full (6/6). Replace or return a unit."
                : "Drop the unit onto a squad slot.";
            return false;
        }

        public void TryDismissMember(int memberId)
        {
            var member = Preparation.FindMember(memberId);
            if (member == null) return;
            if (member.Unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain)
            {
                warehouseHint.text = "Cannot dismiss your Captain!";
                return;
            }
            if (!Preparation.TryDismissMember(memberId))
            {
                warehouseHint.text = "Cannot dismiss your last remaining unit!";
                return;
            }
            warehouseHint.text = $"Dismissed {LegacyUnitLooks.Name(member.Unit.Role)} Lv.{member.Unit.Level}.";
            GameAnalytics.Track("unit_dismiss", ("role", member.Unit.Role.ToString()), ("rarity", member.Unit.Rarity.ToString()),
                ("unit_level", member.Unit.Level));
        }

        private void Refresh()
        {
            RefreshWarehouseCards();
            countLabel.text = $"{Preparation.DeployedCount}/{SquadPreparation.MaxDeployed}";
            startGameButton.interactable = CanStartWave && levelPreview == null;
            warehouseHint.text = "Drag units into squad  |  Right-click unit to dismiss";
            foreach (var member in Preparation.Members)
            {
                var deployed = Preparation.IsDeployed(member.MemberId);
                cardGroups[member.MemberId].alpha = deployed ? 0.45f : 1f;
                cardStatuses[member.MemberId].text = deployed ? "IN SQUAD" : "AVAILABLE";
            }
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (levelPreview != null) Destroy(levelPreview.gameObject);
            if (claimOverlay != null) Destroy(claimOverlay.gameObject);
            if (startGameButton != null) startGameButton.onClick.RemoveListener(StartWave);
            if (Preparation != null) Preparation.Changed -= Refresh;
            if (screen != null) Destroy(screen);
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\LevelOneTutorial.cs === 
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Analytics;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    // Adapted from Team5 HowToPlay's six freeze-frame lessons. Observes simulation
    // state/events and uses the real formation editor; never writes HP, slots or damage.
    [DisallowMultipleComponent, DefaultExecutionOrder(300)]
    public sealed class LevelOneTutorial : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private BattleSession session;
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private LegacyBattleHud hud;
        private BattleUnitInspection inspection;
        private Coroutine routine;
        private GameObject ui, blocker;
        private RectTransform root, banner;
        private TMP_Text title, body;
        private Button nextButton;
        private Camera camera;
        private Vector3 originalPosition, targetPosition;
        private Quaternion originalRotation;
        private float originalOrthoSize, targetOrthoSize;
        private bool cameraOwned, hasCameraSnapshot, ownsPause, wasPaused, next, firstHit;
        private BattlePresentation presentation;
        private Renderer[] glowingRenderers;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock[] originalGlowProperties;
        private MaterialPropertyBlock glowProperties;
        private Image glowingButton;
        private Color originalButtonColor;
        private bool blueWorldGlow;
        private RectTransform squadArrow;
        private readonly Vector3[] buttonCorners = new Vector3[4];
        private const int SpeedStep = 6;
        private bool speedPrompt;
        private Canvas speedCanvas;
        public int CurrentStep { get; private set; }
        private int TotalSteps => simulation != null && simulation.Towers.Count > 1 ? 9 : 7;
        public bool IsActive => routine != null;
        private int checkpointStep, resumeStep;
        private bool checkpointResult, resumeResult;
        private string checkpointHeading, checkpointMessage;
        private void PrepareRewind() { resumeStep = checkpointStep; resumeResult = checkpointResult; }
        public void SuspendForDefeat() => StopTutorial();

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene)) return;
            if (session == null) session = GetComponent<BattleSession>();
            if (session == null) return;
            session.SimulationCreated += Bind;
            session.TutorialRewindRequested += PrepareRewind;
            Bind(session.Simulation);
        }
        private void Start() { if (!BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene) && session != null) Bind(session.Simulation); }
        private void Bind(BattleSimulation value)
        {
            if (simulation == value && routine != null) return;
            StopTutorial();
            simulation = value;
            if (value == null || !isActiveAndEnabled) return;
            routine = StartCoroutine(Run());
        }
        private void OnDamaged(DamageEvent value)
        {
            // The coroutine acquires pause on the next frame, outside the simulation tick.
            if (Captain() != null && value.UnitId == Captain().Id) firstHit = true;
        }
        private UnitState Captain()
        {
            if (simulation != null)
                foreach (var unit in simulation.Units)
                    if (unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain && unit.IsAlive) return unit;
            return null;
        }
        private bool Running => session != null && session.Simulation == simulation && simulation != null &&
            simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && Captain() != null;

        private IEnumerator Run()
        {
            var startStep = resumeStep;
            var replayResult = resumeResult;
            resumeStep = 0; resumeResult = false;
            yield return null;
            while (simulation != null && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && Captain() == null) yield return null;
            if (!Running) { routine = null; yield break; }
            foreach (var candidate in FindObjectsByType<LegacyBattleHud>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                if (candidate.Session == session) { hud = candidate; break; }
            foreach (var candidate in FindObjectsByType<BattlePresentation>(FindObjectsSortMode.None))
                if (candidate.Session == session) { presentation = candidate; inspection = candidate.GetComponent<BattleUnitInspection>(); break; }
            camera = Camera.main;
            if (hud == null || inspection == null || camera == null) { routine = null; yield break; }
            originalPosition = camera.transform.position; originalRotation = camera.transform.rotation;
            originalOrthoSize = camera.orthographicSize; hasCameraSnapshot = true;
            BuildUi();
            simulation.UnitDamaged += OnDamaged;
            firstHit = startStep >= 3;
            if (startStep == 0) yield return new WaitForSecondsRealtime(0.55f);
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            AcquirePause();
            if (startStep > 0) wasPaused = session.CheckpointPausedBeforeHint;
            if (startStep <= 1)
            {
            Focus(Captain().Position, 2);
            GlowCaptain();
            Show(1, "YOU ARE THE ATTACKER!", "\nThe gold crown is your Captain. It marches automatically.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            }

            var firstTower = simulation.Towers.Count > 0 ? simulation.Towers[0] : null;
            if (startStep <= 2)
            {
            RestoreCamera();
            if (firstTower != null)
            {
                GlowWorld(session.Towers[0].transform, blue: true);
            }
            Show(2, "ENEMY TOWERS", "\nBlue strips mark fixed firing lanes.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            }
            if (startStep <= 3)
            {
            Hide(); RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause();
            while (Running && !firstHit) yield return null;
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            AcquirePause();
            FocusCaptainAndTower(firstTower);
            GlowCaptain();
            Show(3, "LIVE STATS", $"\nThe Captain was hit. Click a unit during normal play to inspect its live stats.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            }
            if (startStep <= 5)
            {
                if (startStep == 5 && replayResult)
                {
                    FocusCaptainAndTower(firstTower); GlowCaptain();
                    Show(5, checkpointHeading, checkpointMessage, true, true);
                    yield return WaitForNext();
                }
                else yield return GuideFormation(4, firstTower, startStep == 5);
            }
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            if (startStep <= SpeedStep)
            {
                yield return GuideSpeed();
                if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            }
            Hide(); RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause();

            var secondTower = simulation.Towers.Count > 1 ? simulation.Towers[1] : null;
            if (secondTower != null)
            {
                while (Running && secondTower.IsAlive && !Approaching(Captain(), secondTower)) yield return null;
                if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
                if (secondTower.IsAlive)
                {
                    AcquirePause();
                    if (OutsideLane(Captain(), secondTower))
                    {
                        Focus((Captain().Position + secondTower.Position) / 2, Vector3.Distance(Captain().Position, secondTower.Position) / 2 + 2);
                        GlowWorld(session.Towers[1].transform, blue: true);
                        Show(7, "AVOID THE TOWER", "\nThis tower fires upward. Your Captain is already outside its firing line.\nDifferent tower directions require different formation positions.", true);
                        yield return WaitForNext();
                    }
                    else yield return GuideFormation(7, secondTower);
                    Hide(); RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause();
                }
            }
            var route = session.Route.CaptureTopCenters();
            var length = 0f;
            for (var i = 1; i < route.Length; i++) length += Vector3.Distance(route[i - 1], route[i]);
            while (Running && length - Captain().RouteProgress > 6) yield return null;
            if (!Running) { Finish(); yield break; }
            AcquirePause();
            Focus(route[route.Length - 1], 3);
            GlowWorld(session.Route.Cells[session.Route.Cells.Count - 1].transform);
            Show(TotalSteps, "REACH THE GOAL TO WIN!", "\nGet your Captain to the end of the road to win.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
            if (Running) TutorialAnalytics.Complete();
            Finish();
        }

        private IEnumerator GuideFormation(int step, TowerState tower, bool reopenEditor = false)
        {
            if (!Running) yield break;
            inspection.CloseImmediately(); RestoreCamera();
            ClearWorldGlow();
            Show(step, step == 4 ? "EDIT YOUR FORMATION" : "AVOID THE TOWER",
                step == 4 ? "\nClick the highlighted squad crown to edit formation."
                    : "Click the highlighted squad crown.\nMove Captain to the <b>left or right column</b>, outside the vertical firing strip. Choose <b>APPLY & RESUME</b>.", false);
            GlowButton(hud.SquadButton(0));
            if (reopenEditor) hud.SelectSquad();
            var moved = false;
            while (Running && !moved)
            {
                var originalSlot = Captain().SlotId;
                while (Running && (hud.FormationEditor == null || !hud.FormationEditor.gameObject.activeSelf)) yield return null;
                if (!Running) yield break;
                ClearButtonGlow();
                Show(step + 1, "EDIT YOUR FORMATION",
                    step == 4 ? "\nDrag your unit <b>down by one cell to avoid attack</b>."
                        : "Move Captain to the <b>left or right column</b>, outside the vertical firing strip. Choose <b>APPLY & RESUME</b>.", false);
                while (Running && hud.FormationEditor != null && hud.FormationEditor.gameObject.activeSelf) yield return null;
                if (!Running) yield break;
                // Let inspection release its view; the editor restores the tutorial's pause.
                yield return null;
                moved = Captain().SlotId != originalSlot && (tower == null || OutsideLane(Captain(), tower));
                if (!moved)
                {
                    Show(step, "EDIT YOUR FORMATION", "The Captain is still in the firing lane, or the draft was canceled.\nOpen the squad again, move to a safe row / column, then <b>APPLY & RESUME</b>.", false);
                    GlowButton(hud.SquadButton(0));
                }
            }
            if (!Running) yield break;
            FocusCaptainAndTower(tower);
            GlowCaptain();
            Show(step + 1, "POSITIONING AVOIDS DAMAGE", "\nYour Captain moved to a different slot.\n The new position is outside this tower's firing line.", true, true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
        }
        private IEnumerator GuideSpeed()
        {
            if (!Running || hud.SpeedSlider == null) yield break;
            inspection.CloseImmediately(); RestoreCamera(); ClearWorldGlow();
            if (session.PlaybackSpeed < 3)
            {
                Show(SpeedStep, "SPEED IT UP", "Drag the speed slider at the top right to <b>3x</b>.\nYou can change it any time.", false);
                speedCanvas = hud.SpeedSlider.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();
                GlowImage(hud.SpeedSlider.targetGraphic as Image);
                speedPrompt = true; hud.TutorialAllowsSpeed = true;
                while (Running && session.PlaybackSpeed < 3) yield return null;
                speedPrompt = false; hud.TutorialAllowsSpeed = false; ClearButtonGlow();
                if (!Running) yield break;
            }
            Show(SpeedStep, "NICE!", "\nThe battle now runs 3x faster.\nSlow it down any time with the same slider.", true);
            yield return WaitForNext();
        }
        public static bool OutsideLane(UnitState unit, TowerState tower)
        {
            if (unit == null || tower == null) return false;
            var offset = unit.Position - tower.Position;
            if (tower.Definition.Area == AttackArea.Square)
                return !SquadCombatRules.Contains(offset, tower.Definition.Area, tower.Definition.Range, tower.Definition.AttackWidth);
            var direction = SquadCombatRules.Direction(tower.Definition.Area);
            return Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(offset, new Vector3(-direction.z, 0, direction.x))) > tower.Definition.AttackWidth / 2;
        }
        private static bool Approaching(UnitState unit, TowerState tower)
        {
            var offset = unit.Position - tower.Position;
            var direction = SquadCombatRules.Direction(tower.Definition.Area);
            var forward = Vector3.Dot(offset, direction);
            var side = Mathf.Abs(Vector3.Dot(offset, new Vector3(-direction.z, 0, direction.x)));
            return forward >= 0 && forward <= tower.Definition.Range + 3 && side <= 3;
        }
        private IEnumerator WaitForNext()
        {
            while (Running && !next) yield return null;
            next = false;
        }
        public void Continue() => next = true;
        public void Skip()
        {
            if (routine != null) TutorialAnalytics.Skip(CurrentStep);
            StopTutorial();
        }
        [ContextMenu("Replay Level 1 Tutorial")]
        public void Replay()
        {
            if (!Application.isPlaying || session == null) return;
            StopTutorial();
            simulation = null;
            session.StartBattle();
        }
        private void AcquirePause()
        {
            if (ownsPause || simulation == null) return;
            wasPaused = simulation.IsPaused; simulation.SetPaused(true); ownsPause = true;
            hud.TutorialOwnsPause = true; inspection.IsInputSuppressed = true;
        }
        private void ReleasePause()
        {
            if (ownsPause && session != null && session.Simulation == simulation && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running)
                simulation.SetPaused(wasPaused);
            ownsPause = false;
            if (hud != null) hud.TutorialOwnsPause = false;
            if (inspection != null) inspection.IsInputSuppressed = false;
        }
        private void Finish()
        {
            Cleanup(); routine = null;
        }
        private void StopTutorial()
        {
            if (routine != null) StopCoroutine(routine);
            routine = null; Cleanup();
        }
        private void Cleanup()
        {
            if (simulation != null) simulation.UnitDamaged -= OnDamaged;
            if (inspection != null && ownsPause) inspection.CloseImmediately();
            RestoreCamera(); ReleasePause(); Hide();
            firstHit = false; next = false; CurrentStep = 0; hasCameraSnapshot = false;
            speedPrompt = false; if (hud != null) hud.TutorialAllowsSpeed = false;
        }
        private void FocusCaptainAndTower(TowerState tower)
        {
            var captain = Captain();
            if (captain == null) return;
            if (tower == null) { Focus(captain.Position, 2); return; }
            inspection.CloseImmediately();
            var center = (captain.Position + tower.Position) / 2;
            var bounds = new Bounds(captain.Position, Vector3.one);
            if (presentation != null && presentation.TryGetView(captain.Id, out var view) && view.TryGetVisibleBounds(out var visible)) bounds = visible;
            foreach (var sceneTower in session.Towers)
                if (sceneTower.RuntimeState == tower)
                    foreach (var renderer in sceneTower.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>()) bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
            var extent = Vector3.zero;
            for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x += 2)
            for (var y = -1; y <= 1; y += 2)
            for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z += 2)
            {
                var corner = Quaternion.Inverse(originalRotation) * (bounds.center + Vector3.Scale(bounds.extents, new Vector3(x, y, z)) - center);
                extent = Vector3.Max(extent, new Vector3(Mathf.Abs(corner.x), Mathf.Abs(corner.y), Mathf.Abs(corner.z)));
            }
            // Frame both complete models around their midpoint, above the tutorial banner.
            targetOrthoSize = Mathf.Max(2, extent.y / 0.6f, extent.x / (camera.aspect * 0.85f));
            var tan = Mathf.Tan(camera.fieldOfView * Mathf.Deg2Rad / 2);
            var distance = Mathf.Max(6, targetOrthoSize / tan + extent.z, camera.nearClipPlane + extent.z + 1);
            var offset = camera.orthographic ? targetOrthoSize * 0.3f : distance * tan * 0.3f;
            targetPosition = center - originalRotation * Vector3.forward * distance - originalRotation * Vector3.up * offset;
            cameraOwned = true;
        }
        private void Focus(Vector3 point, float radius)
        {
            inspection.CloseImmediately();
            cameraOwned = true;
            var tan = Mathf.Tan(camera.fieldOfView * Mathf.Deg2Rad / 2);
            var distance = Mathf.Max(6, radius * 1.2f / tan);
            targetOrthoSize = radius * 1.2f;
            var verticalOffset = camera.orthographic ? targetOrthoSize * 0.3f : distance * tan * 0.3f;
            targetPosition = point - originalRotation * Vector3.forward * distance - originalRotation * Vector3.up * verticalOffset;
        }
        private void RestoreCamera()
        {
            if (cameraOwned && hasCameraSnapshot && camera != null)
            { camera.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(originalPosition, originalRotation); camera.orthographicSize = originalOrthoSize; }
            cameraOwned = false;
        }
        private void GlowCaptain()
        {
            var captain = Captain();
            if (captain != null && presentation != null && presentation.TryGetView(captain.Id, out var view))
                GlowWorld(view.transform, true);
            else ClearWorldGlow();
        }
        private static Color GoldPulse => Color.Lerp(new Color(1, 0.64f, 0.08f), new Color(1, 0.94f, 0.48f),
            (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4) + 1) / 2);
        private Color WorldPulse => blueWorldGlow ? Color.Lerp(new Color(0.12f, 0.35f, 0.95f), new Color(0.45f, 0.82f, 1f),
            (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4) + 1) / 2) : GoldPulse;
        private void GlowWorld(Transform target, bool keepCrown = false, bool blue = false)
        {
            ClearWorldGlow();
            if (target == null) return;
            blueWorldGlow = blue;
            var renderers = new List<Renderer>();
            foreach (var renderer in target.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>())
                if (!keepCrown || renderer.name == "Body" || renderer.name == "Outline") renderers.Add(renderer);
            glowingRenderers = renderers.ToArray();
            originalGlowProperties = new MaterialPropertyBlock[glowingRenderers.Length];
            for (var i = 0; i < glowingRenderers.Length; i++)
            {
                originalGlowProperties[i] = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
                glowingRenderers[i].GetPropertyBlock(originalGlowProperties[i]);
            }
            PulseWorld(WorldPulse);
        }
        private void PulseWorld(Color gold)
        {
            if (glowingRenderers == null) return;
            if (glowProperties == null) glowProperties = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            foreach (var renderer in glowingRenderers)
            {
                if (renderer == null) continue;
                renderer.GetPropertyBlock(glowProperties);
                glowProperties.SetColor("_BaseColor", gold); glowProperties.SetColor("_Color", gold);
                renderer.SetPropertyBlock(glowProperties);
            }
        }
        private void ClearWorldGlow()
        {
            if (glowingRenderers != null)
                for (var i = 0; i < glowingRenderers.Length; i++)
                    if (glowingRenderers[i] != null) glowingRenderers[i].SetPropertyBlock(originalGlowProperties[i]);
            glowingRenderers = null; originalGlowProperties = null;
        }
        private void GlowButton(Button button) => GlowImage(button != null ? button.targetGraphic as Image : null);
        private void GlowImage(Image image)
        {
            ClearButtonGlow();
            glowingButton = image;
            if (glowingButton == null) return;
            originalButtonColor = glowingButton.color; glowingButton.color = GoldPulse;
        }
        private void ClearButtonGlow()
        {
            if (glowingButton != null) glowingButton.color = originalButtonColor;
            glowingButton = null;
            if (squadArrow != null) squadArrow.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
        private void Show(int step, string heading, string message, bool modal, bool formationResult = false)
        {
            checkpointStep = step; checkpointHeading = heading; checkpointMessage = message; checkpointResult = formationResult;
            session.CaptureTutorialCheckpoint(wasPaused);
            TutorialAnalytics.Step(step, TotalSteps, heading);
            CurrentStep = step; next = false; ui.SetActive(true); banner.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            title.text = $"{step}/{TotalSteps} - {heading}"; body.text = message;
            LayoutBanner(!modal);
            blocker.SetActive(modal); nextButton.gameObject.SetActive(modal);
            ClearButtonGlow();
        }
        private void Hide()
        {
            ClearWorldGlow(); ClearButtonGlow();
            if (ui != null) ui.SetActive(false);
        }
        private void LateUpdate()
        {
            if (cameraOwned && camera != null)
            {
                var alpha = 1 - Mathf.Exp(-12 * Time.unscaledDeltaTime);
                camera.transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(camera.transform.position, targetPosition, alpha);
                if (camera.orthographic) camera.orthographicSize = Mathf.Lerp(camera.orthographicSize, targetOrthoSize, alpha);
            }
            if (ui == null || !ui.activeSelf) return;
            var gold = GoldPulse;
            PulseWorld(WorldPulse);
            if (glowingButton != null) glowingButton.color = gold;
            var showArrow = CurrentStep == 4 && glowingButton != null;
            var showSpeedArrow = !showArrow && speedPrompt && hud != null && hud.SpeedSlider != null;
            squadArrow.gameObject.SetActive(showArrow || showSpeedArrow);
            if (showSpeedArrow) PlaceSpeedArrow();
            else if (showArrow)
            {
                squadArrow.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
                glowingButton.rectTransform.GetWorldCorners(buttonCorners);
                var sourceCanvas = glowingButton.GetComponentInParent<Canvas>();
                var eventCamera = sourceCanvas != null && sourceCanvas.renderMode != RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay ? sourceCanvas.worldCamera : null;
                var screenPoint = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(eventCamera, (buttonCorners[2] + buttonCorners[3]) / 2);
                if (!RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle(root, screenPoint, null, out var right)) return;
                var scale = Mathf.Lerp(0.8f, 1.15f, (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4) + 1) / 2);
                squadArrow.localScale = Vector3.one * scale;
                squadArrow.SetAsLastSibling();
                // Keep the left-pointing tip next to the icon while the body grows and shrinks.
                squadArrow.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(right.x + 12 + 42 * scale, right.y);
            }
        }
        private void PlaceSpeedArrow()
        {
            ((RectTransform)hud.SpeedSlider.transform).GetWorldCorners(buttonCorners);
            var eventCamera = speedCanvas != null && speedCanvas.renderMode != RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay ? speedCanvas.worldCamera : null;
            var screenPoint = RectTransformUtility.WorldToScreenPoint(eventCamera, (buttonCorners[0] + buttonCorners[1]) / 2);
            if (!RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle(root, screenPoint, null, out var left)) return;
            var scale = Mathf.Lerp(0.8f, 1.15f, (Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 4) + 1) / 2);
            squadArrow.localScale = Vector3.one * scale;
            squadArrow.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, 180);
            squadArrow.SetAsLastSibling();
            squadArrow.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(left.x - 12 - 42 * scale, left.y);
        }
        private void BuildUi()
        {
            if (ui != null) return;
            ui = new GameObject("Level 1 Tutorial", typeof(RectTransform)); ui.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCanvas(ui, 900, new Vector2(1280, 720), 0.5f); root = (RectTransform)ui.transform;
            var shade = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Modal Input Blocker", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, new Color(0, 0, 0, 0.08f));
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(shade.rectTransform); shade.raycastTarget = true; blocker = shade.gameObject;
            // Match the original HowToPlay.BuildFreezeFrameUI card and TMP typography.
            // Both projects use LiberationSans SDF through TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset.
            var panel = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Tutorial Banner", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(880, 175), new Color(0.08f, 0.11f, 0.17f, 0.96f));
            panel.raycastTarget = true; banner = panel.rectTransform;
            var outline = panel.gameObject.AddComponent<Outline>();
            outline.effectColor = new Color(0.3f, 0.85f, 0.4f, 1);
            outline.effectDistance = new Vector2(3, -3);
            title = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(banner, "Title", "", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, 30, new Color(0.35f, 0.95f, 0.45f), FontStyles.Bold);
            title.enableAutoSizing = true; title.fontSizeMin = 26; title.fontSizeMax = 30;
            body = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(banner, "Body", "", Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero, 23, Color.white);
            body.textWrappingMode = TextWrappingModes.Normal; body.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
            // Keep every existing message, including its line breaks, visible in the legacy card.
            body.enableAutoSizing = true; body.fontSizeMin = 18; body.fontSizeMax = 23;
            var skip = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(banner, "Skip Tutorial", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(110, 30), Color.white, 0);
            var skipRect = (RectTransform)skip.transform;
            skipRect.anchorMin = skipRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(0, 0); skipRect.pivot = new Vector2(0, 0.5f);
            skipRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(24, 22);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(skip.transform, "Label", "SKIP", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(100, 28), 18, new Color(0.196f, 0.196f, 0.196f));
            skip.onClick.AddListener(Skip);
            nextButton = LegacyUiKit.CreateButton(banner, "Continue", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(144, 30), new Color(0.2f, 0.6f, 0.2f), 0);
            var nextRect = (RectTransform)nextButton.transform;
            nextRect.anchorMin = nextRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1, 0); nextRect.pivot = new Vector2(1, 0.5f);
            nextRect.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(-24, 22);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(nextButton.transform, "Label", "CONTINUE", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(138, 28), 18, Color.white);
            nextButton.onClick.AddListener(Continue);
            LayoutBanner(false);
            squadArrow = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(root, "Team1 Tutorial Arrow", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(84, 62));
            // Native Image strokes use the same rendering path as the existing working HUD.
            ArrowStroke("Shaft Border", new Vector2(-34, 0), new Vector2(42, 0), 12, Color.black);
            ArrowStroke("Upper Border", new Vector2(-38, 0), new Vector2(-10, 22), 12, Color.black);
            ArrowStroke("Lower Border", new Vector2(-38, 0), new Vector2(-10, -22), 12, Color.black);
            ArrowStroke("Shaft", new Vector2(-34, 0), new Vector2(38, 0), 6, Color.white);
            ArrowStroke("Upper", new Vector2(-37, 0), new Vector2(-10, 22), 6, Color.white);
            ArrowStroke("Lower", new Vector2(-37, 0), new Vector2(-10, -22), 6, Color.white);
            squadArrow.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }
        private void LayoutBanner(bool interactive)
        {
            // The full-width legacy card would cover the embedded formation editor's Apply button.
            // Use the same visual style on a narrower right-hand card during interactive lessons.
            banner.anchorMin = banner.anchorMax = new Vector2(interactive ? 0.75f : 0.5f, 0);
            banner.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0);
            banner.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0, 24);
            banner.sizeDelta = interactive ? new Vector2(560, 230) : new Vector2(880, 175);
            LayoutBannerText(title.rectTransform, interactive ? 0.72f : 0.68f, 0.96f, 40);
            LayoutBannerText(body.rectTransform, interactive ? 0.20f : 0.24f, interactive ? 0.72f : 0.68f, 50);
        }
        private static void LayoutBannerText(RectTransform rect, float bottom, float top, float horizontalInset)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = new Vector2(0, bottom); rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(1, top);
            rect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f); rect.anchoredPosition = Vector2.zero;
            rect.sizeDelta = new Vector2(-horizontalInset, 0);
        }
        private void ArrowStroke(string name, Vector2 from, Vector2 to, float thickness, Color tint)
        {
            var delta = to - from;
            var image = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(squadArrow, name, (from + to) / 2, new Vector2(delta.magnitude, thickness), tint);
            image.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0, 0, Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
        }
        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (session != null) { session.SimulationCreated -= Bind; session.TutorialRewindRequested -= PrepareRewind; }
            StopTutorial(); simulation = null;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class NextMapData
    {
        private NextMapData(NextMapRoute route, IReadOnlyList<NextMapTower> towers, Bounds cells, bool hasCells, Vector3 spawn, Vector3 goal)
        {
            Route = route;
            Towers = towers;
            Cells = cells;
            HasCells = hasCells;
            Spawn = spawn;
            Goal = goal;
        }

        public NextMapRoute Route { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<NextMapTower> Towers { get; }
        public Bounds Cells { get; }
        public bool HasCells { get; }
        public Vector3 Spawn { get; }
        public Vector3 Goal { get; }
        public bool HasMap => HasCells && Route.Count >= 2;

        public static NextMapData Capture(Scene scene)
        {
            BattleSession session = null;
            SceneRoute sceneRoute = null;
            var cells = new List<BattleCell>();
            var sceneTowers = new List<BattleTower>();
            foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
            {
                if (session == null) session = root.GetComponentInChildren<BattleSession>(true);
                if (sceneRoute == null) sceneRoute = root.GetComponentInChildren<SceneRoute>(true);
                cells.AddRange(root.GetComponentsInChildren<BattleCell>(true));
                sceneTowers.AddRange(root.GetComponentsInChildren<BattleTower>(true));
            }
            if (session != null && session.Route != null) sceneRoute = session.Route;

            var hasCells = false;
            var cellBounds = new Bounds();
            Vector3 spawnSum = Vector3.zero, goalSum = Vector3.zero;
            int spawnCount = 0, goalCount = 0;
            foreach (var cell in cells)
            {
                if (cell == null || !cell.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) continue;
                if (RendererBounds(cell, out var bounds))
                {
                    if (hasCells) cellBounds.Encapsulate(bounds);
                    else { cellBounds = bounds; hasCells = true; }
                }
                if (cell.Kind == CellKind.Spawn) { spawnSum += cell.TopCenter; spawnCount++; }
                else if (cell.Kind == CellKind.Goal) { goalSum += cell.TopCenter; goalCount++; }
            }

            var route = new NextMapRoute(CaptureRoute(sceneRoute));
            var towers = new List<NextMapTower>();
            var source = session != null ? session.Towers : sceneTowers;
            foreach (var tower in source)
            {
                if (tower == null || !tower.gameObject.activeInHierarchy || !tower.HasValidConfiguration) continue;
                var hasModel = RendererBounds(tower, out var model);
                towers.Add(new NextMapTower(tower.CaptureDefinition(towers.Count + 1), model, hasModel));
            }

            var start = route.Count > 0 ? route[0] : Vector3.zero;
            var end = route.Count > 0 ? route[route.Count - 1] : Vector3.zero;
            return new NextMapData(route, towers, cellBounds, hasCells,
                spawnCount > 0 ? spawnSum / spawnCount : start,
                goalCount > 0 ? goalSum / goalCount : end);
        }

        private static Vector3[] CaptureRoute(SceneRoute route)
        {
            if (route == null) return Array.Empty<Vector3>();
            try { return route.CaptureTopCenters(); }
            catch (Exception) { return Array.Empty<Vector3>(); }
        }

        private static bool RendererBounds(Component owner, out Bounds bounds)
        {
            bounds = new Bounds();
            var found = false;
            foreach (var renderer in owner.GetComponentsInChildren<Renderer>(true))
            {
                if (!renderer.enabled || !renderer.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) continue;
                if (found) bounds.Encapsulate(renderer.bounds);
                else { bounds = renderer.bounds; found = true; }
            }
            return found;
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class NextMapOverlay : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private static readonly Color LaneColor = new Color(0.25f, 0.55f, 1f);
        private static readonly Color RouteColor = new Color(1f, 0.88f, 0.25f);
        private static readonly Color SpawnColor = new Color(0.9f, 0.25f, 0.25f);
        private static readonly Color GoalColor = new Color(0.2f, 0.75f, 0.9f);
        private static readonly Color GoalInk = new Color(0.02f, 0.13f, 0.17f);
        private static readonly int BaseColor = Shader.PropertyToID("_BaseColor");

        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Tiles between arrowheads on the route line.")] private float arrowSpacing = 3f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Route line thickness in tiles.")] private float routeWidth = 0.3f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Opacity of a lane's fill.")] private float laneFillAlpha = 0.2f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Opacity of a lane's edge line.")] private float laneEdgeAlpha = 1f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("World width of a lane's edge line.")] private float laneEdgeWidth = 0.06f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Opacity of a lane's diagonal hatching.")] private float hatchAlpha = 0.55f;
        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Height in pixels of the START and GOAL labels above the center of their cells.")] private float labelLift = 39f;

        private MaterialPropertyBlock block;
        private RectTransform lanes;
        private RectTransform road;
        private RectTransform labels;

        public void Build(NextMapData data, NextMapProjection projection, IReadOnlyList<TowerRangeView> ranges)
        {
            block = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            lanes = Layer("Lane Hatching");
            road = Layer("Route");
            labels = Layer("Labels");
            foreach (var range in ranges)
            {
                var edge = range.GetComponentInChildren<LineRenderer>(true);
                if (edge != null) edge.widthMultiplier = laneEdgeWidth;
                Tint(range.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>(), new Color(LaneColor.r, LaneColor.g, LaneColor.b, laneFillAlpha));
                Tint(edge, new Color(LaneColor.r, LaneColor.g, LaneColor.b, laneEdgeAlpha));
            }
            foreach (var tower in data.Towers) Hatch(tower, projection);
            BuildRoute(data.Route, projection);
            Pin("Start Pin", "START", projection.ToPanel(data.Spawn), SpawnColor, Color.white);
            Pin("Goal Pin", "GOAL", projection.ToPanel(data.Goal), GoalColor, GoalInk);
            var order = NextMapRules.RoadOrder(data.Route, data.Towers);
            for (var number = 0; number < order.Length; number++) Badge(data.Towers[order[number]], number + 1, projection);
        }

        public static void Legend(Transform parent, Vector2 start)
        {
            var x = start.x;
            var route = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(parent, "Legend Route", new Vector2(x + 11f, start.y), new Vector2(22f, 5f), RouteColor, 2);
            x = LegendLabel(parent, "ROUTE", route.rectTransform, x, start.y);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(parent, "Legend Lane Edge", new Vector2(x + 11f, start.y), new Vector2(22f, 14f), LaneColor, 3);
            var lane = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(parent, "Legend Lane", new Vector2(x + 11f, start.y), new Vector2(19f, 11f), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 2);
            var hatch = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(lane.transform, "Hatch", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(19f, 11f), new Color(LaneColor.r, LaneColor.g, LaneColor.b, 0.7f));
            hatch.sprite = NextMapShapes.Hatch;
            hatch.type = Image.Type.Tiled;
            x = LegendLabel(parent, "TOWER LANE", lane.rectTransform, x, start.y);
            var spawn = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(parent, "Legend Start", new Vector2(x + 11f, start.y), new Vector2(22f, 14f), SpawnColor, 3);
            x = LegendLabel(parent, "START", spawn.rectTransform, x, start.y);
            var goal = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(parent, "Legend Goal", new Vector2(x + 11f, start.y), new Vector2(22f, 14f), GoalColor, 3);
            LegendLabel(parent, "GOAL", goal.rectTransform, x, start.y);
        }

        private static float LegendLabel(Transform parent, string text, RectTransform swatch, float x, float y)
        {
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(parent, "Legend " + text, text, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(160f, 20f), 13, LegacyUiKit.MutedColor, FontStyles.Bold, 2);
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Left;
            label.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(x + 30f + 80f, y);
            return x + 30f + label.GetPreferredValues(text).x + 26f;
        }

        private RectTransform Layer(string name)
        {
            var layer = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(transform, name, Vector2.zero, Vector2.zero);
            LegacyUiKit.Stretch(layer);
            return layer;
        }

        private void Tint(Renderer target, Color color)
        {
            if (target == null) return;
            target.GetPropertyBlock(block);
            block.SetColor(BaseColor, color);
            target.SetPropertyBlock(block);
        }

        private void Hatch(NextMapTower tower, NextMapProjection projection)
        {
            var lane = tower.Lane;
            var a = projection.ToPanel(lane.min);
            var b = projection.ToPanel(lane.max);
            var size = new Vector2(Mathf.Abs(b.x - a.x), Mathf.Abs(b.y - a.y));
            var hatch = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(lanes, "Lane Hatch", (a + b) / 2f, size, new Color(LaneColor.r, LaneColor.g, LaneColor.b, hatchAlpha));
            hatch.sprite = NextMapShapes.Hatch;
            hatch.type = Image.Type.Tiled;
        }

        private void BuildRoute(NextMapRoute route, NextMapProjection projection)
        {
            var thickness = Mathf.Max(3f, routeWidth * projection.PixelsPerUnit);
            var corners = Corners(route);
            for (var i = 1; i < corners.Count; i++)
            {
                var from = projection.ToPanel(corners[i - 1]);
                var to = projection.ToPanel(corners[i]);
                var delta = to - from;
                var segment = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(road, "Route Segment", (from + to) / 2f, new Vector2(delta.magnitude, thickness), RouteColor);
                segment.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, Mathf.Atan2(delta.y, delta.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
            }
            foreach (var corner in corners) LegacyUiKit.CreateCircle(road, "Route Joint", projection.ToPanel(corner), thickness, RouteColor);
            var arrowSize = Mathf.Max(7f, 0.42f * projection.PixelsPerUnit) * 2.1f;
            for (var distance = 2f; distance < route.Length - 1f; distance += arrowSpacing)
            {
                var point = route.Sample(distance);
                var at = projection.ToPanel(point);
                var ahead = projection.ToPanel(point + route.Direction(distance)) - at;
                var arrow = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(road, "Route Arrow", at, new Vector2(arrowSize, arrowSize), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor);
                arrow.sprite = NextMapShapes.Triangle;
                arrow.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, Mathf.Atan2(ahead.y, ahead.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
            }
        }

        private static List<Vector3> Corners(NextMapRoute route)
        {
            var corners = new List<Vector3>();
            for (var i = 0; i < route.Count; i++)
            {
                if (i == 0 || i == route.Count - 1)
                {
                    corners.Add(route[i]);
                    continue;
                }
                var before = (route[i] - route[i - 1]).normalized;
                var after = (route[i + 1] - route[i]).normalized;
                if (Vector3.Dot(before, after) < 0.999f) corners.Add(route[i]);
            }
            return corners;
        }

        private void Pin(string name, string text, Vector2 point, Color fill, Color ink)
        {
            var root = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(labels, name, point + new Vector2(0f, labelLift), Vector2.zero);
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Label", text, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(120f, 26f), 15, ink, FontStyles.Bold, 4);
            var back = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Back", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(label.GetPreferredValues(text).x + 20f, 26f), fill, 6);
            back.transform.SetSiblingIndex(label.transform.GetSiblingIndex());
        }

        private void Badge(NextMapTower tower, int number, NextMapProjection projection)
        {
            var definition = tower.Definition;
            var square = definition.Area == AttackArea.Square;
            var text = (square ? "r " : string.Empty) + definition.Range.ToString("0.##", CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            var root = LegacyUiKit.CreateRect(labels, "Tower Badge " + number, projection.ToPanel(tower.BadgeAnchor) + new Vector2(0f, 16f), Vector2.zero);
            var value = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Range", text, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(80f, 24f), 14, LegacyUiKit.TextColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            var width = 54f + value.GetPreferredValues(text).x;
            var left = -width / 2f;
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Outline", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(width + 4f, 30f), LaneColor, 15);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Back", Vector2.zero, new Vector2(width, 26f), LegacyUiKit.PanelColor, 13);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateCircle(root, "Number Back", new Vector2(left + 13f, 0f), 20f, LaneColor);
            LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Number", number.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), new Vector2(left + 13f, 0f), new Vector2(20f, 20f), 13, Color.white, FontStyles.Bold);
            var arrow = LegacyUiKit.CreateImage(root, "Direction", new Vector2(left + 34f, 0f), square ? new Vector2(9f, 9f) : new Vector2(11f, 11f), LegacyUiKit.TextColor);
            if (!square)
            {
                arrow.sprite = NextMapShapes.Triangle;
                var screen = projection.ToPanel(definition.Position + SquadCombatRules.Direction(definition.Area)) - projection.ToPanel(definition.Position);
                arrow.rectTransform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, Mathf.Atan2(screen.y, screen.x) * Mathf.Rad2Deg);
            }
            value.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Left;
            value.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(left + 44f + 40f, 0f);
            value.transform.SetAsLastSibling();
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class NextMapProjection
    {
        private readonly Vector3 right;
        private readonly Vector3 up;
        private readonly Vector3 forward;
        private readonly float centerX;
        private readonly float centerY;
        private readonly float nearDepth;
        private readonly float depthRange;

        private NextMapProjection(Quaternion rotation, Vector2 panelSize, float pixelsPerUnit, float centerX, float centerY, float nearDepth, float depthRange)
        {
            Rotation = rotation;
            PanelSize = panelSize;
            PixelsPerUnit = pixelsPerUnit;
            right = rotation * Vector3.right;
            up = rotation * Vector3.up;
            forward = rotation * Vector3.forward;
            this.centerX = centerX;
            this.centerY = centerY;
            this.nearDepth = nearDepth;
            this.depthRange = depthRange;
        }

        public Quaternion Rotation { get; }
        public Vector2 PanelSize { get; }
        public float PixelsPerUnit { get; }
        public float GroundSquash => new Vector2(up.x, up.z).magnitude;

        public static NextMapProjection Fit(Quaternion rotation, IEnumerable<Vector3> points, Vector2 panelSize, float side, float top, float bottom)
        {
            var right = rotation * Vector3.right;
            var up = rotation * Vector3.up;
            var forward = rotation * Vector3.forward;
            float minX = float.MaxValue, maxX = float.MinValue, minY = float.MaxValue, maxY = float.MinValue;
            float minZ = float.MaxValue, maxZ = float.MinValue;
            foreach (var point in points)
            {
                var x = Vector3.Dot(point, right);
                var y = Vector3.Dot(point, up);
                var z = Vector3.Dot(point, forward);
                minX = Mathf.Min(minX, x); maxX = Mathf.Max(maxX, x);
                minY = Mathf.Min(minY, y); maxY = Mathf.Max(maxY, y);
                minZ = Mathf.Min(minZ, z); maxZ = Mathf.Max(maxZ, z);
            }
            if (minX > maxX) { minX = maxX = minY = maxY = minZ = maxZ = 0f; }
            var scale = Mathf.Min((panelSize.x - 2f * side) / Mathf.Max(maxX - minX, 0.5f),
                (panelSize.y - top - bottom) / Mathf.Max(maxY - minY, 0.5f));
            var centerX = (minX + maxX) / 2f;
            var centerY = (minY + maxY) / 2f + (top - bottom) / (2f * scale);
            return new NextMapProjection(rotation, panelSize, scale, centerX, centerY, minZ, maxZ - minZ);
        }

        public Vector2 ToPanel(Vector3 world) =>
            new Vector2((Vector3.Dot(world, right) - centerX) * PixelsPerUnit, (Vector3.Dot(world, up) - centerY) * PixelsPerUnit);

        public void Apply(Camera camera)
        {
            camera.orthographic = true;
            camera.orthographicSize = PanelSize.y / 2f / PixelsPerUnit;
            camera.aspect = PanelSize.x / PanelSize.y;
            camera.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(right * centerX + up * centerY + forward * (nearDepth - 10f), Rotation);
            camera.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
            camera.farClipPlane = depthRange + 20f;
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\NextMapRoute.cs === 
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class NextMapRoute
    {
        private readonly Vector3[] points;

        public NextMapRoute(IReadOnlyList<Vector3> waypoints)
        {
            points = new Vector3[waypoints != null ? waypoints.Count : 0];
            var length = 0f;
            for (var i = 0; i < points.Length; i++)
            {
                points[i] = waypoints[i];
                if (i > 0) length += Vector3.Distance(points[i - 1], points[i]);
            }
            Length = length;
        }

        public float Length { get; }
        public int Count => points.Length;
        public Vector3 this[int index] => points[index];

        public Vector3 Sample(float distance)
        {
            if (points.Length == 0) return Vector3.zero;
            for (var i = 1; i < points.Length; i++)
            {
                var segment = Vector3.Distance(points[i - 1], points[i]);
                if (segment <= 0f) continue;
                if (distance <= segment) return Vector3.Lerp(points[i - 1], points[i], distance / segment);
                distance -= segment;
            }
            return points[points.Length - 1];
        }

        public Vector3 Direction(float distance)
        {
            var last = Vector3.forward;
            for (var i = 1; i < points.Length; i++)
            {
                var segment = Vector3.Distance(points[i - 1], points[i]);
                if (segment <= 0f) continue;
                last = (points[i] - points[i - 1]) / segment;
                if (distance <= segment) return last;
                distance -= segment;
            }
            return last;
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Presentation\Minimal\LegacyUI\NextMapRules.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public static class NextMapRules
    {
        public const float SampleStep = 0.05f;

        public static bool Inside(TowerDefinition tower, Vector3 position) =>
            SquadCombatRules.Contains(position - tower.Position, tower.Area, tower.Range, tower.AttackWidth);

        public static float FirstContact(NextMapRoute route, TowerDefinition tower)
        {
            for (var distance = 0f; distance <= route.Length + 0.0001f; distance += SampleStep)
            {
                var anchor = route.Sample(distance);
                for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                    if (Inside(tower, anchor + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot))) return distance;
            }
            return float.PositiveInfinity;
        }

        public static int[] RoadOrder(NextMapRoute route, IReadOnlyList<NextMapTower> towers)
        {
            var contact = new float[towers.Count];
            var order = new int[towers.Count];
            for (var i = 0; i < towers.Count; i++)
            {
                contact[i] = FirstContact(route, towers[i].Definition);
                order[i] = i;
            }
            Array.Sort(order, (a, b) =>
            {
                var compare = contact[a].CompareTo(contact[b]);
                return compare != 0 ? compare : a.CompareTo(b);
            });
            return order;
        }
    }
}
.

using System;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public static class NextMapShapes
    {
        private const int TriangleSize = 64;
        private const int HatchSize = 24;
        private const float HatchPeriod = 12f;
        private const float HatchStripe = 3f;

        private static Sprite triangle;
        private static Sprite hatch;

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void ResetStatics()
        {
            triangle = null;
            hatch = null;
        }

        public static Sprite Triangle => triangle != null ? triangle : (triangle = CreateTriangle());
        public static Sprite Hatch => hatch != null ? hatch : (hatch = CreateHatch());

        private static Sprite CreateTriangle()
        {
            var tip = new Vector2(TriangleSize - 6f, TriangleSize / 2f);
            var bottom = new Vector2(10f, 6f);
            var top = new Vector2(10f, TriangleSize - 6f);
            return Create("Next Map Triangle", TriangleSize, TextureWrapMode.Clamp, TriangleSize, point =>
                Mathf.Min(Edge(point, tip, bottom), Mathf.Min(Edge(point, bottom, top), Edge(point, top, tip))));
        }

        private static Sprite CreateHatch()
        {
            return Create("Next Map Hatch", HatchSize, TextureWrapMode.Repeat, 100f, point =>
            {
                var along = Mathf.Repeat(point.x + point.y, HatchPeriod);
                return (HatchStripe / 2f - Mathf.Abs(along - HatchPeriod / 2f)) / Mathf.Sqrt(2f);
            });
        }

        private static float Edge(Vector2 point, Vector2 from, Vector2 to)
        {
            var edge = to - from;
            return Vector2.Dot(point - from, new Vector2(edge.y, -edge.x).normalized);
        }

        private static Sprite Create(string name, int size, TextureWrapMode wrap, float pixelsPerUnit, Func<Vector2, float> inside)
        {
            var texture = new Texture2D(size, size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false)
            {
                name = name,
                wrapMode = wrap,
                filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear
            };
            var pixels = new Color32[size * size];
            for (var y = 0; y < size; y++)
                for (var x = 0; x < size; x++)
                {
                    var alpha = Mathf.Clamp01(inside(new Vector2(x + 0.5f, y + 0.5f)) + 0.5f);
                    pixels[y * size + x] = new Color32(255, 255, 255, (byte)Mathf.RoundToInt(alpha * 255f));
                }
            texture.SetPixels32(pixels);
            texture.Apply(false, true);
            return Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0f, 0f, size, size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), pixelsPerUnit, 0, SpriteMeshType.FullRect);
        }
    }
}
.

using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class NextMapTower
    {
        public NextMapTower(TowerDefinition definition, Bounds model, bool hasModel)
        {
            Definition = definition;
            Model = model;
            HasModel = hasModel;
        }

        public TowerDefinition Definition { get; }
        public Bounds Model { get; }
        public bool HasModel { get; }

        public Bounds Lane => SquadCombatRules.AreaBounds(Definition.Position, Definition.Area, Definition.Range, Definition.AttackWidth);

        public Vector3 BadgeAnchor => HasModel
            ? new Vector3(Model.center.x, Model.max.y, Model.center.z)
            : Definition.Position + Vector3.up * 1.2f;
    }

using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class StartWaveNotice : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const string Message = "Drag a unit into your squad to start";
        private static readonly Color EmptyCountColor = new Color(1f, 0.35f, 0.35f);

        [SerializeField, Tooltip("Gap in pixels between the bottom of Start Wave and the center of the notice.")] private float gap = 18f;

        private LegacyUpgradeScene owner;
        private RectTransform root;
        private RectTransform button;
        private TMP_Text label;
        private TMP_Text count;
        private Color countColor;
        private bool shown;

        public static StartWaveNotice Attach(LegacyUpgradeScene owner, RectTransform root)
        {
            if (owner == null || root == null || owner.StartWaveButton == null) return null;
            var label = LegacyUiKit.CreateText(root, "Start Wave Notice", Message, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(480f, 26f), 16,
                LegacyUiKit.HighlightColor, FontStyles.Bold);
            var buttonText = owner.StartWaveButton.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>(true);
            if (buttonText != null) label.font = buttonText.font;
            label.enabled = false;
            var notice = label.gameObject.AddComponent<StartWaveNotice>();
            notice.owner = owner;
            notice.root = root;
            notice.label = label;
            notice.button = (RectTransform)owner.StartWaveButton.transform;
            notice.count = owner.SquadCountLabel;
            if (notice.count != null) notice.countColor = notice.count.color;
            return notice;
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            var empty = owner != null && owner.Preparation != null && owner.Preparation.DeployedCount == 0;
            if (empty != shown)
            {
                shown = empty;
                label.enabled = empty;
                if (count != null) count.color = empty ? EmptyCountColor : countColor;
            }
            if (shown) Place();
        }

        private void Place()
        {
            var rect = button.rect;
            var bottom = root.InverseTransformPoint(button.TransformPoint(new Vector3(rect.center.x, rect.yMin, 0f)));
            label.rectTransform.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(bottom.x, bottom.y - gap);
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            if (count != null) count.color = countColor;
        }
    }
}
