using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI
{
    public sealed class WarehouseUnitCardUI : MonoBehaviour, IBeginDragHandler, IDragHandler, IEndDragHandler, IPointerClickHandler
    {
        private LegacyUpgradeScene owner;
        private RectTransform dragLayer;
        private MemberSpawnDefinition member;
        private LegacyUnitIconUI ghost;

        public void Initialize(LegacyUpgradeScene owner, RectTransform dragLayer, MemberSpawnDefinition member)
        {
            this.owner = owner;
            this.dragLayer = dragLayer;
            this.member = member;
        }

        public void OnPointerClick(PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (data.button == PointerEventData.InputButton.Right && owner != null && member != null)
            {
                owner.TryDismissMember(member.MemberId);
            }
        }

        public void OnBeginDrag(PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (data.button != PointerEventData.InputButton.Left) return;
            if (ghost != null) Destroy(ghost.gameObject);
            ghost = LegacyUnitIconUI.Create(dragLayer, Vector2.zero, 90);
            ghost.SetType(member.Unit.Role);
            ghost.transform.SetAsLastSibling();
            OnDrag(data);
        }

        public void OnDrag(PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (ghost != null && RectTransformUtility.ScreenPointToLocalPointInRectangle(dragLayer, data.position, data.pressEventCamera, out var point))
                ghost.Rect.anchoredPosition = point;
        }

        public void OnEndDrag(PointerEventData data)
        {
            if (ghost == null) return;
            Destroy(ghost.gameObject);
            ghost = null;
            owner.TryPlaceMember(member.MemberId, data);
        }

        private void OnDisable()
        {
            if (ghost != null) Destroy(ghost.gameObject);
            ghost = null;
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Session\BattleSceneFlow.cs === 
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Session
{
    public sealed class BattleSceneFlow : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const string StartScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/StartGame.unity";
        public const string UpgradeScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/UpgradeScene.unity";
        public const string PrototypeBattleScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/SquadBattlePrototype.unity";
        public const string BattleScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/Level1GridBattle.unity";
        public const string LevelTwoScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/Level2GridBattle.unity";
        public const string LevelThreeScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/Level3GridBattle.unity";
        public const string LevelFourScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/Level4GridBattle.unity";
        public const string LevelFiveScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/Level5GridBattle.unity";
        public const string LevelSixScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/Level6GridBattle.unity";
        public const string LevelSevenScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/Level7GridBattle.unity";
        public const string TestStartScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/TestStartScene.unity";
        public const string TestUpdateScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/TestUpdateScene.unity";
        public const string TestLevelScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/TestLevel.unity";
        public const string SettlementScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/LevelSettlement.unity";
        public const string EndScene = "Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/EndGame.unity";

        private static readonly string[] battleScenes =
        {
            BattleScene,
            LevelTwoScene,
            LevelThreeScene,
            LevelFourScene,
            LevelFiveScene,
            LevelSixScene,
            LevelSevenScene
        };

        public static int TotalCampaignLevels => battleScenes.Length;

        public static string SceneForLevel(int level)
        {
            if (level < 1 || level > battleScenes.Length) throw new System.ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(level));
            return battleScenes[level - 1];
        }

        private static bool IsCampaignScene(string path) => System.Array.IndexOf(battleScenes, path) >= 0;

        private static string previewScenePath;
        public static string NextBattleScenePath => IsTestRun ? TestLevelScene : SceneForLevel(NextBattleLevelNumber);
        private string PreparationScenePath => testRun ? TestUpdateScene : UpgradeScene;
        private static bool IsInPreparationScene => current != null && SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path == current.PreparationScenePath;
        private static bool IsTestScenePath(string path) => path == TestStartScene || path == TestUpdateScene || path == TestLevelScene;
        public static bool IsTestRun => current != null && current.testRun;
        public static bool IsPreviewScene(Scene scene) => previewScenePath != null && scene.path == previewScenePath;

        public static bool TryBeginLevelPreview()
        {
            if (current == null || current.loading || current.livesExhausted || previewScenePath != null || !IsInPreparationScene)
                return false;
            previewScenePath = NextBattleScenePath;
            return true;
        }

        public static void EndLevelPreview(string path)
        {
            if (previewScenePath == path) previewScenePath = null;
        }

        private static BattleSceneFlow current;
        private bool testRun;
        private BattleSession session;
        private BattleSimulation simulation;
        private bool loading;
        private SquadPreparation preparation;
        private ClaimCardShop claimCards;
        private IReadOnlyList<MemberSpawnDefinition> deployment;
        private int nextBattleLevelNumber = 1;
        private int highestClearedLevel = 0;
        private int failedAttempts;
        private bool settled;
        private bool livesExhausted;
        private CampaignWallet wallet;
        private GameEconomySettings economySettings;

        public static ClaimCardShop CardShop => current != null ? current.claimCards : null;
        public static int HighestClearedLevel => current != null ? current.highestClearedLevel : 0;
        public static bool IsOutOfLives => current != null && current.livesExhausted;
        public static int LastRewardGold { get; private set; }
        public static int LastStageClearGold { get; private set; }
        public static int LastTowerBonusGold { get; private set; }
        public static int LastTowersDestroyedCount { get; private set; }
        public static int GoldBalance => current != null && current.wallet != null ? current.wallet.Balance : 0;
        public static bool HasUnlimitedGold => current != null && current.wallet != null && current.wallet.IsUnlimited;

        public static bool TrySpendGold(int price)
        {
            if (price <= 0) throw new System.ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(price));
            return current != null && !current.loading && !current.livesExhausted && IsInPreparationScene
                && current.wallet.TrySpend(price);
        }

        public static BattleOutcome LastOutcome { get; private set; }
        public static int CompletedLevelNumber { get; private set; }
        public static bool IsRunActive => current != null;
        public static int NextBattleLevelNumber => current != null ? current.nextBattleLevelNumber : 1;
        public static int CurrentLevelLivesRemaining => System.Math.Max(0, BattleSession.DefaultLivesPerLevel - (current != null ? current.failedAttempts : 0));
        // Raised after BeginRun queues the first scene, and once per settled non-test attempt with its final state.
        public static event System.Action RunStarted;
        public static event System.Action<BattleOutcome, int> LevelSettled;

        public static IReadOnlyList<UnitDefinition> OpenSummonOffer()
        {
            if (current == null || current.loading || current.livesExhausted || !IsInPreparationScene || current.claimCards == null)
                return System.Array.Empty<UnitDefinition>();
            return current.claimCards.BeginOffer();
        }

        public static bool TrySummonUnit(int offerIndex) =>
            current != null && !current.loading && !current.livesExhausted &&
            IsInPreparationScene &&
            current.claimCards != null && current.claimCards.TrySummon(offerIndex);

        public static void RestartCurrentLevel()
        {
            if (current == null || current.loading || current.livesExhausted || current.session == null || !current.session.RecoveryRequired || current.failedAttempts >= current.session.AttemptsPerLevel)
                return;
            current.QueueScene(current.session.gameObject.scene.path);
        }

        public static bool RewindLevelOne() =>
            current != null && !current.loading && current.session != null && current.session.RewindTutorialCheckpoint();

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.SubsystemRegistration)]
        private static void ResetStatics()
        {
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= EnsureLevelFlow;
            current = null;
            previewScenePath = null;
            LastOutcome = BattleOutcome.Running;
            CompletedLevelNumber = 0;
            LastRewardGold = 0;
            LastStageClearGold = 0;
            LastTowerBonusGold = 0;
            LastTowersDestroyedCount = 0;
        }

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.BeforeSceneLoad)]
        private static void InstallLevelFlow()
        {
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= EnsureLevelFlow;
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded += EnsureLevelFlow;
        }

        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.AfterSceneLoad)]
        private static void BindInitialLevel() => EnsureLevelFlow(SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), LoadSceneMode.Single);

        private static void EnsureLevelFlow(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
        {
            if (IsPreviewScene(scene)) return;
            if ((!IsCampaignScene(scene.path) && scene.path != TestLevelScene) || current != null) return;
            GetOrCreate().OnSceneLoaded(scene, mode);
        }

        public static void BeginRun() => BeginRun(false);
        public static void BeginTestRun() => BeginRun(true);

        private static void BeginRun(bool test)
        {
            var flow = GetOrCreate();
            if (flow.loading || previewScenePath != null) return;
            flow.testRun = test;
            flow.gameObject.name = test ? "Test Scene Flow" : "Battle Scene Flow";
            flow.preparation = null;
            flow.claimCards = null;
            flow.deployment = null;
            flow.nextBattleLevelNumber = 1;
            flow.highestClearedLevel = 0;
            flow.failedAttempts = 0;
            flow.livesExhausted = false;
            LastRewardGold = 0;
            LastStageClearGold = 0;
            LastTowerBonusGold = 0;
            LastTowersDestroyedCount = 0;
            flow.wallet = new CampaignWallet(flow.economySettings != null ? flow.economySettings.StartingGold : 35, unlimited: test);
            LastOutcome = BattleOutcome.Running;
            CompletedLevelNumber = 0;
            flow.QueueScene(test ? TestUpdateScene : BattleScene);
            RunStarted?.Invoke();
        }

        public static void ContinueToUpgrade()
        {
            var flow = GetOrCreate();
            if (!flow.livesExhausted) flow.QueueScene(flow.PreparationScenePath);
        }

        public static SquadPreparation PrepareSquad(SquadRoster roster)
        {
            var flow = GetOrCreate();
            if (flow.preparation == null)
            {
                flow.preparation = new SquadPreparation(roster);
                var baseCost = flow.economySettings != null ? flow.economySettings.BaseSummonCost : 30;
                var stepCost = flow.economySettings != null ? flow.economySettings.SummonCostIncreasePerPull : 15;
                flow.claimCards = new ClaimCardShop(flow.wallet, flow.preparation, baseCost, stepCost);
            }
            if (flow.preparation.Roster != roster)
                throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Preparation and battle must share the same roster.");

            flow.claimCards.SetStageProgress(flow.highestClearedLevel);
            return flow.preparation;
        }

        public static void StartPreparedBattle(SquadPreparation draft)
        {
            var flow = GetOrCreate();
            if (flow.loading || previewScenePath != null) return;
            if (draft == null || draft != flow.preparation) throw new System.InvalidOperationException("No current squad preparation.");
            if (flow.livesExhausted) return;
            flow.deployment = draft.CaptureDeployment();
            flow.QueueScene(flow.testRun ? TestLevelScene : SceneForLevel(flow.nextBattleLevelNumber));
        }

        public static void ReturnToStart()
        {
            var flow = GetOrCreate();
            flow.QueueScene(flow.testRun ? TestStartScene : StartScene);
        }

        private static BattleSceneFlow GetOrCreate()
        {
            if (current != null) return current;
            current = new GameObject("Battle Scene Flow").AddComponent<BattleSceneFlow>();
            current.testRun = IsTestScenePath(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path);
            current.economySettings = Resources.Load<GameEconomySettings>("RebuildEconomy");
            current.wallet = new CampaignWallet(current.economySettings != null ? current.economySettings.StartingGold : 35, unlimited: current.testRun);
            DontDestroyOnLoad(current.gameObject);
            return current;
        }

        private void OnEnable() => SceneManager.sceneLoaded += OnSceneLoaded;

        private void OnDisable()
        {
            SceneManager.sceneLoaded -= OnSceneLoaded;
            Unbind();
            if (current == this) current = null;
        }

        private void QueueScene(string path)
        {
            if (loading) return;
            loading = true;
            StartCoroutine(ChangeScene(path));
        }

        private IEnumerator ChangeScene(string path)
        {
            yield return null;
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(path, LoadSceneMode.Single);
            loading = false;
        }

        private void OnSceneLoaded(Scene scene, LoadSceneMode mode)
        {
            if (IsPreviewScene(scene)) return;
            loading = false;
            Unbind();
            if (testRun)
            {
                if (scene.path == TestUpdateScene) return;
                if (scene.path != TestLevelScene) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
            }
            else
            {
                if (scene.path == EndScene || scene.path == UpgradeScene || scene.path == SettlementScene) return;
                if (!IsCampaignScene(scene.path)) { Destroy(gameObject); return; }
            }

            foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
            {
                session = root.GetComponentInChildren<BattleSession>();
                if (session != null) break;
            }
            if (session == null) { Debug.LogError("Battle Scene Flow cannot find BattleSession.", this); return; }

            nextBattleLevelNumber = session.DisplayedLevelNumber;
            settled = false;
            session.RestoreFailedAttempts(failedAttempts);

            if (deployment != null && preparation != null && !session.UsesSceneRosterOnly)
            {
                if (session.InitialRoster != preparation.Roster)
                    throw new System.InvalidOperationException("Battle roster differs from preparation.");
                session.SetPreparedMembers(deployment);
            }
            session.SimulationCreated += Bind;
            Bind(session.Simulation);
        }

        private void Bind(BattleSimulation next)
        {
            if (simulation != null) simulation.BattleEnded -= OnBattleEnded;
            simulation = next;
            if (simulation == null) return;
            simulation.BattleEnded += OnBattleEnded;
            settled = false;
            if (simulation.Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running) Settle(simulation.Outcome);
        }

        private void OnBattleEnded(BattleEndedEvent result) => Settle(result.Outcome);

        private void Settle(BattleOutcome outcome)
        {
            if (settled || loading || outcome == BattleOutcome.Running) return;
            settled = true;
            ApplySettlement(outcome);
            if (!testRun) LevelSettled?.Invoke(outcome, CompletedLevelNumber);
        }

        private void ApplySettlement(BattleOutcome outcome)
        {
            LastOutcome = outcome;
            LastRewardGold = 0;
            LastStageClearGold = 0;
            LastTowerBonusGold = 0;
            LastTowersDestroyedCount = simulation != null ? simulation.TowersDestroyedCount : 0;

            if (testRun)
            {
                CompletedLevelNumber = 0;
                failedAttempts = 0;
                session.RestoreFailedAttempts(0);
                return;
            }
            CompletedLevelNumber = session.DisplayedLevelNumber;

            if (outcome == BattleOutcome.Victory)
            {
                var clearGold = economySettings != null
                    ? economySettings.GetStageClearGold(CompletedLevelNumber)
                    : CompletedLevelNumber * 45;

                if (wallet.TryRewardLevel(CompletedLevelNumber, clearGold))
                    LastStageClearGold = clearGold;

                var towerGold = simulation != null ? simulation.TowerBountyGoldEarned : 0;
                if (towerGold > 0)
                {
                    wallet.AddBonusGold(towerGold);
                    LastTowerBonusGold = towerGold;
                }

                LastRewardGold = LastStageClearGold + LastTowerBonusGold;

                highestClearedLevel = Mathf.Max(highestClearedLevel, CompletedLevelNumber);
                if (claimCards != null) claimCards.SetStageProgress(highestClearedLevel);

                failedAttempts = 0;
                var nextLevel = CompletedLevelNumber + 1;
                if (CompletedLevelNumber >= battleScenes.Length || !Application.CanStreamedLevelBeLoaded(SceneForLevel(nextLevel)))
                {
                    QueueScene(EndScene);
                    return;
                }
                nextBattleLevelNumber = nextLevel;
            }
            else
            {
                failedAttempts++;
                if (failedAttempts >= session.AttemptsPerLevel)
                {
                    livesExhausted = true;
                    session.RecoveryRequired = true;
                    return;
                }
                if (CompletedLevelNumber == 1)
                {
                    session.RecoveryRequired = true;
                    return;
                }
            }
            QueueScene(SettlementScene);
        }

        private void Unbind()
        {
            if (session != null) session.SimulationCreated -= Bind;
            if (simulation != null) simulation.BattleEnded -= OnBattleEnded;
            session = null;
            simulation = null;
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Session\BattleSession.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Session
{
    [Serializable]
    public sealed class SquadMemberConfiguration
    {
        public bool enabled = true;
        [Min(1)] public int memberId = 1;
        [Range(0, 8)] public int slotId = 4;
        public UnitRole role = UnitRole.Attack;
        [Min(0)] public float spawnTime;
    }

    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattleSession : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private BattleGrid grid;
        [SerializeField] private SceneRoute route;
        [SerializeField] private List<BattleTower> towers = new List<BattleTower>();
        [SerializeField, Min(1)] private int unitCount = 12;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float unitHp = 65;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float unitSpeed = 1.4f;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float spawnInterval = 0.65f;
        [SerializeField, Min(1)] private int goalLives = 3;
        [SerializeField] private int formationSize = 1;
        [SerializeField] private bool autoStart = true;
        [SerializeField] private bool logEvents = true;
        [SerializeField] private bool squadCombat;
        [SerializeField] private List<SquadMemberConfiguration> members = new List<SquadMemberConfiguration>();
        [SerializeField] private SquadRoster initialRoster;
        [SerializeField] private bool useSceneRosterOnly;
        public bool UsesSceneRosterOnly => useSceneRosterOnly;
        private IReadOnlyList<MemberSpawnDefinition> preparedMembers;
        private IEnumerable<SquadMemberConfiguration> ConfiguredMembers => initialRoster != null ? initialRoster.Members : members;
        public SquadRoster InitialRoster => initialRoster;

        public void SetPreparedMembers(IReadOnlyList<MemberSpawnDefinition> deployment)
        {
            if (useSceneRosterOnly) throw new InvalidOperationException("This battle uses its fixed scene roster.");
            if (Simulation != null || advancing) throw new InvalidOperationException("Set deployment before starting the battle.");
            if (deployment == null || deployment.Count < 1 || deployment.Count > SquadPreparation.MaxDeployed ||
                deployment.Any(member => member == null || member.Unit.Role == UnitRole.Test) ||
                deployment.Select(member => member.MemberId).Distinct().Count() != deployment.Count ||
                deployment.Select(member => member.SlotId).Distinct().Count() != deployment.Count)
                throw new ArgumentException("Deployment requires 1..6 unique members and unique slots.");
            preparedMembers = Array.AsReadOnly(deployment.ToArray());
        }

        private IReadOnlyList<MemberSpawnDefinition> CaptureSquadMembers()
        {
            if (preparedMembers != null) return preparedMembers;
            // Direct Editor play uses the first six enabled configured members for debugging.
            var result = new List<MemberSpawnDefinition>();
            foreach (var member in ConfiguredMembers)
            {
                if (member == null) throw new ArgumentException("Missing member configuration.");
                if (!member.enabled) continue;
                result.Add(new MemberSpawnDefinition(member.memberId, member.slotId, SquadCombatRules.Create(member.role), member.spawnTime));
                if (result.Count == SquadPreparation.MaxDeployed) break;
            }
            return result.AsReadOnly();
        }
        [SerializeField, Min(1)] private int displayedLevelNumber = 1;
        public const int DefaultLivesPerLevel = 3;
        [SerializeField, Min(1)] private int attemptsPerLevel = DefaultLivesPerLevel;
        private int failedAttempts;
        internal void RestoreFailedAttempts(int count) => failedAttempts = Math.Max(0, count);
        private readonly List<BattleTower> runtimeTowers = new List<BattleTower>();
        private bool advancing;
        public BattleGrid Grid => grid;
        public SceneRoute Route => route;
        // Unity leaves a null list entry when an authored tower is deleted from the Scene.
        // All consumers use the same surviving order so simulation IDs and views stay aligned.
        public IReadOnlyList<BattleTower> Towers => towers.Where(tower => tower != null).ToArray();
        public BattleSimulation Simulation { get; private set; }
        public bool SquadCombat => squadCombat;
        public int DisplayedLevelNumber => displayedLevelNumber;
        public int AttemptsPerLevel => attemptsPerLevel;
        public int AttemptsRemaining => Math.Max(0, attemptsPerLevel - failedAttempts);
        public int PlaybackSpeed { get; private set; } = 1;
        public const float BaseTimeMultiplier = 0.8f;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float levelTimeMultiplier = 1;
        public float LevelTimeMultiplier => levelTimeMultiplier;
        public const float InspectionTimeMultiplier = 0.1f;
        public bool IsUnitInspectionActive { get; private set; }
        public float EffectivePlaybackSpeed => BaseTimeMultiplier * levelTimeMultiplier * (IsUnitInspectionActive ? InspectionTimeMultiplier : PlaybackSpeed);
        public void SetUnitInspectionActive(bool active) => IsUnitInspectionActive = active;
        // Presentation can subscribe before the first tick, and resubscribe after a restart.
        public event Action<BattleSimulation> SimulationCreated;
        public event Action TutorialRewindRequested;
        private BattleSimulation.Checkpoint tutorialCheckpoint;
        private int checkpointSpeed;
        private bool checkpointPaused;
        public bool RecoveryRequired { get; internal set; }
        public bool HasTutorialCheckpoint => tutorialCheckpoint != null;
        public bool CheckpointPausedBeforeHint => checkpointPaused;

        public void CaptureTutorialCheckpoint(bool pausedBeforeHint)
        {
            if (advancing || displayedLevelNumber != 1 || Simulation == null) return;
            tutorialCheckpoint = Simulation.CaptureCheckpoint();
            checkpointSpeed = PlaybackSpeed;
            checkpointPaused = pausedBeforeHint;
        }

        internal bool RewindTutorialCheckpoint()
        {
            if (advancing || !RecoveryRequired || AttemptsRemaining == 0 || tutorialCheckpoint == null) return false;
            var replayingHint = TutorialRewindRequested != null;
            TutorialRewindRequested?.Invoke();
            var restored = tutorialCheckpoint.Restore();
            restored.SetPaused(replayingHint || checkpointPaused);
            PlaybackSpeed = checkpointSpeed;
            RecoveryRequired = false;
            IsUnitInspectionActive = false;
            BindSimulation(restored);
            return true;
        }

        private void Start()
        {
            if (BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene)) return;
            if (autoStart) StartBattle();
        }

        private void FixedUpdate()
        {
            if (Simulation != null && Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running) AdvancePlaybackFrame(Time.fixedDeltaTime);
        }

        public void SetPlaybackSpeed(int multiplier)
        {
            if (multiplier < 1 || multiplier > 3) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(multiplier));
            PlaybackSpeed = multiplier;
        }

        // Inspection overrides the slider: one slower tick per frame, without global Time.timeScale.
        public void AdvancePlaybackFrame(float fixedDeltaTime)
        {
            if (!float.IsFinite(fixedDeltaTime) || fixedDeltaTime <= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(fixedDeltaTime));
            var ticks = IsUnitInspectionActive ? 1 : PlaybackSpeed;
            var step = fixedDeltaTime * BaseTimeMultiplier * levelTimeMultiplier * (IsUnitInspectionActive ? InspectionTimeMultiplier : 1f);
            for (var i = 0; i < ticks && Simulation != null && Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running && !Simulation.IsPaused; i++)
                Advance(step);
        }

        public List<string> ValidateConfiguration()
        {
            var errors = new List<string>();
            if (!float.IsFinite(levelTimeMultiplier) || levelTimeMultiplier <= 0) errors.Add("Level time multiplier must be finite and positive.");
            if (grid == null || route == null) { errors.Add("BattleSession requires a BattleGrid and SceneRoute."); return errors; }
            if (grid.gameObject.scene != gameObject.scene || route.gameObject.scene != gameObject.scene)
                errors.Add("Grid and route must belong to the session's Scene.");
            grid.RebuildIndex(errors);
            route.CollectErrors(grid, errors);
            if ((formationSize != 1 && formationSize != 3) || route.RequiredWidth != formationSize)
                errors.Add("Formation size must be 1 or 3 and match the route's required width.");
            if (formationSize == 3 && unitCount > 9)
                errors.Add("This prototype supports one squad of up to nine independent members.");
            var seen = new HashSet<BattleTower>();
            var towerFootprints = new HashSet<Vector3Int>();
            foreach (var tower in towers)
            {
                if (tower == null) continue;
                if (!seen.Add(tower)) errors.Add($"Duplicate tower reference: {tower.name}.");
                if (tower.gameObject.scene != gameObject.scene || !tower.isActiveAndEnabled)
                    errors.Add($"{tower.name}: tower must be active in this Scene.");
                if (!tower.HasValidConfiguration) errors.Add($"{tower.name}: invalid tower stats or position.");
                tower.CollectFootprintErrors(grid, towerFootprints, errors);
                // Support is determined from this same world position; there are no tower slots.
                var position = tower.transform.position;
                var supportKey = new Vector3Int(Mathf.RoundToInt(position.x),
                    Mathf.RoundToInt((position.y - BattleCell.LayerHeight / 2) / BattleCell.LayerHeight), Mathf.RoundToInt(position.z));
                if (!grid.TryGetCell(supportKey, out var support) || support.Kind != CellKind.TowerPlatform ||
                    !grid.HasClearTop(support) || Mathf.Abs(position.y - support.TopCenter.y) > 0.001f ||
                    Mathf.Abs(position.x - support.TopCenter.x) > 0.5f || Mathf.Abs(position.z - support.TopCenter.z) > 0.5f)
                    errors.Add($"{tower.name}: tower Transform must stand on a clear TowerPlatform top surface.");
            }
            if (unitCount <= 0 || goalLives <= 0 || !float.IsFinite(unitHp) || unitHp <= 0 ||
                !float.IsFinite(unitSpeed) || unitSpeed <= 0 || !float.IsFinite(spawnInterval) || spawnInterval <= 0)
                errors.Add("Unit count, HP, speed, spawn interval and goal lives must be positive and finite.");
            if (squadCombat)
            {
                if (formationSize != 3) errors.Add("Squad combat requires the 3x3 formation.");
                var ids = new HashSet<int>();
                var slots = new HashSet<int>();
                try
                {
                    var captured = CaptureSquadMembers();
                    foreach (var member in captured)
                        if (!ids.Add(member.MemberId) || !slots.Add(member.SlotId) || member.Unit.Role == UnitRole.Test)
                            errors.Add("Members require unique IDs, unique slots and a combat role.");
                    if (captured.Count < 1 || captured.Count > SquadPreparation.MaxDeployed)
                        errors.Add("Squad combat requires 1..6 members.");
                }
                catch (ArgumentException error) { errors.Add(error.Message); }
            }
            return errors;
        }

        public BattleDefinition CaptureDefinition()
        {
            var errors = ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count > 0) throw new InvalidOperationException(string.Join("\n", errors));
            var towerDefinitions = new List<TowerDefinition>();
            var sceneTowers = Towers;
            for (var i = 0; i < sceneTowers.Count; i++) towerDefinitions.Add(sceneTowers[i].CaptureDefinition(i + 1));
            if (squadCombat)
            {
                var spawns = CaptureSquadMembers();
                var passable = new List<Vector3Int>();
                foreach (var cell in grid.SceneCells)
                    if (cell.IsRouteSurface && grid.HasClearTop(cell)) passable.Add(cell.Occupancy);
                return new BattleDefinition(route.CaptureTopCenters(), towerDefinitions, spawns, passable);
            }
            return new BattleDefinition(route.CaptureTopCenters(), towerDefinitions,
                new UnitDefinition(unitHp, unitSpeed), unitCount, spawnInterval, goalLives, formationSize);
        }

        [ContextMenu("Start / Restart Battle")]
        public void StartBattle()
        {
            if (BattleSceneFlow.IsPreviewScene(gameObject.scene)) return;
            if (!Application.isPlaying) { Debug.LogWarning("Start Battle requires Play Mode.", this); return; }
            if (advancing) throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot restart a battle from a tick event.");
            IsUnitInspectionActive = false;
            var errors = ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count > 0)
            {
                ClearRuntimeBindings();
                Simulation = null;
                Debug.LogError("Battle configuration invalid:\n" + string.Join("\n", errors), this);
                return;
            }
            var next = new BattleSimulation(CaptureDefinition());
            tutorialCheckpoint = null;
            RecoveryRequired = false;
            BindSimulation(next);
        }

        private void BindSimulation(BattleSimulation next)
        {
            ClearRuntimeBindings();
            Simulation = next;
            next.BattleEnded += result =>
            {
                if (ReferenceEquals(Simulation, next) && result.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Defeat) failedAttempts++;
            };
            runtimeTowers.AddRange(Towers);
            for (var i = 0; i < runtimeTowers.Count; i++) runtimeTowers[i].BindRuntimeState(Simulation.Towers[i]);
            if (logEvents)
            {
                Simulation.UnitSpawned += value => Debug.Log($"UnitSpawned #{value.UnitId} at {value.Position}", this);
                Simulation.UnitDamaged += value => Debug.Log($"UnitDamaged #{value.UnitId}: -{value.Amount}, HP {value.RemainingHp}", this);
                Simulation.UnitDied += value => Debug.Log($"UnitDied #{value.UnitId}", this);
                Simulation.UnitReachedGoal += value => Debug.Log($"UnitReachedGoal #{value.UnitId}", this);
                Simulation.BattleEnded += value => Debug.Log($"BattleEnded {value.Outcome}: killed {value.Killed}, reached {value.ReachedGoal}", this);
                Simulation.TowerDamaged += value => Debug.Log($"TowerDamaged #{value.TowerId}: -{value.Amount}, HP {value.RemainingHp}", this);
                Simulation.UnitHealed += value => Debug.Log($"UnitHealed #{value.MemberId}: +{value.Amount}, HP {value.RemainingHp}", this);
            }
            SimulationCreated?.Invoke(Simulation);
        }

        public void Advance(float deltaTime)
        {
            if (Simulation == null) throw new InvalidOperationException("Start the battle first.");
            if (advancing) throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot advance recursively from a tick event.");
            advancing = true;
            try
            {
                // No logical tower clone: runtime positions always come from the original Scene entities.
                for (var i = 0; i < runtimeTowers.Count; i++)
                    if (runtimeTowers[i] != null) Simulation.SetTowerPosition(i + 1, runtimeTowers[i].transform.position);
                Simulation.Step(deltaTime);
            }
            finally { advancing = false; }
        }

        [ContextMenu("Validate Scene Configuration")]
        private void LogValidation()
        {
            var errors = ValidateConfiguration();
            if (errors.Count == 0) Debug.Log("Battle scene configuration is valid.", this);
            else Debug.LogError(string.Join("\n", errors), this);
        }

        private void ClearRuntimeBindings()
        {
            foreach (var tower in runtimeTowers) if (tower != null) tower.BindRuntimeState(null);
            runtimeTowers.Clear();
        }
        private void OnDestroy() => ClearRuntimeBindings();
    }
}
.

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Session
{
    public sealed class CampaignWallet
    {
        private readonly HashSet<int> rewardedLevels = new HashSet<int>();
        public int Balance { get; private set; }
        public bool IsUnlimited { get; }

        public CampaignWallet(int startingGold = 0, bool unlimited = false)
        {
            if (startingGold < 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(startingGold));
            IsUnlimited = unlimited;
            Balance = unlimited ? int.MaxValue : startingGold;
        }

        public bool TryRewardLevel(int level, int amount)
        {
            if (level < 1) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(level));
            if (amount < 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(amount));
            if (rewardedLevels.Contains(level)) return false;
            var total = IsUnlimited ? Balance : checked(Balance + amount);
            rewardedLevels.Add(level);
            Balance = total;
            return true;
        }

        public void AddBonusGold(int amount)
        {
            if (amount <= 0 || IsUnlimited) return;
            Balance = checked(Balance + amount);
        }

        public bool TrySpend(int price)
        {
            if (price <= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(price));
            if (IsUnlimited) return true;
            if (Balance < price) return false;
            Balance -= price;
            return true;
        }
    }
}.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Session\ClaimCardShop.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Session
{
    public sealed class ClaimCardShop
    {
        private static readonly UnitRole[] SummonableRoles =
        {
            UnitRole.Attack,
            UnitRole.Tank,
            UnitRole.Heal,
            UnitRole.Sniper,
            UnitRole.Vanguard,
            UnitRole.Demolisher
        };

        private readonly CampaignWallet wallet;
        private readonly SquadPreparation preparation;
        private readonly int baseCost;
        private readonly int costStep;
        private readonly List<UnitDefinition> currentOffer = new List<UnitDefinition>();

        public int SummonsCompleted { get; private set; }
        public int HighestStageCleared { get; private set; }
        public int NextPrice => baseCost + SummonsCompleted * costStep;
        public bool CanClaim => wallet.Balance >= NextPrice && preparation.CanAddMember;

        public ClaimCardShop(CampaignWallet wallet, SquadPreparation preparation, int baseCost = 30, int costStep = 15)
        {
            this.wallet = wallet ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(wallet));
            this.preparation = preparation ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(preparation));
            this.baseCost = Math.Max(1, baseCost);
            this.costStep = Math.Max(0, costStep);
        }

        public void SetStageProgress(int highestStageCleared)
        {
            HighestStageCleared = Math.Max(HighestStageCleared, highestStageCleared);
            currentOffer.Clear();
        }

        public (int minLevel, int maxLevel) GetLevelRange()
        {
            var stage = Math.Max(1, HighestStageCleared);
            return (stage, stage + 1);
        }

        // Higher rarity units also roll higher level ranges based on stage + rarity tier
        public (int minLevel, int maxLevel) GetLevelRangeForRarity(UnitRarity rarity)
        {
            var stage = Math.Max(1, HighestStageCleared);
            var rarityBonus = rarity switch
            {
                UnitRarity.Common => 0,
                UnitRarity.Rare => 1,
                UnitRarity.Epic => 2,
                UnitRarity.Legendary => 3,
                _ => 0
            };
            return (stage + rarityBonus, stage + 1 + rarityBonus);
        }

        // Frontloads Commons early and keeps high rarities genuinely scarce
        public (int common, int rare, int epic, int legendary) GetRarityWeights()
        {
            return HighestStageCleared switch
            {
                <= 1 => (90, 10, 0, 0),
                2 => (80, 20, 0, 0),
                3 => (70, 30, 0, 0),
                4 => (50, 45, 5, 0),
                5 => (40, 50, 9, 1),
                6 => (25, 55, 16, 4),
                _ => (0, 70, 24, 6)
            };
        }

        private static string RarityBox(string hex) =>
            $"<size=72%><voffset=0.12em><mark=#{hex}FF><color=#{hex}>M</color></mark></voffset></size>";

        public string GetBannerSummary()
        {
            var (minLv, maxLv) = GetLevelRange();
            var (c, r, e, l) = GetRarityWeights();

            var cBox = RarityBox("BFBFBF"); // Common (Silver)
            var rBox = RarityBox("338CE6"); // Rare (Blue)
            var eBox = RarityBox("9440D9"); // Epic (Purple)
            var lBox = RarityBox("FFBF26"); // Legendary (Gold)

            return $"Summon Tier {Math.Max(1, HighestStageCleared)}  |  Base Lv.{minLv}-{maxLv} (+Rarity Lv Bonus)  |  {cBox} {c}%  {rBox} {r}%  {eBox} {e}%  {lBox} {l}%";
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<UnitDefinition> BeginOffer()
        {
            if (!CanClaim) return Array.Empty<UnitDefinition>();
            if (currentOffer.Count == 0)
            {
                for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                    currentOffer.Add(RollUnit());
            }
            return currentOffer.AsReadOnly();
        }

        public bool TrySummon(int offerIndex)
        {
            if (!CanClaim || offerIndex < 0 || offerIndex >= currentOffer.Count) return false;
            var chosen = currentOffer[offerIndex];
            if (!wallet.TrySpend(NextPrice)) return false;

            SummonsCompleted++;
            currentOffer.Clear();
            preparation.TryAddSummonedUnit(chosen);
            return true;
        }

        private UnitDefinition RollUnit()
        {
            var role = SummonableRoles[UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, SummonableRoles.Length)];
            var rarity = RollRarity();
            var (minLv, maxLv) = GetLevelRangeForRarity(rarity);
            var level = UnityEngine.Random.Range(minLv, maxLv + 1);
            return SquadCombatRules.Create(role, rarity, level);
        }

        private UnitRarity RollRarity()
        {
            var (c, r, e, l) = GetRarityWeights();
            var roll = UnityEngine.Random.Range(0, c + r + e + l);
            if (roll < c) return UnitRarity.Common;
            roll -= c;
            if (roll < r) return UnitRarity.Rare;
            roll -= r;
            if (roll < e) return UnitRarity.Epic;
            return UnitRarity.Legendary;
        }
    }
}.

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Session
{
    [Serializable]
    public sealed class LevelRewardConfiguration
    {
        [Min(1)] public int level = 1;
        [Min(0)] public int gold = 45;
    }

    [CreateAssetMenu(menuName = "Team5/Rebuild/Game Economy Settings")]
    public sealed class GameEconomySettings : ScriptableObject
    {
        private static readonly int[] BalancedStageRewards = { 45, 65, 90, 120, 155, 195, 240 };

        [SerializeField, Min(0)] private int startingGold = 35;
        [SerializeField, Min(1)] private int baseSummonCost = 30;
        [SerializeField, Min(0)] private int summonCostIncreasePerPull = 15;
        [SerializeField, Min(0)] private int baseTowerBounty = 25;
        [SerializeField, Min(0)] private int aoeTowerBounty = 45;
        [SerializeField] private List<LevelRewardConfiguration> levelRewards = new List<LevelRewardConfiguration>();

        public int StartingGold => Mathf.Max(0, startingGold);
        public int BaseSummonCost => Mathf.Max(1, baseSummonCost);
        public int SummonCostIncreasePerPull => Mathf.Max(0, summonCostIncreasePerPull);
        public int BaseTowerBounty => Mathf.Max(5, baseTowerBounty);
        public int AoeTowerBounty => Mathf.Max(10, aoeTowerBounty);
        public IReadOnlyList<LevelRewardConfiguration> LevelRewards => levelRewards.AsReadOnly();

        public int GetStageClearGold(int level)
        {
            var match = RewardForLevel(level);
            if (match != null && match.gold > 0) return match.gold;
            var index = Mathf.Clamp(level - 1, 0, BalancedStageRewards.Length - 1);
            return BalancedStageRewards[index];
        }

        public int GetTowerDestroyGold(bool isAoe, int level)
        {
            var baseBounty = isAoe ? AoeTowerBounty : BaseTowerBounty;
            var stageScale = 1f + Mathf.Max(0, level - 1) * 0.20f;
            return Mathf.RoundToInt(baseBounty * stageScale);
        }

        public LevelRewardConfiguration RewardForLevel(int level)
        {
            foreach (var reward in levelRewards)
            {
                if (reward != null && reward.level == level) return reward;
            }
            return null;
        }
    }
}.

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Session
{
    public sealed class SquadPreparation
    {
        public const int WarehouseRows = 2, WarehouseColumns = 6;
        public const int WarehouseCapacity = WarehouseRows * WarehouseColumns;
        public const int MaxDeployed = 6;

        private readonly List<MemberSpawnDefinition> members;
        private readonly int[] memberIds = new int[9];

        public bool CanAddMember => members.Count < WarehouseCapacity && !members.Exists(m => m.MemberId == int.MaxValue);
        public SquadRoster Roster { get; }
        public IReadOnlyList<MemberSpawnDefinition> Members => members.AsReadOnly();
        public event Action Changed;

        public int DeployedCount
        {
            get
            {
                var count = 0;
                foreach (var id in memberIds) if (id != 0) count++;
                return count;
            }
        }

        public bool CanStart => DeployedCount > 0 && DeployedCount <= MaxDeployed;

        public SquadPreparation(SquadRoster roster)
            : this(roster != null ? roster.CaptureMembers() : throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(roster)))
            => Roster = roster;

        public SquadPreparation(IReadOnlyList<MemberSpawnDefinition> availableMembers)
        {
            if (availableMembers == null) throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(availableMembers));
            members = new List<MemberSpawnDefinition>(availableMembers);
            var ids = new HashSet<int>();
            foreach (var member in members)
                if (member == null || !ids.Add(member.MemberId))
                    throw new ArgumentException("Warehouse members require unique IDs.");
            if (members.Count > WarehouseCapacity)
                throw new ArgumentException("Warehouse supports up to twelve members.");
        }

        public bool TryAddSummonedUnit(UnitDefinition unit)
        {
            if (unit == null || unit.Role == UnitRole.Test) throw new ArgumentException(nameof(unit));
            if (!CanAddMember) return false;
            var id = 0;
            foreach (var member in members) id = Math.Max(id, member.MemberId);
            members.Add(new MemberSpawnDefinition(checked(id + 1), 4, unit));
            Changed?.Invoke();
            return true;
        }

        public bool TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole role) =>
            TryAddSummonedUnit(SquadCombatRules.Create(role));

        // Allows dismissing older/weaker units to free up storage space (protects Captain and last unit)
        public bool TryDismissMember(int memberId)
        {
            if (members.Count <= 1) return false;
            var member = FindMember(memberId);
            if (member == null || member.Unit.Role == UnitRole.Captain) return false;

            var slot = Array.IndexOf(memberIds, memberId);
            if (slot >= 0) memberIds[slot] = 0;

            members.Remove(member);
            Changed?.Invoke();
            return true;
        }

        public int MemberAtSlot(int slot) => slot >= 0 && slot < 9 ? memberIds[slot] : 0;
        public UnitRole RoleAtSlot(int slot) => FindMember(MemberAtSlot(slot))?.Unit.Role ?? UnitRole.Test;
        public bool IsDeployed(int memberId) => memberId > 0 && Array.IndexOf(memberIds, memberId) >= 0;

        public MemberSpawnDefinition FindMember(int memberId)
        {
            foreach (var member in members)
                if (member.MemberId == memberId) return member;
            return null;
        }

        public bool TryAssign(int memberId, int slot)
        {
            if (slot < 0 || slot >= 9 || FindMember(memberId) == null) return false;
            var previous = Array.IndexOf(memberIds, memberId);
            if (previous == slot) return true;
            if (previous < 0 && memberIds[slot] == 0 && DeployedCount >= MaxDeployed) return false;
            if (previous >= 0) memberIds[previous] = memberIds[slot];
            memberIds[slot] = memberId;
            Changed?.Invoke();
            return true;
        }

        public bool TryMove(int from, int to)
        {
            if (from < 0 || from >= 9 || to < 0 || to >= 9 || memberIds[from] == 0) return false;
            return TryAssign(memberIds[from], to);
        }

        public bool TryRemove(int slot)
        {
            if (slot < 0 || slot >= 9 || memberIds[slot] == 0) return false;
            memberIds[slot] = 0;
            Changed?.Invoke();
            return true;
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<MemberSpawnDefinition> CaptureDeployment()
        {
            if (!CanStart) throw new InvalidOperationException("Deploy between one and six members before starting.");
            var result = new List<MemberSpawnDefinition>();
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
            {
                var member = FindMember(memberIds[slot]);
                if (member != null)
                    result.Add(new MemberSpawnDefinition(member.MemberId, slot, member.Unit, member.SpawnTime));
            }
            return result.AsReadOnly();
        }
    }
}.

using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Simulation\BattleDefinition.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    public enum TargetRule { FurthestAlongRoute, Nearest }
    public enum UnitLifeState { Alive, Dead, ReachedGoal }
    public enum BattleOutcome { Running, Victory, Defeat }

    internal static class BattleNumbers
    {
        internal static bool Finite(float value) => !float.IsNaN(value) && !float.IsInfinity(value);
        internal static bool Finite(Vector3 value) => Finite(value.x) && Finite(value.y) && Finite(value.z);
    }

    public sealed class UnitDefinition
    {
        public float MaxHp { get; }
        public float Speed { get; }
        public UnitRole Role { get; }
        public UnitRarity Rarity { get; }
        public int Level { get; }
        public float Damage { get; }
        public float Range { get; }
        public float AttackInterval { get; }
        public float Defense { get; }
        public float HealPerSecond { get; }
        public float TowerDamageMultiplier { get; }

        public UnitDefinition(
            float maxHp,
            float speed,
            UnitRole role = UnitRole.Test,
            float damage = 0,
            float range = 2.5f,
            float attackInterval = 1,
            float defense = 0,
            float healPerSecond = 0.2f,
            UnitRarity rarity = UnitRarity.Common,
            int level = 1,
            float towerDamageMultiplier = 1f)
        {
            if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(maxHp) || maxHp <= 0 || !BattleNumbers.Finite(speed) || speed <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(maxHp), "HP and speed must be finite and positive.");
            if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(UnitRole), role) || !BattleNumbers.Finite(damage) || damage < 0 ||
                !BattleNumbers.Finite(range) || range < 0 || !BattleNumbers.Finite(attackInterval) || attackInterval <= 0 ||
                !BattleNumbers.Finite(defense) || defense < 0 || defense >= 1)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(role));
            if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(healPerSecond) || healPerSecond < 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(healPerSecond));
            if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(towerDamageMultiplier) || towerDamageMultiplier <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(towerDamageMultiplier));

            MaxHp = maxHp;
            Speed = speed;
            Role = role;
            Rarity = rarity;
            Level = Math.Max(1, level);
            Damage = damage;
            Range = range;
            AttackInterval = attackInterval;
            Defense = defense;
            HealPerSecond = healPerSecond;
            TowerDamageMultiplier = towerDamageMultiplier;
        }
    }

    public sealed class TowerDefinition
    {
        public int Id { get; }
        public Vector3 Position { get; }
        public float Range { get; }
        public float Damage { get; }
        public float AttackInterval { get; }
        public TargetRule TargetRule { get; }
        public int FootprintSize { get; }
        public float MaxHp { get; }
        public AttackArea Area { get; }
        public float AttackWidth { get; }
        public bool IsAoe { get; }
        public int GoldBounty { get; }

        public TowerDefinition(int id, Vector3 position, float range, float damage, float attackInterval, TargetRule targetRule,
            int footprintSize = 1, float maxHp = 20, AttackArea area = AttackArea.Square, float attackWidth = 0.3f,
            bool isAoe = false, int goldBounty = 20)
        {
            if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(position) || !BattleNumbers.Finite(range) || range < 0 ||
                !BattleNumbers.Finite(damage) || damage <= 0 || !BattleNumbers.Finite(attackInterval) || attackInterval <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(range), "Invalid tower configuration.");
            if (!Enum.IsDefined(typeof(TargetRule), targetRule)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(targetRule));
            if (footprintSize != 1 && footprintSize != 3) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(footprintSize));
            if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(maxHp) || maxHp <= 0 || !BattleNumbers.Finite(attackWidth) || attackWidth <= 0 ||
                !Enum.IsDefined(typeof(AttackArea), area)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(maxHp));

            Id = id;
            Position = position;
            Range = range;
            Damage = damage;
            AttackInterval = attackInterval;
            TargetRule = targetRule;
            FootprintSize = footprintSize;
            MaxHp = maxHp;
            Area = area;
            AttackWidth = attackWidth;
            IsAoe = isAoe;
            GoldBounty = Math.Max(0, goldBounty);
        }
    }

    public sealed class BattleDefinition
    {
        public ReadOnlyCollection<Vector3> Waypoints { get; }
        public ReadOnlyCollection<TowerDefinition> Towers { get; }
        public UnitDefinition Unit { get; }
        public int UnitCount { get; }
        public float SpawnInterval { get; }
        public int GoalLives { get; }
        public int FormationSize { get; }
        public int SquadCapacity => FormationSize * FormationSize;
        public BattleObjective Objective { get; private set; }
        public ReadOnlyCollection<MemberSpawnDefinition> Members { get; private set; }
        public ReadOnlyCollection<Vector3Int> WalkableCells { get; private set; }

        public BattleDefinition(IEnumerable<Vector3> waypoints, IEnumerable<TowerDefinition> towers,
            IEnumerable<MemberSpawnDefinition> members, IEnumerable<Vector3Int> walkableCells = null)
            : this(waypoints, towers, FirstUnit(members, out var captured), captured.Count, 1, 1, 3)
        {
            Objective = BattleObjective.ReachGoal;
            var ids = new HashSet<int>();
            var slots = new HashSet<int>();
            foreach (var member in captured)
                if (member == null || !ids.Add(member.MemberId) || !slots.Add(member.SlotId) || Mathf.Abs(member.Unit.Speed - Unit.Speed) > 0.00001f)
                    throw new ArgumentException("Members require unique IDs/slots and a shared formation speed.");
            Members = captured.AsReadOnly();
            WalkableCells = walkableCells == null ? null : new List<Vector3Int>(walkableCells).AsReadOnly();
        }

        private static UnitDefinition FirstUnit(IEnumerable<MemberSpawnDefinition> members, out List<MemberSpawnDefinition> captured)
        {
            captured = members == null ? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(members)) : new List<MemberSpawnDefinition>(members);
            if (captured.Count < 1 || captured.Count > 9 || captured[0] == null)
                throw new ArgumentException("A squad needs 1..9 planned members.");
            return captured[0].Unit;
        }

        public BattleDefinition(IEnumerable<Vector3> waypoints, IEnumerable<TowerDefinition> towers,
            UnitDefinition unit, int unitCount, float spawnInterval, int goalLives, int formationSize = 1)
        {
            if (waypoints == null || towers == null || unit == null) throw new ArgumentNullException();
            var points = new List<Vector3>(waypoints);
            if (points.Count < 2) throw new ArgumentException("A route needs at least two waypoints.");
            for (var i = 0; i < points.Count; i++)
            {
                if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(points[i])) throw new ArgumentException("Invalid waypoint.");
                if (i > 0 && Vector3.Distance(points[i - 1], points[i]) < 0.0001f)
                    throw new ArgumentException("Consecutive waypoints must differ.");
            }
            if (unitCount <= 0 || goalLives <= 0 || !BattleNumbers.Finite(spawnInterval) || spawnInterval <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(unitCount));
            if (formationSize != 1 && formationSize != 3) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(formationSize));
            if (formationSize == 3 && unitCount > 9)
                throw new ArgumentException("This prototype supports one squad of up to nine independent members.");
            var towerList = new List<TowerDefinition>(towers);
            var ids = new HashSet<int>();
            foreach (var tower in towerList)
                if (tower == null || !ids.Add(tower.Id)) throw new ArgumentException("Tower IDs must be unique.");

            Waypoints = points.AsReadOnly();
            Towers = towerList.AsReadOnly();
            Unit = unit;
            UnitCount = unitCount;
            SpawnInterval = spawnInterval;
            GoalLives = goalLives;
            FormationSize = formationSize;
        }
    }
}.

using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    public readonly struct UnitSpawnedEvent
    {
        public int UnitId { get; }
        public Vector3 Position { get; }
        public float Hp { get; }
        public UnitSpawnedEvent(UnitState unit) { UnitId = unit.Id; Position = unit.Position; Hp = unit.Hp; }
    }
    public readonly struct UnitMovedEvent
    {
        public int UnitId { get; }
        public Vector3 Position { get; }
        public float RouteProgress { get; }
        public UnitMovedEvent(UnitState unit) { UnitId = unit.Id; Position = unit.Position; RouteProgress = unit.RouteProgress; }
    }
    public readonly struct DamageEvent
    {
        public int TowerId { get; }
        public int UnitId { get; }
        public float Amount { get; }
        public float RemainingHp { get; }
        public Vector3 Position { get; }
        public DamageEvent(int towerId, UnitState unit, float amount)
        { TowerId = towerId; UnitId = unit.Id; Amount = amount; RemainingHp = unit.Hp; Position = unit.Position; }
    }
    public readonly struct UnitEndedEvent
    {
        public int UnitId { get; }
        public Vector3 Position { get; }
        public UnitEndedEvent(UnitState unit) { UnitId = unit.Id; Position = unit.Position; }
    }
    public readonly struct TowerAttackEvent
    {
        public int TowerId { get; }
        public int UnitId { get; }
        public Vector3 Origin { get; }
        public Vector3 TargetPosition { get; }
        public TowerAttackEvent(TowerState tower, UnitState unit)
        { TowerId = tower.Id; UnitId = unit.Id; Origin = tower.Position; TargetPosition = unit.Position; }
    }
    public readonly struct BattleEndedEvent
    {
        public BattleOutcome Outcome { get; }
        public int Killed { get; }
        public int ReachedGoal { get; }
        public BattleEndedEvent(BattleOutcome outcome, int killed, int reachedGoal)
        { Outcome = outcome; Killed = killed; ReachedGoal = reachedGoal; }
    }
    public readonly struct TowerDamageEvent
    {
        public int MemberId { get; }
        public int TowerId { get; }
        public float Amount { get; }
        public float RemainingHp { get; }
        public TowerDamageEvent(int memberId, TowerState tower, float amount)
        { MemberId = memberId; TowerId = tower.Id; Amount = amount; RemainingHp = tower.Hp; }
    }
    public readonly struct TowerDestroyedEvent
    {
        public int KillerMemberId { get; }
        public int TowerId { get; }
        public int GoldBounty { get; }
        public bool WasAoe { get; }
        public Vector3 Position { get; }
        public TowerDestroyedEvent(int killerMemberId, TowerState tower)
        {
            KillerMemberId = killerMemberId;
            TowerId = tower.Id;
            GoldBounty = tower.Definition.GoldBounty;
            WasAoe = tower.Definition.IsAoe;
            Position = tower.Position;
        }
    }
    public readonly struct HealEvent
    {
        public int HealerId { get; }
        public int MemberId { get; }
        public float Amount { get; }
        public float RemainingHp { get; }
        public HealEvent(int healerId, UnitState unit, float amount)
        { HealerId = healerId; MemberId = unit.Id; Amount = amount; RemainingHp = unit.Hp; }
    }
