=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Simulation\BattleSimulation.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    public sealed class BattleSimulation
    {
        private readonly BattleDefinition definition;
        private readonly List<UnitState> units = new List<UnitState>();
        private readonly List<TowerState> towers = new List<TowerState>();
        private readonly List<ProjectileState> projectiles = new List<ProjectileState>();
        private readonly HashSet<int> spawnedMembers = new HashSet<int>();
        private readonly HashSet<Vector3Int> walkable;
        private double time;
        private bool stepping;
        private double healTimer;
        private int projectileSequence;
        public ReadOnlyCollection<UnitState> Units { get; }
        public ReadOnlyCollection<TowerState> Towers { get; }
        public ReadOnlyCollection<ProjectileState> Projectiles { get; }
        public BattleOutcome Outcome { get; private set; } = BattleOutcome.Running;
        public BattleObjective Objective => definition.Objective;
        public bool IsPaused { get; private set; }
        public int SpawnedCount { get; private set; }
        public int PendingSpawnCount => definition.UnitCount - SpawnedCount;
        public int KilledCount { get; private set; }
        public int ReachedGoalCount { get; private set; }
        public int TowersDestroyedCount { get; private set; }
        public int TowerBountyGoldEarned { get; private set; }
        public int LivesRemaining => Math.Max(0, definition.GoalLives - ReachedGoalCount);
        public int AliveCount => SpawnedCount - KilledCount - ReachedGoalCount;
        public double ElapsedTime => time;
        public event Action<UnitSpawnedEvent> UnitSpawned;
        public event Action<UnitMovedEvent> UnitMoved;
        public event Action<DamageEvent> UnitDamaged;
        public event Action<UnitEndedEvent> UnitDied;
        public event Action<UnitEndedEvent> UnitReachedGoal;
        public event Action<TowerAttackEvent> TowerAttacked;
        public event Action<BattleEndedEvent> BattleEnded;
        public event Action<TowerDamageEvent> TowerDamaged;
        public event Action<int> TowerDied;
        public event Action<TowerDestroyedEvent> TowerDestroyed;
        public event Action<HealEvent> UnitHealed;
        public event Action<int> HealerPulsed;
        public event Action FormationChanged;

        public sealed class Checkpoint
        {
            private readonly BattleSimulation saved;
            internal Checkpoint(BattleSimulation saved) { this.saved = saved; }
            public BattleSimulation Restore() => saved.CopyState();
        }

        public Checkpoint CaptureCheckpoint()
        {
            if (stepping || Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Capture a running battle outside tick callbacks.");
            return new Checkpoint(CopyState());
        }

        private BattleSimulation CopyState()
        {
            var copy = new BattleSimulation(definition);
            copy.towers.Clear();
            foreach (var tower in towers) copy.towers.Add(tower.Copy());
            foreach (var unit in units) copy.units.Add(unit.Copy());
            foreach (var shot in projectiles) copy.projectiles.Add(shot.Copy());
            copy.spawnedMembers.UnionWith(spawnedMembers);
            copy.time = time; copy.healTimer = healTimer; copy.projectileSequence = projectileSequence;
            copy.Outcome = Outcome; copy.IsPaused = IsPaused;
            copy.SpawnedCount = SpawnedCount; copy.KilledCount = KilledCount; copy.ReachedGoalCount = ReachedGoalCount;
            copy.TowersDestroyedCount = TowersDestroyedCount; copy.TowerBountyGoldEarned = TowerBountyGoldEarned;
            return copy;
        }

        public BattleSimulation(BattleDefinition definition)
        {
            this.definition = definition ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(definition));
            foreach (var tower in definition.Towers) towers.Add(new TowerState(tower));
            Units = units.AsReadOnly(); Towers = towers.AsReadOnly(); Projectiles = projectiles.AsReadOnly();
            if (definition.WalkableCells == null) return;
            walkable = new HashSet<Vector3Int>(definition.WalkableCells);
            for (var i = 1; i < definition.Waypoints.Count; i++)
                for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                    foreach (var cell in FormationClearance.SweptCells(definition.Waypoints[i - 1] + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot),
                        definition.Waypoints[i] + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot)))
                        if (!walkable.Contains(cell) || HasTowerFootprint(cell))
                            throw new ArgumentException($"Formation trajectory blocked at {cell}, slot {slot}, segment {i - 1}.");
        }

        public void SetTowerPosition(int towerId, Vector3 position)
        {
            if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(position)) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(position));
            if (stepping) throw new InvalidOperationException("Cannot change tower position during a tick callback.");
            var tower = towers.Find(value => value.Id == towerId);
            if (tower == null) throw new ArgumentException("Unknown tower ID.");
            tower.Position = position;
        }
        public void SetPaused(bool paused)
        {
            if (stepping) throw new InvalidOperationException("Submit pause outside tick callbacks.");
            if (Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running) IsPaused = paused;
        }
        public UnitState GetMemberAtSlot(int slotId) => units.Find(unit => unit.IsAlive && unit.SlotId == slotId);

        public bool TryApplyFormation(IReadOnlyList<int> sourceSlots)
        {
            if (stepping || !IsPaused || Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running || definition.FormationSize != 3 ||
                sourceSlots == null || sourceSlots.Count != 9 || PendingSpawnCount != 0) return false;
            var seen = new HashSet<int>();
            var destinations = new int[9];
            for (var destination = 0; destination < 9; destination++)
            {
                var source = sourceSlots[destination];
                if (source < 0 || source > 8 || !seen.Add(source)) return false;
                destinations[source] = destination;
            }
            foreach (var unit in units)
                if (unit.IsAlive && !CanStand(unit.AnchorPosition + SquadCombatRules.Offset(destinations[unit.SlotId]))) return false;
            foreach (var unit in units)
            {
                if (!unit.IsAlive) continue;
                var anchor = unit.AnchorPosition;
                var destination = destinations[unit.SlotId];
                unit.FormationSlot = SquadCombatRules.Slot(destination);
                unit.FormationOffset = SquadCombatRules.Offset(destination);
                unit.Position = anchor + unit.FormationOffset;
            }
            FormationChanged?.Invoke();
            return true;
        }

        public void Step(float deltaTime)
        {
            if (!BattleNumbers.Finite(deltaTime) || deltaTime <= 0) throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(deltaTime));
            if (stepping) throw new InvalidOperationException("Simulation.Step cannot be called from an event listener.");
            if (Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running || IsPaused) return;
            stepping = true;
            try
            {
                Spawn();
                foreach (var unit in units)
                {
                    if (!unit.IsAlive) continue;
                    Move(unit, deltaTime);
                    if (Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running) return;
                    if (Objective == BattleObjective.DefendGoal && ReachedGoalCount >= definition.GoalLives) break;
                }
                if (ReachedGoalCount < definition.GoalLives)
                {
                    foreach (var tower in towers) Attack(tower, deltaTime);
                    if (Objective == BattleObjective.ReachGoal)
                    {
                        foreach (var unit in units) AttackTower(unit, deltaTime);
                        TickProjectiles(deltaTime);
                        Heal(deltaTime);
                    }
                }
                time += deltaTime;
                if (Objective == BattleObjective.ReachGoal)
                {
                    if (PendingSpawnCount == 0 && AliveCount == 0) End(BattleOutcome.Defeat);
                }
                else if (ReachedGoalCount >= definition.GoalLives) End(BattleOutcome.Defeat);
                else if (PendingSpawnCount == 0 && AliveCount == 0) End(BattleOutcome.Victory);
            }
            finally { stepping = false; }
        }

        private void Spawn()
        {
            if (definition.Members != null)
            {
                foreach (var member in definition.Members)
                {
                    if (spawnedMembers.Contains(member.MemberId) || member.SpawnTime > time + 0.000001) continue;
                    spawnedMembers.Add(member.MemberId);
                    var unit = new UnitState(member.MemberId, member.Unit,
                        SampleAnchor((float)time * member.Unit.Speed) + SquadCombatRules.Offset(member.SlotId),
                        1, SquadCombatRules.Slot(member.SlotId), SquadCombatRules.Offset(member.SlotId));
                    unit.RouteProgress = Mathf.Min((float)time * member.Unit.Speed, RouteLength());
                    unit.NextWaypoint = NextWaypoint(unit.RouteProgress);
                    units.Add(unit); SpawnedCount++;
                    UnitSpawned?.Invoke(new UnitSpawnedEvent(unit));
                }
                return;
            }
            while (SpawnedCount < definition.UnitCount &&
                (SpawnedCount / definition.SquadCapacity) * (double)definition.SpawnInterval <= time + 0.000001)
            {
                var squadId = SpawnedCount / definition.SquadCapacity + 1;
                var count = Math.Min(definition.SquadCapacity, definition.UnitCount - SpawnedCount);
                for (var slot = 0; slot < count; slot++)
                {
                    var coordinate = new Vector2Int(slot % definition.FormationSize, slot / definition.FormationSize);
                    var radius = definition.FormationSize / 2;
                    var offset = new Vector3(coordinate.x - radius, 0, coordinate.y - radius);
                    var unit = new UnitState(++SpawnedCount, definition.Unit, definition.Waypoints[0] + offset, squadId, coordinate, offset);
                    units.Add(unit); UnitSpawned?.Invoke(new UnitSpawnedEvent(unit));
                }
            }
        }

        private void Move(UnitState unit, float deltaTime)
        {
            var remaining = unit.Speed * deltaTime;
            while (remaining > 0 && unit.NextWaypoint < definition.Waypoints.Count)
            {
                var target = definition.Waypoints[unit.NextWaypoint] + unit.FormationOffset;
                var distance = Vector3.Distance(unit.Position, target);
                var travel = Math.Min(remaining, distance);
                unit.Position = Vector3.MoveTowards(unit.Position, target, travel);
                unit.RouteProgress += travel; remaining -= travel;
                if (travel >= distance) unit.NextWaypoint++; else break;
            }
            UnitMoved?.Invoke(new UnitMovedEvent(unit));
            if (unit.NextWaypoint < definition.Waypoints.Count) return;
            unit.LifeState = UnitLifeState.ReachedGoal; ReachedGoalCount++;
            if (Objective == BattleObjective.ReachGoal) Outcome = BattleOutcome.Victory;
            UnitReachedGoal?.Invoke(new UnitEndedEvent(unit));
            if (Objective == BattleObjective.ReachGoal) BattleEnded?.Invoke(new BattleEndedEvent(Outcome, KilledCount, ReachedGoalCount));
        }

        private void Attack(TowerState tower, float deltaTime)
        {
            if (!tower.IsAlive) { tower.TargetId = null; return; }
            tower.CooldownRemaining = Math.Max(0, tower.CooldownRemaining - deltaTime);
            tower.TargetId = null;
            if (tower.CooldownRemaining > 0.000001f) return;

            // AOE Tower Mode: hits ALL living units inside its coverage area simultaneously
            if (tower.Definition.IsAoe)
            {
                var hitAny = false;
                foreach (var unit in units)
                {
                    if (!unit.IsAlive) continue;
                    var offset = unit.Position - tower.Position;
                    if (!SquadCombatRules.Contains(offset, tower.Definition.Area, tower.Definition.Range, tower.Definition.AttackWidth)) continue;
                    hitAny = true;
                    tower.TargetId = unit.Id;
                    TowerAttacked?.Invoke(new TowerAttackEvent(tower, unit));
                    if (Objective == BattleObjective.DefendGoal)
                    {
                        DamageUnit(tower.Id, unit, tower.Definition.Damage);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        projectiles.Add(new ProjectileState
                        {
                            Id = ++projectileSequence,
                            SourceId = tower.Id,
                            TargetKind = ProjectileTargetKind.FormationSlot,
                            TargetId = unit.SlotId,
                            Position = tower.Position,
                            Origin = tower.Position,
                            Damage = tower.Definition.Damage,
                            Straight = false,
                            Range = tower.Definition.Range,
                            Width = tower.Definition.AttackWidth
                        });
                    }
                }
                if (hitAny) tower.CooldownRemaining = tower.Definition.AttackInterval;
                return;
            }

            // Standard Single-Target Mode
            UnitState target = null;
            var bestDistance = float.MaxValue;
            foreach (var unit in units)
            {
                if (!unit.IsAlive) continue;
                var offset = unit.Position - tower.Position;
                var distance = offset.sqrMagnitude;
                if (!SquadCombatRules.Contains(offset, tower.Definition.Area, tower.Definition.Range, tower.Definition.AttackWidth)) continue;
                var better = target == null || (tower.Definition.TargetRule == TargetRule.Nearest ? distance < bestDistance :
                    unit.RouteProgress > target.RouteProgress ||
                    (Objective == BattleObjective.ReachGoal && Mathf.Abs(unit.RouteProgress - target.RouteProgress) <= 0.001f && distance < bestDistance));
                if (!better) continue;
                target = unit; bestDistance = distance;
            }
            if (target == null) return;
            tower.TargetId = target.Id; tower.CooldownRemaining = tower.Definition.AttackInterval;
            TowerAttacked?.Invoke(new TowerAttackEvent(tower, target));
            if (Objective == BattleObjective.DefendGoal) DamageUnit(tower.Id, target, tower.Definition.Damage);
            else projectiles.Add(new ProjectileState
            {
                Id = ++projectileSequence,
                SourceId = tower.Id,
                TargetKind = ProjectileTargetKind.FormationSlot,
                TargetId = target.SlotId,
                Position = tower.Position,
                Origin = tower.Position,
                Damage = tower.Definition.Damage,
                Straight = tower.Definition.Area != AttackArea.Square,
                Direction = SquadCombatRules.Direction(tower.Definition.Area),
                Range = tower.Definition.Range,
                Width = tower.Definition.AttackWidth
            });
        }

        private void AttackTower(UnitState unit, float deltaTime)
        {
            if (!unit.IsAlive || !SquadCombatRules.CanAttackTowers(unit.Role)) return;
            unit.AttackCooldown = Math.Max(0, unit.AttackCooldown - deltaTime);
            if (unit.AttackCooldown > 0.000001f) return;
            TowerState target = null;
            var nearest = unit.Definition.Range * unit.Definition.Range;
            foreach (var tower in towers)
            {
                if (!tower.IsAlive) continue;
                var distance = (tower.Position - unit.Position).sqrMagnitude;
                if (distance > nearest) continue;
                target = tower; nearest = distance;
            }
            if (target == null) return;
            unit.AttackCooldown = unit.Definition.AttackInterval;
            var effectiveTowerDamage = unit.Definition.Damage * unit.Definition.TowerDamageMultiplier;
            projectiles.Add(new ProjectileState
            {
                Id = ++projectileSequence,
                SourceId = unit.Id,
                TargetKind = ProjectileTargetKind.Tower,
                TargetId = target.Id,
                Position = unit.Position,
                Damage = effectiveTowerDamage
            });
        }

        private void TickProjectiles(float deltaTime)
        {
            foreach (var shot in projectiles)
            {
                if (!shot.IsActive) continue;
                UnitState member = null; TowerState tower = null;
                Vector3 aim;
                if (shot.TargetKind == ProjectileTargetKind.Tower)
                {
                    tower = towers.Find(value => value.Id == shot.TargetId && value.IsAlive);
                    if (tower == null) { shot.IsActive = false; continue; }
                    aim = tower.Position;
                }
                else
                {
                    member = GetMemberAtSlot(shot.TargetId);
                    aim = member != null ? member.Position : SampleAnchor(((float)time + deltaTime) * definition.Unit.Speed) + SquadCombatRules.Offset(shot.TargetId);
                }
                bool hit;
                if (shot.Straight)
                {
                    var previous = shot.Travel;
                    shot.Travel = Mathf.Min(shot.Range, previous + SquadCombatRules.ProjectileSpeed * deltaTime);
                    shot.Position = shot.Origin + shot.Direction * shot.Travel;
                    var forward = Vector3.Dot(aim - shot.Origin, shot.Direction);
                    hit = member != null && SquadCombatRules.Contains(aim - shot.Origin,
                        DirectionArea(shot.Direction), shot.Range, shot.Width) && forward >= previous - 0.05f && forward <= shot.Travel + 0.05f;
                }
                else
                {
                    shot.Position = Vector3.MoveTowards(shot.Position, aim, SquadCombatRules.ProjectileSpeed * deltaTime);
                    hit = (shot.Position - aim).sqrMagnitude <= 0.0025f;
                }
                if (hit)
                {
                    shot.IsActive = false;
                    if (tower != null) DamageTower(shot.SourceId, tower, shot.Damage);
                    else if (member != null) DamageUnit(shot.SourceId, member, shot.Damage);
                }
                shot.Age += deltaTime;
                if (shot.Age >= 5 || (shot.Straight && shot.Travel >= shot.Range)) shot.IsActive = false;
            }
        }

        private void DamageUnit(int sourceId, UnitState unit, float rawDamage)
        {
            if (!unit.IsAlive) return;
            var applied = Math.Min(unit.Hp, rawDamage * (1 - unit.Definition.Defense));
            unit.Hp = Math.Max(0, unit.Hp - applied);
            if (unit.Hp == 0) { unit.LifeState = UnitLifeState.Dead; KilledCount++; }
            UnitDamaged?.Invoke(new DamageEvent(sourceId, unit, applied));
            if (!unit.IsAlive) UnitDied?.Invoke(new UnitEndedEvent(unit));
        }

        private void DamageTower(int sourceId, TowerState tower, float rawDamage)
        {
            if (!tower.IsAlive) return;
            var applied = Math.Min(tower.Hp, rawDamage);
            tower.Hp = Math.Max(0, tower.Hp - applied);
            // Active suppression: hitting a tower slightly delays its next shot
            if (tower.IsAlive)
                tower.CooldownRemaining = Mathf.Min(tower.Definition.AttackInterval, tower.CooldownRemaining + 0.08f);

            TowerDamaged?.Invoke(new TowerDamageEvent(sourceId, tower, applied));
            if (!tower.IsAlive)
            {
                tower.TargetId = null;
                TowersDestroyedCount++;
                TowerBountyGoldEarned += tower.Definition.GoldBounty;
                TowerDestroyed?.Invoke(new TowerDestroyedEvent(sourceId, tower));
                TowerDied?.Invoke(tower.Id);
            }
        }

        private void Heal(float deltaTime)
        {
            healTimer += deltaTime;
            if (healTimer + 0.000001f < SquadCombatRules.HealInterval) return;
            healTimer -= SquadCombatRules.HealInterval;
            foreach (var healer in units)
            {
                if (!healer.IsAlive || healer.Role != UnitRole.Heal) continue;
                HealerPulsed?.Invoke(healer.Id);
                foreach (var member in units)
                {
                    if (!member.IsAlive || member.SquadId != healer.SquadId || !SquadCombatRules.AreNeighbors(healer.SlotId, member.SlotId)) continue;
                    var amount = Math.Min(member.MaxHp - member.Hp, healer.Definition.HealPerSecond * SquadCombatRules.HealInterval);
                    if (amount <= 0) continue;
                    member.Hp += amount; UnitHealed?.Invoke(new HealEvent(healer.Id, member, amount));
                }
            }
        }

        private void End(BattleOutcome outcome)
        {
            if (Outcome != BattleOutcome.Running) return;
            Outcome = outcome; BattleEnded?.Invoke(new BattleEndedEvent(outcome, KilledCount, ReachedGoalCount));
        }
        private float RouteLength()
        {
            var length = 0f;
            for (var i = 1; i < definition.Waypoints.Count; i++) length += Vector3.Distance(definition.Waypoints[i - 1], definition.Waypoints[i]);
            return length;
        }
        private int NextWaypoint(float distance)
        {
            for (var i = 1; i < definition.Waypoints.Count; i++)
            {
                var segment = Vector3.Distance(definition.Waypoints[i - 1], definition.Waypoints[i]);
                if (distance < segment) return i;
                distance -= segment;
            }
            return definition.Waypoints.Count;
        }
        private Vector3 SampleAnchor(float distance)
        {
            for (var i = 1; i < definition.Waypoints.Count; i++)
            {
                var segment = Vector3.Distance(definition.Waypoints[i - 1], definition.Waypoints[i]);
                if (distance <= segment) return Vector3.Lerp(definition.Waypoints[i - 1], definition.Waypoints[i], distance / segment);
                distance -= segment;
            }
            return definition.Waypoints[definition.Waypoints.Count - 1];
        }
        private bool CanStand(Vector3 position)
        {
            foreach (var cell in FormationClearance.SweptCells(position, position))
                if ((walkable != null && !walkable.Contains(cell)) || HasTowerFootprint(cell)) return false;
            return true;
        }
        private bool HasTowerFootprint(Vector3Int cell)
        {
            foreach (var tower in towers)
            {
                var center = SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(tower.Position);
                var radius = tower.Definition.FootprintSize / 2;
                if (center.y == cell.y && Math.Abs(center.x - cell.x) <= radius && Math.Abs(center.z - cell.z) <= radius) return true;
            }
            return false;
        }
        public SmallCellOccupancy GetCellOccupancy(Vector3Int cell)
        {
            var towerIds = new List<int>(); var unitIds = new List<int>();
            foreach (var tower in towers)
            {
                var center = SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(tower.Position);
                var radius = tower.Definition.FootprintSize / 2;
                if (center.y == cell.y && Math.Abs(center.x - cell.x) <= radius && Math.Abs(center.z - cell.z) <= radius) towerIds.Add(tower.Id);
            }
            foreach (var unit in units) if (unit.IsAlive && unit.OccupiedCell == cell) unitIds.Add(unit.Id);
            return new SmallCellOccupancy(towerIds, unitIds);
        }
        private static AttackArea DirectionArea(Vector3 direction) => direction.x > 0 ? AttackArea.PositiveX : direction.x < 0 ? AttackArea.NegativeX :
            direction.z > 0 ? AttackArea.PositiveZ : AttackArea.NegativeZ;
    }
}.

using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    public sealed class UnitState
    {
        internal UnitState Copy() => (UnitState)MemberwiseClone();
        public int Id { get; }
        public int MemberId => Id;
        public UnitDefinition Definition { get; }
        public UnitRole Role => Definition.Role;
        public int SlotId => FormationSlot.y * 3 + FormationSlot.x;
        public float AttackCooldown { get; internal set; }
        public float MaxHp { get; }
        public float Hp { get; internal set; }
        public float Speed { get; }
        public int SquadId { get; }
        public Vector2Int FormationSlot { get; internal set; }
        public Vector3 FormationOffset { get; internal set; }
        public Vector3 AnchorPosition => Position - FormationOffset;
        // Half-open cell boundaries avoid banker's rounding collisions at exact half-cell steps.
        public Vector3Int OccupiedCell => SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(Position);
        public Vector3 Position { get; internal set; }
        public float RouteProgress { get; internal set; }
        public int NextWaypoint { get; internal set; } = 1;
        public UnitLifeState LifeState { get; internal set; } = UnitLifeState.Alive;
        public bool IsAlive => LifeState == UnitLifeState.Alive;
        internal UnitState(int id, UnitDefinition definition, Vector3 position, int squadId = 1,

            Definition = definition;
            MaxHp = Hp = definition.MaxHp;
            Speed = definition.Speed;
            Position = position;
            SquadId = squadId;
            FormationSlot = formationSlot;
            FormationOffset = formationOffset;
        }

    }

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Simulation\FormationClearance.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    public static class FormationClearance
    {
        // Positive-area terrain overlap of a 1x1 member footprint swept along an axis.
        // Corners consist of two segments. This never assumes that road width alone suffices.
        public static IEnumerable<Vector3Int> SweptCells(Vector3 from, Vector3 to)
        {
            if (Mathf.Abs(from.y - to.y) > 0.001f ||
                (Mathf.Abs(from.x - to.x) > 0.001f && Mathf.Abs(from.z - to.z) > 0.001f))
                throw new ArgumentException("Formation trajectories must be equal-height and axis aligned.");
            var minX = Mathf.FloorToInt(Mathf.Min(from.x, to.x) + 0.0001f);
            var maxX = Mathf.CeilToInt(Mathf.Max(from.x, to.x) - 0.0001f);
            var minZ = Mathf.FloorToInt(Mathf.Min(from.z, to.z) + 0.0001f);
            var maxZ = Mathf.CeilToInt(Mathf.Max(from.z, to.z) - 0.0001f);
            var layer = SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(from).y;
            for (var z = minZ; z <= maxZ; z++)
            for (var x = minX; x <= maxX; x++) yield return new Vector3Int(x, layer, z);
        }
    }
}

using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    // A derived query result, never a second editable map or a renderer/collider query.
    public sealed class SmallCellOccupancy
    {
        public ReadOnlyCollection<int> TowerIds { get; }
        public ReadOnlyCollection<int> UnitIds { get; }
        public bool IsOccupied => TowerIds.Count != 0 || UnitIds.Count != 0;
        internal SmallCellOccupancy(List<int> towers, List<int> units)
        { TowerIds = towers.AsReadOnly(); UnitIds = units.AsReadOnly(); }
        public static Vector3Int PositionToCell(Vector3 feet) => new Vector3Int(
            Mathf.FloorToInt(feet.x + 0.5f), Mathf.FloorToInt((feet.y - 0.25f) / 0.5f + 0.5f), Mathf.FloorToInt(feet.z + 0.5f));
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\Simulation\SquadCombatRules.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Simulation
{
    public enum UnitRole { Test, Attack, Tank, Heal, Captain, Sniper, Vanguard, Demolisher }
    public enum UnitRarity { Common = 0, Rare = 1, Epic = 2, Legendary = 3 }
    public enum BattleObjective { DefendGoal, ReachGoal }
    public enum AttackArea { Square = 0, PositiveX = 1, NegativeX = 2, PositiveZ = 3, NegativeZ = 4 }
    public enum ProjectileTargetKind { Tower, FormationSlot }

    public readonly struct RoleBaseStats
    {
        public readonly float MaxHp, Damage, Range, AttackInterval, Defense, HealPerSecond, TowerDamageMultiplier;
        public RoleBaseStats(
            float maxHp,
            float damage = 0f,
            float range = 2.5f,
            float interval = 1f,
            float defense = 0f,
            float heal = 0f,
            float towerDamageMultiplier = 1f)
        {
            MaxHp = maxHp;
            Damage = damage;
            Range = range;
            AttackInterval = interval;
            Defense = defense;
            HealPerSecond = heal;
            TowerDamageMultiplier = towerDamageMultiplier;
        }
    }

    public static class SquadCombatRules
    {
        public const float Speed = 0.5f;
        public const float AttackInterval = 1f;
        public const float AttackRange = 2.5f;
        public const float ProjectileSpeed = 8f;
        public const float HealInterval = 2f;
        public const float HealAmount = 0.4f;
        public const float LevelStatGrowth = 0.15f;

        // Modular role table including new offensive/hybrid units and tower-breaker multipliers
        private static readonly Dictionary<UnitRole, RoleBaseStats> RoleTable = new Dictionary<UnitRole, RoleBaseStats>
        {
            { UnitRole.Attack,     new RoleBaseStats(maxHp: 4.5f, damage: 1.6f, range: 2.8f, interval: 0.9f, towerDamageMultiplier: 1.50f) },
            { UnitRole.Tank,       new RoleBaseStats(maxHp: 9.0f, defense: 0.25f) },
            { UnitRole.Heal,       new RoleBaseStats(maxHp: 3.5f, heal: 0.35f) },
            { UnitRole.Captain,    new RoleBaseStats(maxHp: 6.5f, damage: 0.8f, range: 2.6f, interval: 1.0f, defense: 0.50f, towerDamageMultiplier: 1.25f) },
            { UnitRole.Sniper,     new RoleBaseStats(maxHp: 3.2f, damage: 2.4f, range: 4.2f, interval: 1.35f, towerDamageMultiplier: 1.85f) },
            { UnitRole.Vanguard,   new RoleBaseStats(maxHp: 7.0f, damage: 1.1f, range: 2.2f, interval: 1.05f, defense: 0.20f, towerDamageMultiplier: 1.35f) },
            { UnitRole.Demolisher, new RoleBaseStats(maxHp: 5.0f, damage: 2.2f, range: 2.5f, interval: 1.15f, defense: 0.10f, towerDamageMultiplier: 2.25f) }
        };

        public static bool CanAttackTowers(UnitRole role) =>
            role == UnitRole.Attack || role == UnitRole.Captain ||
            role == UnitRole.Sniper || role == UnitRole.Vanguard || role == UnitRole.Demolisher;

        // Higher rarities are much harder to pull, so their base multiplier is significantly stronger
        public static float RarityMultiplier(UnitRarity rarity) => rarity switch
        {
            UnitRarity.Common => 1.00f,
            UnitRarity.Rare => 1.85f,
            UnitRarity.Epic => 3.10f,
            UnitRarity.Legendary => 5.00f,
            _ => 1.00f
        };

        // Per-level stat growth also scales steeper with rarity
        public static float RarityLevelGrowth(UnitRarity rarity) => rarity switch
        {
            UnitRarity.Common => 0.12f,
            UnitRarity.Rare => 0.20f,
            UnitRarity.Epic => 0.30f,
            UnitRarity.Legendary => 0.45f,
            _ => LevelStatGrowth
        };

        public static float ScaleMultiplier(UnitRarity rarity, int level)
        {
            var clampedLevel = Mathf.Max(1, level);
            return RarityMultiplier(rarity) * (1f + RarityLevelGrowth(rarity) * (clampedLevel - 1));
        }

        public static UnitDefinition Create(UnitRole role, UnitRarity rarity = UnitRarity.Common, int level = 1)
        {
            if (!RoleTable.TryGetValue(role, out var baseStats))
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(role), $"No base stats configured for role {role}.");

            var mult = ScaleMultiplier(rarity, level);
            var hp = Mathf.Round(baseStats.MaxHp * mult * 10f) / 10f;
            var dmg = Mathf.Round(baseStats.Damage * mult * 10f) / 10f;
            var heal = baseStats.HealPerSecond > 0f ? Mathf.Round(baseStats.HealPerSecond * mult * 10f) / 10f : 0.2f;
            var def = baseStats.Defense > 0f
                ? Mathf.Min(0.78f, baseStats.Defense + (int)rarity * 0.06f + (Mathf.Max(1, level) - 1) * 0.025f)
                : 0f;

            return new UnitDefinition(
                maxHp: hp,
                speed: Speed,
                role: role,
                damage: dmg,
                range: baseStats.Range,
                attackInterval: baseStats.AttackInterval,
                defense: def,
                healPerSecond: heal,
                rarity: rarity,
                level: Mathf.Max(1, level),
                towerDamageMultiplier: baseStats.TowerDamageMultiplier
            );
        }

        public static Vector2Int Slot(int id) => new Vector2Int(id % 3, id / 3);
        public static Vector3 Offset(int id) => new Vector3(id % 3 - 1, 0, id / 3 - 1);
        public static bool AreNeighbors(int first, int second) =>
            first != second && Math.Abs(first % 3 - second % 3) <= 1 && Math.Abs(first / 3 - second / 3) <= 1;

        public static Vector3 Direction(AttackArea area) => area switch
        {
            AttackArea.PositiveX => Vector3.right,
            AttackArea.NegativeX => Vector3.left,
            AttackArea.PositiveZ => Vector3.forward,
            AttackArea.NegativeZ => Vector3.back,
            _ => Vector3.zero
        };

        public static bool Contains(Vector3 offset, AttackArea area, float range, float width)
        {
            if (area == AttackArea.Square) return Mathf.Abs(offset.x) <= range && Mathf.Abs(offset.z) <= range;
            var direction = Direction(area);
            var forward = Vector3.Dot(offset, direction);
            var side = Vector3.Dot(offset, new Vector3(-direction.z, 0, direction.x));
            return forward >= 0 && forward <= range && Mathf.Abs(side) <= width / 2;
        }

        public static Bounds AreaBounds(Vector3 position, AttackArea area, float range, float width)
        {
            if (area == AttackArea.Square) return new Bounds(position, new Vector3(range * 2, 0, range * 2));
            var direction = Direction(area);
            var alongX = direction.x != 0;
            return new Bounds(position + direction * (range / 2), new Vector3(alongX ? range : width, 0, alongX ? width : range));
        }
    }

    public sealed class MemberSpawnDefinition
    {
        public int MemberId { get; }
        public int SlotId { get; }
        public float SpawnTime { get; }
        public UnitDefinition Unit { get; }

        public MemberSpawnDefinition(int memberId, int slotId, UnitDefinition unit, float spawnTime = 0)
        {
            if (memberId <= 0 || slotId < 0 || slotId > 8 || !BattleNumbers.Finite(spawnTime) || spawnTime < 0)
                throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException(nameof(memberId));
            MemberId = memberId;
            SlotId = slotId;
            Unit = unit ?? throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(unit));
            SpawnTime = spawnTime;
        }
    }
}.

using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattleCell : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const float LayerHeight = 0.5f;
        public static Vector3 Size => new Vector3(1, LayerHeight, 1);
        [SerializeField] private CellKind kind;
        public CellKind Kind => kind;
        public Vector3Int Occupancy => new Vector3Int(Mathf.RoundToInt(transform.position.x),
            Mathf.RoundToInt(transform.position.y / LayerHeight), Mathf.RoundToInt(transform.position.z));
        public Vector3 TopCenter => transform.position + Vector3.up * (LayerHeight / 2);
        public bool IsRouteSurface => kind == CellKind.Road || kind == CellKind.Spawn || kind == CellKind.Goal;

        public void CollectErrors(List<string> errors)
        {
            var position = transform.position;
            var key = Occupancy;
            if (!float.IsFinite(position.x) || !float.IsFinite(position.y) || !float.IsFinite(position.z) ||
                Vector3.Distance(position, new Vector3(key.x, key.y * LayerHeight, key.z)) > 0.001f)
                errors.Add($"{name}: cell center must align to X/Z = 1 and Y = 0.5.");
            if (Quaternion.Angle(transform.rotation, Quaternion.identity) > 0.01f ||
                Vector3.Distance(transform.lossyScale, Vector3.one) > 0.001f)
                errors.Add($"{name}: cells require identity world rotation and scale; physical size is 1 x 0.5 x 1.");
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\World\BattleGrid.cs === 
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattleGrid : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const int MacroSize = 3;
        // Only a temporary lookup. The Scene's child BattleCells remain the authority.
        private readonly Dictionary<Vector3Int, BattleCell> index = new Dictionary<Vector3Int, BattleCell>();
        public BattleCell[] SceneCells => GetComponentsInChildren<BattleCell>(true);

        public void RebuildIndex(List<string> errors)
        {
            index.Clear();
            var cells = SceneCells;
            if (cells.Length == 0) errors.Add("BattleGrid contains no cells.");
            foreach (var cell in cells)
            {
                cell.CollectErrors(errors);
                if (!cell.gameObject.activeInHierarchy) errors.Add($"{cell.name}: configured cells must be active.");
                if (index.TryGetValue(cell.Occupancy, out var other))
                    errors.Add($"Grid conflict at {cell.Occupancy}: {other.name} and {cell.name}.");
                else index.Add(cell.Occupancy, cell);
            }
        }

        // RebuildIndex must be called before querying after an edit.
        public bool TryGetCell(Vector3Int occupancy, out BattleCell cell) => index.TryGetValue(occupancy, out cell);
        public bool Contains(BattleCell cell) => cell != null && index.TryGetValue(cell.Occupancy, out var value) && value == cell;
        public bool HasClearTop(BattleCell cell) => !index.ContainsKey(cell.Occupancy + Vector3Int.up);

        public static Vector3Int ToMacroCell(Vector3Int smallCell) => new Vector3Int(
            Mathf.FloorToInt(smallCell.x / 3f), smallCell.y, Mathf.FloorToInt(smallCell.z / 3f));

        // Macro coordinates are (macro X, unchanged height layer, macro Z). No serialized Macro positions.
        public BattleCell[] GetMacroCells(Vector3Int macro)
        {
            var cells = new BattleCell[9];
            for (var z = 0; z < 3; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 3; x++)
                index.TryGetValue(new Vector3Int(macro.x * 3 + x, macro.y, macro.z * 3 + z), out cells[z * 3 + x]);
            return cells;
        }

        public SmallCellOccupancy[] GetMacroOccupancy(Vector3Int macro, BattleSimulation simulation)
        {
            var result = new SmallCellOccupancy[9];
            for (var z = 0; z < 3; z++)
            for (var x = 0; x < 3; x++)
                result[z * 3 + x] = simulation.GetCellOccupancy(new Vector3Int(macro.x * 3 + x, macro.y, macro.z * 3 + z));
            return result;
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class BattleTower : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField, Min(0)] private float range = 2.4f;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float damage = 18;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float attackInterval = 0.8f;
        [SerializeField] private TargetRule targetRule = TargetRule.FurthestAlongRoute;
        [SerializeField] private bool usesMacroFootprint;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float maxHp = 20;
        [SerializeField] private AttackArea attackArea = AttackArea.Square;
        [SerializeField, Min(0.01f)] private float attackWidth = 0.3f;
        [SerializeField] private bool isAoe;
        [SerializeField, Min(0)] private int goldBounty = 25;

        public int FootprintSize => usesMacroFootprint ? 3 : 1;
        public float Range => range;
        public float Damage => damage;
        public float AttackInterval => attackInterval;
        public TargetRule TargetRule => targetRule;
        public bool IsAoe => isAoe;
        public int GoldBounty => goldBounty > 0 ? goldBounty : (isAoe ? 40 : 25);
        public TowerState RuntimeState { get; private set; }
        public float CurrentCooldown => RuntimeState?.CooldownRemaining ?? 0;

        public TowerDefinition CaptureDefinition(int id) =>
            new TowerDefinition(id, transform.position, range, damage, attackInterval, targetRule, FootprintSize, maxHp, attackArea, attackWidth, isAoe, GoldBounty);

        public void BindRuntimeState(TowerState state) => RuntimeState = state;

        public bool HasValidConfiguration => float.IsFinite(range) && range >= 0 &&
            float.IsFinite(damage) && damage > 0 && float.IsFinite(attackInterval) && attackInterval > 0 &&
            float.IsFinite(transform.position.x) && float.IsFinite(transform.position.y) && float.IsFinite(transform.position.z) &&
            Enum.IsDefined(typeof(TargetRule), targetRule) && float.IsFinite(maxHp) && maxHp > 0 &&
            float.IsFinite(attackWidth) && attackWidth > 0 && Enum.IsDefined(typeof(AttackArea), attackArea);

        public void CollectFootprintErrors(BattleGrid grid, HashSet<Vector3Int> occupied, List<string> errors)
        {
            var position = transform.position;
            var center = SmallCellOccupancy.PositionToCell(position);
            if (usesMacroFootprint)
            {
                var macro = BattleGrid.ToMacroCell(center);
                var expected = new Vector3(macro.x * 3 + 1, center.y * 0.5f + 0.25f, macro.z * 3 + 1);
                if (Vector3.Distance(position, expected) > 0.001f)
                    errors.Add($"{name}: 3x3 tower must be centered on a Macro Cell at its top surface.");
            }
            var radius = FootprintSize / 2;
            for (var z = -radius; z <= radius; z++)
                for (var x = -radius; x <= radius; x++)
                {
                    var key = center + new Vector3Int(x, 0, z);
                    if (!occupied.Add(key)) errors.Add($"{name}: tower footprint overlap at {key}.");
                    if (!grid.TryGetCell(key, out var support) || support.Kind != CellKind.TowerPlatform ||
                        !grid.HasClearTop(support) || Mathf.Abs(position.y - support.TopCenter.y) > 0.001f)
                        errors.Add($"{name}: footprint {key} needs a clear, equal-height TowerPlatform surface.");
                }
        }
    }
}.

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Scripts\World\SceneRoute.cs === 
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.World
{
    [DisallowMultipleComponent]
    public sealed class SceneRoute : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] private List<BattleCell> cells = new List<BattleCell>();
        [SerializeField] private int requiredWidth = 1;
        public IReadOnlyList<BattleCell> Cells => cells.AsReadOnly();
        public int RequiredWidth => requiredWidth;

        public void CollectErrors(BattleGrid grid, List<string> errors)
        {
            if (cells.Count < 2) { errors.Add("SceneRoute needs a Spawn and a Goal with an ordered road."); return; }
            var visited = new HashSet<BattleCell>();
            if (requiredWidth != 1 && requiredWidth != 3) errors.Add("Route width must be 1 or 3.");
            var previousPhase = 0;
            for (var i = 0; i < cells.Count; i++)
            {
                var cell = cells[i];
                if (cell == null) { errors.Add($"Route entry {i} is missing."); continue; }
                if (!visited.Add(cell)) errors.Add($"Route repeats {cell.name}.");
                if (!grid.Contains(cell)) errors.Add($"{cell.name}: route cell is outside its BattleGrid.");
                if (!cell.IsRouteSurface || !grid.HasClearTop(cell)) errors.Add($"{cell.name}: route surface is not passable.");
                var expected = i == 0 ? CellKind.Spawn : i == cells.Count - 1 ? CellKind.Goal : CellKind.Road;
                if (requiredWidth == 1 || i == 0 || i == cells.Count - 1)
                {
                    if (cell.Kind != expected) errors.Add($"Route entry {i} must be {expected}.");
                }
                else
                {
                    var phase = cell.Kind == CellKind.Spawn ? 0 : cell.Kind == CellKind.Road ? 1 : 2;
                    if (phase < previousPhase) errors.Add($"Route entry {i}: Spawn/Road/Goal order is invalid.");
                    previousPhase = phase;
                }
                if (requiredWidth == 3)
                    for (var z = -1; z <= 1; z++)
                    for (var x = -1; x <= 1; x++)
                    {
                        var key = cell.Occupancy + new Vector3Int(x, 0, z);
                        if (!grid.TryGetCell(key, out var neighbor) || !neighbor.IsRouteSurface || !grid.HasClearTop(neighbor))
                            errors.Add($"Formation clearance blocked at {key} around route entry {i}.");
                    }
                if (i == 0 || cells[i - 1] == null) continue;
                var offset = cell.Occupancy - cells[i - 1].Occupancy;
                if (offset.y != 0 || Mathf.Abs(offset.x) + Mathf.Abs(offset.z) != 1)
                    errors.Add($"Route disconnected or unequal height between entries {i - 1} and {i}.");
                else if (requiredWidth == 3)
                    for (var slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++)
                        foreach (var swept in FormationClearance.SweptCells(cells[i - 1].TopCenter + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot),
                            cell.TopCenter + SquadCombatRules.Offset(slot)))
                            if (!grid.TryGetCell(swept, out var support) || !support.IsRouteSurface || !grid.HasClearTop(support))
                                errors.Add($"Formation clearance blocked along slot {slot} trajectory, segment {i - 1}, cell {swept}.");
            }
        }

        public Vector3[] CaptureTopCenters()
        {
            var points = new Vector3[cells.Count];
            for (var i = 0; i < points.Length; i++) points[i] = cells[i].TopCenter;
            return points;
        }
    }
}
.
=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\AnalyticsTests.cs === 
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Analytics;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class AnalyticsTests
    {
        private sealed class RecordingSink : IAnalyticsSink
        {
            public readonly List<AnalyticsEvent> Events = new List<AnalyticsEvent>();
            public void Emit(AnalyticsEvent value) => Events.Add(value);
            public List<AnalyticsEvent> Named(string name) => Events.Where(value => value.Name == name).ToList();
        }

        private sealed class ThrowingSink : IAnalyticsSink
        {
            public void Emit(AnalyticsEvent value) => throw new InvalidOperationException("sink failure");
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void ClearSink() => GameAnalytics.SetSink(null);

        [Test]
        public void JsonEscapesStringsAndKeepsUnicode()
        {
            var json = AnalyticsJson.Object(new[]
            {
                new KeyValuePair<string, object>("text", "a\"b\\c\nd\te\u0001f"),
                new KeyValuePair<string, object>("unicode", "\u00e9\u6f22\u2028"),
                new KeyValuePair<string, object>("empty", null)
            });
            Assert.That(json, Is.EqualTo("{\"text\":\"a\\\"b\\\\c\\nd\\te\\u0001f\",\"unicode\":\"\u00e9\u6f22\\u2028\",\"empty\":null}"));
            Assert.That(AnalyticsJson.Batch(new[] { "{\"a\":1}", "{\"b\":2}" }), Is.EqualTo("{\"events\":[{\"a\":1},{\"b\":2}]}"));
        }

        [Test]
        public void JsonNumbersUseInvariantCultureAndRounding()
        {
            var previous = Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture;
            try
            {
                Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = new CultureInfo("de-DE");
                var json = AnalyticsJson.Object(new[]
                {
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("f", 1.5f),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("d", 1234.56789),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("tiny", 0.0004),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("neg", -2.25),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("nan", float.NaN),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("i", 42),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("big", 9000000000L),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("b", true),
                    new KeyValuePair<string, object>("e", UnitRole.Tank)
                });
                Assert.That(json, Is.EqualTo("{\"f\":1.5,\"d\":1234.568,\"tiny\":0,\"neg\":-2.25,\"nan\":null,\"i\":42,\"big\":9000000000,\"b\":true,\"e\":\"Tank\"}"));
            }
            finally { Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = previous; }
        }

        [Test]
        public void FacadeNeverThrowsAndAddsCommonFields()
        {
            GameAnalytics.SetSink(null);
            Assert.That(GameAnalytics.IsActive, Is.False);
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => GameAnalytics.Track("ignored", ("a", 1)));
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => GameAnalytics.TrackBattle("ignored"));
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => GameAnalytics.Track(null, null));

            GameAnalytics.SetSink(new ThrowingSink());
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => GameAnalytics.Track("broken", ("a", 1)));

            var sink = new RecordingSink();
            GameAnalytics.SetSink(sink);
            GameAnalytics.SetLevelContext(3, 2);
            GameAnalytics.Track("probe", ("value", 7), (null, 1), ("value", 8));
            GameAnalytics.Track("probe");
            Assert.That(sink.Events.Count, Is.EqualTo(2));
            var first = sink.Events[0];
            foreach (var key in new[] { "event", "ts", "player_id", "session_id", "run_id", "build", "platform", "is_editor",
                "is_test_run", "scene", "level", "attempt", "seq" })
                Assert.That(first.TryGet(key, out _), Is.True, key);
            Assert.That(first["event"], Is.EqualTo("probe"));
            Assert.That(first["value"], Is.EqualTo(8), "A repeated key overwrites instead of duplicating a column.");
            Assert.That(first.Fields.Count(field => field.Key == "value"), Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(first["level"], Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(first["attempt"], Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(first["is_editor"], Is.True);
            Assert.That((long)sink.Events[1]["seq"], Is.EqualTo((long)first["seq"] + 1));
            Assert.That(first.ToJson(), Does.StartWith("{\"event\":\"probe\",\"ts\":\""));
            Assert.DoesNotThrow(() => DateTime.Parse((string)first["ts"], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
            GameAnalytics.SetLevelContext(0, 0);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SettingsAssetShipsWithoutAnEndpoint()
        {
            var settings = Resources.Load<AnalyticsSettings>(AnalyticsSettings.ResourceName);
            Assert.That(settings, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(settings.Enabled, Is.True);
            Assert.That(settings.EndpointUrl, Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(settings.SendFromEditor, Is.False);
        }

        [Test]
        public void VictoryAttemptReportsOneLevelEndAndTowerRows()
        {
            var sink = new RecordingSink();
            GameAnalytics.SetSink(sink);
            var definition = Definition(new TowerDefinition(1, new Vector3(5, 0, 30), 1, 1, 1, TargetRule.Nearest, maxHp: 30),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain)),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(2, 1, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Attack, UnitRarity.Rare, 3)));
            var simulation = new BattleSimulation(definition);
            var tracker = new BattleAnalyticsTracker(simulation, RouteLength(definition), 1, false);
            Run(simulation, tracker);
            tracker.Finish(45, 3, false);
            tracker.Finish(45, 3, false);

            Assert.That(simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
            var start = sink.Named("level_start").Single();
            Assert.That(start["squad_size"], Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(start["roles"], Is.EqualTo("Attack:1|Captain:1"));
            Assert.That(start["slots"], Is.EqualTo("1:Attack|4:Captain"));
            Assert.That(start["captain_present"], Is.True);
            Assert.That(start["max_rarity"], Is.EqualTo("Rare"));
            Assert.That(start["tower_count"], Is.EqualTo(1));
            var end = sink.Named("level_end").Single();
            Assert.That(sink.Events.IndexOf(start), Is.LessThan(sink.Events.IndexOf(end)));
            Assert.That(end["outcome"], Is.EqualTo("victory"));
            Assert.That(end["killed"], Is.EqualTo(0));
            Assert.That(end["reached_goal"], Is.EqualTo(simulation.ReachedGoalCount));
            Assert.That(end["survivors"], Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(end["gold_reward"], Is.EqualTo(45));
            Assert.That(end["furthest_progress_pct"], Is.EqualTo(100f));
            Assert.That((double)end["sim_s_at_1x"], Is.EqualTo(simulation.ElapsedTime).Within(1e-6));
            Assert.That(sink.Named("unit_death"), Is.Empty);
            var tower = sink.Named("tower_stats").Single();
            Assert.That(tower["tower_id"], Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(tower["destroyed"], Is.False);
            Assert.That(tower["destroyed_at_s"], Is.EqualTo(-1));
            Assert.That(tower["kills"], Is.EqualTo(0));
        }

        [Test]
        public void DefeatAttemptAttributesEveryDeathToTheKillingTower()
        {
            var sink = new RecordingSink();
            GameAnalytics.SetSink(sink);
            var definition = Definition(new TowerDefinition(1, new Vector3(5, 0, 2), 4, 1000, 0.2f, TargetRule.Nearest, maxHp: 100000),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Tank)),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(2, 3, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Heal)));
            var simulation = new BattleSimulation(definition);
            var tracker = new BattleAnalyticsTracker(simulation, RouteLength(definition), 2, false);
            Run(simulation, tracker);
            tracker.Finish(0, 1, false);

            Assert.That(simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Defeat));
            var deaths = sink.Named("unit_death");
            Assert.That(deaths.Count, Is.EqualTo(simulation.KilledCount));
            Assert.That(deaths.Count, Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(deaths.All(value => (int)value["killer_tower_id"] == 1), Is.True);
            Assert.That(deaths.All(value => (float)value["progress_pct"] > 0 && (float)value["progress_pct"] < 100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(deaths.Select(value => value["role"]), Is.EquivalentTo(new[] { "Tank", "Heal" }));
            var end = sink.Named("level_end").Single();
            Assert.That(end["outcome"], Is.EqualTo("defeat"));
            Assert.That(end["killed"], Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That(end["survivors"], Is.EqualTo(0));
            Assert.That(end["squad_hp_left_pct"], Is.EqualTo(0f));
            Assert.That((float)end["damage_taken"], Is.EqualTo(simulation.Units.Sum(unit => unit.MaxHp)).Within(1e-3));
            Assert.That((float)end["furthest_progress_pct"], Is.GreaterThan(0).And.LessThan(100));
            Assert.That(end["lives_left"], Is.EqualTo(1));
            var tower = sink.Named("tower_stats").Single();
            Assert.That(tower["kills"], Is.EqualTo(2));
            Assert.That((float)tower["damage_dealt"], Is.EqualTo((float)end["damage_taken"]).Within(1e-3));
            Assert.That(sink.Named("tower_stats").Count, Is.EqualTo(simulation.Towers.Count));
        }

        [Test]
        public void AbandonedAttemptReportsOnceAndStopsListening()
        {
            var sink = new RecordingSink();
            GameAnalytics.SetSink(sink);
            var definition = Definition(new TowerDefinition(1, new Vector3(5, 0, 2), 4, 1000, 0.2f, TargetRule.Nearest, maxHp: 100000),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Tank)));
            var simulation = new BattleSimulation(definition);
            var tracker = new BattleAnalyticsTracker(simulation, RouteLength(definition), 1, false);
            simulation.Step(0.02f);
            tracker.Abandon();
            tracker.Finish(0, 3, false);
            for (var i = 0; i < 5000 && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) simulation.Step(0.02f);
            Assert.That(sink.Named("level_abandon").Count, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(sink.Named("level_end"), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(sink.Named("unit_death"), Is.Empty);
        }

        [Test]
        public void MovedMembersCountsOnlyOccupiedSlotsThatChange()
        {
            var definition = Definition(new TowerDefinition(1, new Vector3(5, 0, 30), 1, 1, 1, TargetRule.Nearest),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain)),
                new MemberSpawnDefinition(2, 1, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Tank)));
            var simulation = new BattleSimulation(definition);
            simulation.Step(0.02f);
            var identity = Enumerable.Range(0, 9).ToArray();
            Assert.That(BattleAnalytics.MovedMembers(simulation, identity), Is.Zero);
            var swap = identity.ToArray();
            swap[4] = 7; swap[7] = 4;
            Assert.That(BattleAnalytics.MovedMembers(simulation, swap), Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(BattleAnalytics.MovedMembers(simulation, null), Is.Zero);
        }

        private static BattleDefinition Definition(TowerDefinition tower, params MemberSpawnDefinition[] members) =>
            new BattleDefinition(new[] { Vector3.zero, new Vector3(10, 0, 0) }, new[] { tower }, members);

        private static float RouteLength(BattleDefinition definition)
        {
            var length = 0f;
            for (var i = 1; i < definition.Waypoints.Count; i++) length += Vector3.Distance(definition.Waypoints[i - 1], definition.Waypoints[i]);
            return length;
        }

        private static void Run(BattleSimulation simulation, BattleAnalyticsTracker tracker)
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < 5000 && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++)
            {
                simulation.Step(0.02f);
                tracker.Sample(1);
            }
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\LevelAuthoringStageOneTests.cs === 
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class LevelAuthoringStageOneTests
    {
        private string folder;
        private SceneSetup[] setup;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            setup = EditorSceneManager.GetSceneManagerSetup();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            folder = "Assets/__LevelAuthoringStage1_" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            AssetDatabase.CreateFolder("Assets", Path.GetFileName(folder));
            Undo.ClearAll();
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            Undo.ClearAll();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Single);
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(folder) && folder.StartsWith("Assets/__LevelAuthoringStage1_", StringComparison.Ordinal))
                AssetDatabase.DeleteAsset(folder);
            // A headless Test Runner can start with no loaded/saved scene setup.
            if (setup.Any(s => s.isLoaded && s.isActive && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s.path)))
                EditorSceneManager.RestoreSceneManagerSetup(setup);
        }

        private Scene OpenCopy(string name = "LevelA")
        {
            var asset = LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(folder + "/" + name + ".unity");
            return EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(asset));
        }

        [Test]
        public void CopyCreatesIndependentGuidWithoutChangingExistingScenesOrBuildList()
        {
            var template = File.ReadAllBytes(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath);
            var test = File.Exists(LevelTemplateUtility.ReservedTestScenePath) ? File.ReadAllBytes(LevelTemplateUtility.ReservedTestScenePath) : null;
            var builds = EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Select(s => s.path + "|" + s.enabled).ToArray();
            var a = LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(folder + "/LevelA.unity");
            var b = LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(folder + "/LevelB.unity");
            var guids = new[] { LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath, AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(a), AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(b) }
                .Select(path => AssetDatabase.AssetPathToGUID(path)).ToArray();
            Assert.That(guids.Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(File.ReadAllBytes(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath), Is.EqualTo(template));
            if (test != null) Assert.That(File.ReadAllBytes(LevelTemplateUtility.ReservedTestScenePath), Is.EqualTo(test));
            Assert.That(EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Select(s => s.path + "|" + s.enabled), Is.EqualTo(builds));
        }

        [Test]
        public void CopyRefusesExistingTemplateTestLevelAndOutsideAssetsDestinations()
        {
            var path = folder + "/Existing.unity";
            LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(path);
            var bytes = File.ReadAllBytes(path);
            Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(path));
            Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath));
            Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(LevelTemplateUtility.ReservedTestScenePath));
            Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/../Scenes/TestLevel.unity"));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate("Assets/../../Outside.unity"));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(folder + "/Wrong.asset"));
            Assert.That(File.ReadAllBytes(path), Is.EqualTo(bytes));
        }

        [Test]
        public void TemplateAndCopyValidateWithoutMutatingOrStartingSession()
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath);
            var dirty = scene.isDirty;
            Assert.That(LevelValidation.Validate(scene).Where(i => i.IsError), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(scene.isDirty, Is.EqualTo(dirty));
            Assert.That(LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(scene).Single().Simulation, Is.Null);
            var copy = OpenCopy();
            Assert.That(LevelValidation.Validate(copy).Where(i => i.IsError), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(copy.isDirty, Is.False);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TemplateCopyPreservesSourceCameraAndCompletesItsBattle()
        {
            var sourceScene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);
            var sourceSession = LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(sourceScene).Single();
            var definition = sourceSession.CaptureDefinition();
            var camera = LevelValidation.Components<Camera>(sourceScene).Single();
            var position = camera.transform.position;
            var rotation = camera.transform.rotation;
            var lens = (camera.orthographic, camera.orthographicSize, camera.fieldOfView, camera.nearClipPlane, camera.farClipPlane);
            var copy = OpenCopy();
            camera = LevelValidation.Components<Camera>(copy).Single();
            Assert.That(camera.transform.position, Is.EqualTo(position));
            Assert.That(camera.transform.rotation, Is.EqualTo(rotation));
            Assert.That((camera.orthographic, camera.orthographicSize, camera.fieldOfView, camera.nearClipPlane, camera.farClipPlane), Is.EqualTo(lens));
            var session = LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(copy).Single();
            var copied = session.CaptureDefinition();
            Assert.That(copied.Waypoints, Is.EqualTo(definition.Waypoints));
            Assert.That(copied.Towers.Select(t => (t.Position, t.Area, t.Range, t.Damage, t.AttackInterval, t.MaxHp, t.FootprintSize)),
                Is.EqualTo(definition.Towers.Select(t => (t.Position, t.Area, t.Range, t.Damage, t.AttackInterval, t.MaxHp, t.FootprintSize))));
            var simulation = new BattleSimulation(copied);
            for (var i = 0; i < 10000 && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) simulation.Step(.02f);
            Assert.That(simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation, Is.Null);
        }

        [Test]
        public void LevelNumberSupportsUndoRedoAndCannotEditTemplate()
        {
            var scene = OpenCopy();
            var session = LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(scene).Single();
            var before = session.DisplayedLevelNumber;
            LevelEditScope.SetDisplayedLevelNumber(session, 9);
            Assert.That(session.DisplayedLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(9));
            Undo.PerformUndo();
            Assert.That(session.DisplayedLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(before));
            Undo.PerformRedo();
            Assert.That(session.DisplayedLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(9));
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath);
            session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => LevelEditScope.SetDisplayedLevelNumber(session, 9));
        }

        [Test]
        public void GroupedCreationDeletionAndRollbackAreAtomic()
        {
            var scene = SceneManager.GetActiveScene();
            using (var edit = new LevelEditScope(scene, "Create pair"))
            {
                edit.RegisterCreated(new GameObject("Stage1 A"));
                edit.RegisterCreated(new GameObject("Stage1 B"));
                edit.Commit();
            }
            Undo.PerformUndo();
            Assert.That(GameObject.Find("Stage1 A"), Is.Null);
            Assert.That(GameObject.Find("Stage1 B"), Is.Null);
            Undo.PerformRedo();
            Assert.That(GameObject.Find("Stage1 A"), Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(GameObject.Find("Stage1 B"), Is.Not.Null);
            using (var edit = new LevelEditScope(scene, "Delete pair"))
            {
                edit.Destroy(GameObject.Find("Stage1 A"));
                edit.Destroy(GameObject.Find("Stage1 B"));
                edit.Commit();
            }
            Undo.PerformUndo();
            var original = GameObject.Find("Stage1 A");
            Assert.That(original, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(GameObject.Find("Stage1 B"), Is.Not.Null);
            using (var edit = new LevelEditScope(scene, "Canceled edit"))
            {
                edit.Record(original);
                original.name = "Changed";
                edit.RegisterCreated(new GameObject("Stage1 C"));
                // No Commit: rollback restores the entire action.
            }
            Assert.That(original.name, Is.EqualTo("Stage1 A"));
            Assert.That(GameObject.Find("Stage1 C"), Is.Null);
        }

        [Test]
        public void UndoRejectsSharedAssetsAndObjectsInAnotherScene()
        {
            var scene = SceneManager.GetActiveScene();
            EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, folder + "/ScopeA.unity");
            var other = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene, NewSceneMode.Additive);
            var foreign = new GameObject("Foreign");
            SceneManager.MoveGameObjectToScene(foreign, other);
            using (var edit = new LevelEditScope(scene, "Scoped edit"))
            {
                Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => edit.Record(foreign));
                Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => edit.Record(AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<SceneAsset>(LevelTemplateUtility.TemplateScenePath)));
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void BasicValidationFindsUnregisteredTowersAndBrokenRoute()
        {
            var scene = OpenCopy();
            var session = LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(scene).Single();
            var extra = new GameObject("Unregistered Tower").AddComponent<BattleTower>();
            var issues = LevelValidation.Validate(scene);
            Assert.That(issues.Any(i => i.IsError && i.Context == extra && i.Message.Contains("not registered")), Is.True);
            Object.DestroyImmediate(extra.gameObject);
            var route = new SerializedObject(session.Route);
            route.FindProperty("cells").ClearArray();
            route.ApplyModifiedPropertiesWithoutUndo();
            Assert.That(LevelValidation.Validate(scene).Any(i => i.IsError && i.Message.Contains("SceneRoute")), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void TargetReferenceResolvesRenamedAssetWithoutSelectingOrModifyingIt()
        {
            var asset = LevelTemplateUtility.CreateLevelFromTemplate(folder + "/Original.unity");
            Assert.That(LevelSceneReference.TryFromAsset(asset, out var reference), Is.True);
            var storedGuid = reference.SceneGuid;
            Assert.That(AssetDatabase.MoveAsset(folder + "/Original.unity", folder + "/Renamed.unity"), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(reference.TryResolvePath(out var path), Is.True);
            Assert.That(path, Is.EqualTo(folder + "/Renamed.unity"));
            Assert.That(reference.SceneGuid, Is.EqualTo(storedGuid));
            Assert.That(default(LevelSceneReference).TryResolvePath(out _), Is.False);
        }
    }
}
