=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\MapBuilderTests.cs === 
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Editor.LevelAuthoring;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class MapBuilderTests
    {
        private MapDraft draft;
        private string folder;
        private SceneSetup[] setup;

        [SetUp]
        public void SetUp()
        {
            setup = EditorSceneManager.GetSceneManagerSetup();
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);
            draft = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<MapDraft>(); draft.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
            draft.ResetGrid(9, 6);
            folder = "Assets/__MapBuilderTests_" + Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N");
            AssetDatabase.CreateFolder("Assets", Path.GetFileName(folder));
        }
        [TearDown]
        public void Close()
        {
            Undo.ClearAll();
            Object.DestroyImmediate(draft);
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);
            AssetDatabase.DeleteAsset(folder);
            if (setup.Any(s => s.isLoaded && s.isActive && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(s.path))) EditorSceneManager.RestoreSceneManagerSetup(setup);
        }
        private void BuildValidMap()
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < draft.tiles.Length; i++) draft.tiles[i] = (int)CellKind.Ground;
            draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(1,1), (int)CellKind.Spawn, 3);
            draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(4,1), (int)CellKind.Goal, 3);
            draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(7,1), (int)CellKind.TowerPlatform, 3);
            draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(1,1)); draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(4,1));
        }
        private GameObject Tower => AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameObject>(MapBuilderUtility.Kit + "Towers/Tower_Attack_1x1_Square.prefab");

        [Test]
        public void MacroBrushBoundsRouteExpansionAndUndoAreAtomic()
        {
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => draft.ResetGrid(2,6));
            Undo.RegisterCompleteObjectUndo(draft, "Paint test");
            draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(1,1), (int)CellKind.Spawn, 3);
            Assert.That(draft.tiles.Count(value => value == (int)CellKind.Spawn), Is.EqualTo(9));
            Undo.PerformUndo(); Assert.That(draft.tiles.All(value => value == -1), Is.True);
            Undo.PerformRedo(); Assert.That(draft.tiles.Count(value => value == (int)CellKind.Spawn), Is.EqualTo(9));
            BuildValidMap();
            Assert.That(draft.route, Is.EqualTo(new[] { new Vector2Int(1,1), new Vector2Int(2,1), new Vector2Int(3,1), new Vector2Int(4,1) }));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(1,1)));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => draft.AppendRoute(new Vector2Int(5,2)));
            Assert.That(draft.route.Count, Is.EqualTo(4));
        }

        [Test]
        public void TowerNeedsSupportAndCannotOverlapOrHideClearanceErrors()
        {
            BuildValidMap();
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => draft.PlaceTower(new Vector2Int(4,1), Tower, AttackArea.Square));
            draft.PlaceTower(new Vector2Int(7,1), Tower, AttackArea.Square);
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => draft.PlaceTower(new Vector2Int(7,1), Tower, AttackArea.Square));
            Assert.That(MapBuilderUtility.Validate(draft), Is.Empty);
            draft.Paint(new Vector2Int(2,0), (int)CellKind.Obstacle, 1);
            Assert.That(MapBuilderUtility.Validate(draft).Any(message => message.Contains("clearance")), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void ValidationDoesNotModifyActiveSceneOrBuildSettings()
        {
            BuildValidMap();
            var active = SceneManager.GetActiveScene();
            var build = File.ReadAllBytes("ProjectSettings/EditorBuildSettings.asset");
            Assert.That(MapBuilderUtility.Validate(draft), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene(), Is.EqualTo(active));
            Assert.That(active.rootCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(File.ReadAllBytes("ProjectSettings/EditorBuildSettings.asset"), Is.EqualTo(build));
        }

        [Test]
        public void GeneratedCameraFitsWideGridWithoutChangingRigAsset()
        {
            draft.ResetGrid(96,6); BuildValidMap();
            var rigPath = MapBuilderUtility.Kit + "Systems/BattleRig_3D.prefab";
            var before = File.ReadAllBytes(rigPath);
            var generated = MapBuilderUtility.Generate(draft);
            var path = folder + "/WideCamera.unity";
            MapBuilderUtility.SaveNew(generated,path);
            var reopened = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(path);
            {
                var camera = LevelValidation.Components<Camera>(reopened).Single();
                camera.aspect = 16f/9;
                foreach (var x in new[] { -.5f, 95.5f })
                foreach (var z in new[] { -.5f, 5.5f })
                foreach (var y in new[] { -.5f, 3.5f })
                {
                    var point = camera.WorldToViewportPoint(new Vector3(x,y,z));
                    Assert.That(point.x, Is.InRange(0f,1f)); Assert.That(point.y, Is.InRange(0f,1f));
                    Assert.That(point.z, Is.InRange(camera.nearClipPlane,camera.farClipPlane));
                }
            }
            Assert.That(File.ReadAllBytes(rigPath), Is.EqualTo(before));
        }

        [Test]
        public void GeneratedSceneSavesReopensAndReadsBackWithActualCellReferences()
        {
            BuildValidMap(); draft.PlaceTower(new Vector2Int(7,1), Tower, AttackArea.Square);
            var scene = MapBuilderUtility.Generate(draft);
            var path = folder + "/Generated.unity";
            MapBuilderUtility.SaveNew(scene,path);
            Assert.Throws<IOException>(() => MapBuilderUtility.SaveNew(scene,path));
            Assert.Throws<InvalidOperationException>(() => MapBuilderUtility.SaveNew(scene,LevelTemplateUtility.ReservedTestScenePath));
            EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);
            scene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(path);
            var session = LevelValidation.Components<BattleSession>(scene).Single();
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Length, Is.EqualTo(54));
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells.All(cell => session.Grid.SceneCells.Contains(cell)), Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Towers.Count, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(session.Towers[0].FootprintSize, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(LevelValidation.Validate(scene).Where(issue => issue.IsError), Is.Empty);
            var copy = MapBuilderUtility.Read(scene);
            try
            {
                Assert.That(copy.tiles, Is.EqualTo(draft.tiles)); Assert.That(copy.route, Is.EqualTo(draft.route));
                Assert.That(copy.towers.Single().area, Is.EqualTo(AttackArea.Square));
                Assert.That(MapBuilderUtility.Validate(copy), Is.Empty);
            }
            finally { Object.DestroyImmediate(copy); }
            var simulation = new BattleSimulation(session.CaptureDefinition());
            for (var i = 0; i < 2000 && simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) simulation.Step(.02f);
            Assert.That(simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\EditMode\UpgradePreparationTests.cs === 
using System;
using System.Linq;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEngine;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class UpgradePreparationTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void SummonPurchasesChosenUnitAndPreservesOfferUntilPurchase()
        {
            var initial = new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(10, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain)) };
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(initial);
            var wallet = new CampaignWallet(100);
            var shop = new ClaimCardShop(wallet, preparation, 3, 1);
            var offer = shop.BeginOffer().ToArray();
            Assert.That(offer.Length, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(offer.All(unit => unit.Role != UnitRole.Captain && unit.Role != UnitRole.Test), Is.True);
            Assert.That(offer.All(unit => unit.Level >= 1 && unit.Level <= 2), Is.True);
            Assert.That(shop.BeginOffer().ToArray(), Is.EqualTo(offer));
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(100));
            Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(-1), Is.False);
            Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(3), Is.False);
            Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(2), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(97));
            Assert.That(preparation.Members[1].Unit, Is.SameAs(offer[2]));
            Assert.That(preparation.Members[1].MemberId, Is.EqualTo(11));
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(initial.Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(shop.NextPrice, Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(0), Is.False, "A purchase consumes its offer.");
            shop.BeginOffer();
            Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(0), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(93));
        }

        [Test]
        public void UnlimitedSummonsCanExceedOldQuotaButRespectWarehouseCapacity()
        {
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain)) });
            var wallet = new CampaignWallet(unlimited: true);
            var shop = new ClaimCardShop(wallet, preparation, 3, 1);
            for (var i = 1; i < SquadPreparation.WarehouseCapacity; i++)
            {
                Assert.That(shop.BeginOffer().Count, Is.EqualTo(3));
                Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(0), Is.True);
            }
            Assert.That(shop.SummonsCompleted, Is.EqualTo(11));
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(int.MaxValue));
            Assert.That(preparation.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(12));
            Assert.That(shop.BeginOffer(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(0), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.Zero);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SummonPoolAdvancesWithStageProgressAndRejectsUnaffordablePurchases()
        {
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(1, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain)) });
            var shop = new ClaimCardShop(new CampaignWallet(2), preparation, 3, 1);
            Assert.That(shop.BeginOffer(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(shop.TrySummon(0), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(1));
            var funded = new ClaimCardShop(new CampaignWallet(100), preparation, 3, 1);
            funded.SetStageProgress(2);
            Assert.That(funded.GetRarityWeights(), Is.EqualTo((50, 35, 15, 0)));
            Assert.That(funded.BeginOffer().All(unit => unit.Level >= 2 && unit.Level <= 3), Is.True);
            funded.SetStageProgress(3);
            Assert.That(funded.GetRarityWeights(), Is.EqualTo((30, 40, 25, 5)));
            funded.SetStageProgress(4);
            funded.SetStageProgress(1);
            Assert.That(funded.HighestStageCleared, Is.EqualTo(4));
            Assert.That(funded.GetRarityWeights(), Is.EqualTo((15, 35, 35, 15)));
            Assert.That(funded.BeginOffer().All(unit => unit.Level >= 4 && unit.Level <= 5), Is.True);
        }

        [Test]
        public void UnlimitedWalletNeverDeductsAndDoesNotChangeNormalWalletRules()
        {
            var unlimited = new CampaignWallet(unlimited: true);
            var normal = new CampaignWallet(10);
            for (var i = 0; i < 100; i++) Assert.That(unlimited.TrySpend(int.MaxValue), Is.True);
            Assert.That(unlimited.IsUnlimited, Is.True);
            Assert.That(unlimited.Balance, Is.EqualTo(int.MaxValue));
            Assert.That(unlimited.TryRewardLevel(1, int.MaxValue), Is.True);
            Assert.That(unlimited.TryRewardLevel(1, int.MaxValue), Is.False);
            Assert.That(unlimited.Balance, Is.EqualTo(int.MaxValue));
            Assert.That(normal.IsUnlimited, Is.False);
            Assert.That(normal.TrySpend(10), Is.True);
            Assert.That(normal.TrySpend(1), Is.False);
            Assert.That(normal.Balance, Is.Zero);
            Assert.Throws<System.ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => unlimited.TrySpend(0));
        }

        [Test]
        public void StageGoldRewardsUseAuthoredValuesAndNewFallbackWithoutGrantingUnits()
        {
            var settings = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<GameEconomySettings>("Assets/Rebuild/Resources/RebuildEconomy.asset");
            Assert.That(settings.GetStageClearGold(1), Is.EqualTo(10));
            Assert.That(settings.GetStageClearGold(2), Is.EqualTo(100));
            Assert.That(settings.GetStageClearGold(3), Is.EqualTo(150));
            Assert.That(settings.GetStageClearGold(4), Is.EqualTo(200));
        }

        [Test]
        public void RewardMemberHasNewIdentityAndDoesNotMutateInventoryOrDeployment()
        {
            var original = new[] { new MemberSpawnDefinition(110, 4, SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Captain)) };
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(original);
            preparation.TryAssign(110, 4);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Tank), Is.True);
            Assert.That(original.Length, Is.EqualTo(1));
            Assert.That(preparation.Members.Select(member => member.MemberId), Is.EqualTo(new[] { 110, 111 }));
            Assert.That(preparation.MemberAtSlot(4), Is.EqualTo(110));
            Assert.That(preparation.IsDeployed(111), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(111, 7), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.CaptureDeployment().Single(member => member.MemberId == 111).Unit.Role, Is.EqualTo(UnitRole.Tank));
            for (var i = preparation.Members.Count; i < SquadPreparation.WarehouseCapacity; i++) preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Attack);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Heal), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(12));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => preparation.TryAddRewardMember(UnitRole.Test));
        }
        [Test]
        public void WalletRewardsEachLevelOnceAndPurchasesNeverOverdraw()
        {
            var wallet = new CampaignWallet();
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100), Is.False);
            Assert.That(wallet.TrySpend(40), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(60));
            Assert.That(wallet.TrySpend(61), Is.False);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(60));
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(2, 100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(160));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TrySpend(-1));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TrySpend(0));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TryRewardLevel(0, 100));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentOutOfRangeException>(() => wallet.TryRewardLevel(3, -1));
            Assert.That(new CampaignWallet().Balance, Is.Zero);
        }

        [Test]
        public void RewardOverflowDoesNotConsumeLevelReward()
        {
            var wallet = new CampaignWallet(int.MaxValue);
            Assert.Throws<OverflowException>(() => wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100));
            Assert.That(wallet.TrySpend(100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.TryRewardLevel(1, 100), Is.True);
            Assert.That(wallet.Balance, Is.EqualTo(int.MaxValue));
        }
        [TearDown] public void Close() => EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.EmptyScene);

        [Test]
        public void UpgradeHasNoMissingDependenciesAndSharesPrototypeRoster()
        {
            var scene = EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
            var upgrade = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            Assert.That(upgrade.Roster, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(upgrade.StartWaveButton, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(upgrade.transform.Find("GoldTXT"), Is.Null);
            foreach (var root in scene.GetRootGameObjects())
                Assert.That(root.GetComponentsInChildren<Component>(true).All(component => component != null), Is.True);
            var roster = upgrade.Roster;
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.PrototypeBattleScene);
            Assert.That(UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>().InitialRoster, Is.SameAs(roster));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SelectedWarehouseMembersKeepIdentityAndStatsInBattle()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.OpenScene(BattleSceneFlow.PrototypeBattleScene);
            var session = UnityEngine.Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var before = session.InitialRoster.CaptureMembers();
            var fixture = Enumerable.Range(1, 8).Select(id => new MemberSpawnDefinition(id, id - 1,
                SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Attack, UnitRarity.Epic, 3), .5f)).ToArray();
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(fixture);
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(preparation.CanStart, Is.False);
            var selected = preparation.Members.First(member => member.SlotId == 4);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(selected.MemberId, 8), Is.True);
            session.SetPreparedMembers(preparation.CaptureDeployment());
            var after = session.CaptureDefinition();
            var result = after.Members.Single();
            Assert.That((result.MemberId, result.SlotId), Is.EqualTo((selected.MemberId, 8)));
            Assert.That(result.Unit, Is.SameAs(selected.Unit));
            Assert.That(result.SpawnTime, Is.EqualTo(selected.SpawnTime));
            Assert.That(session.InitialRoster.CaptureMembers().Select(member => (member.MemberId, member.SlotId)),
                Is.EqualTo(before.Select(member => (member.MemberId, member.SlotId))));
            Assert.Throws<ArgumentException>(() => session.SetPreparedMembers(fixture.Take(7).ToArray()));
        }

        [Test]
        public void SixMemberCapAllowsMovesReplacementAndReturnWithoutDuplicates()
        {
            var fixture = Enumerable.Range(1, 8).Select(id => new MemberSpawnDefinition(id, id - 1,
                SquadCombatRules.Create(UnitRole.Attack))).ToArray();
            var preparation = new SquadPreparation(fixture);
            var available = preparation.Members;
            var changes = 0; preparation.Changed += () => changes++;
            for (var slot = 0; slot < 6; slot++) Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[slot].MemberId, slot), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[6].MemberId, 8), Is.False);
            Assert.That((preparation.DeployedCount, changes), Is.EqualTo((6, 6)));
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[0].MemberId, 8), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.MemberAtSlot(0), Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[6].MemberId, 8), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.IsDeployed(available[0].MemberId), Is.False);
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.EqualTo(6));
            Assert.That(preparation.TryRemove(8), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.DeployedCount, Is.EqualTo(5));
            Assert.That(preparation.TryAssign(available[7].MemberId, 7), Is.True);
            Assert.That(preparation.CaptureDeployment().Select(member => member.MemberId).Distinct().Count(), Is.EqualTo(6));
        }
    }
}

#if UNITY_EDITOR
using System.Collections;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class LevelTemplatePlayModeTests
    {
        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator TemplateStartsStandaloneAndFinishesWithoutChangingCameraOrEnteringCampaign()
        {
            EditorSceneManager.LoadSceneInPlayMode("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/LevelTemplate.unity", new LoadSceneParameters(LoadSceneMode.Single));
            yield return null;
            yield return null;
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var camera = Camera.main;
            var position = camera.transform.position;
            var rotation = camera.transform.rotation;
            Assert.That(session, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation, Is.Not.Null);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Objective, Is.EqualTo(BattleObjective.ReachGoal));
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<TestLevelControls>(), Is.Null);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsRunActive, Is.False);
            var cells = session.Grid.SceneCells;
            var firstId = cells[0].GetInstanceID();
            session.enabled = false;
            for (var i = 0; i < 10000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; i++) session.Advance(.02f);
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.Not.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo("Assets/Rebuild/Scenes/LevelTemplate.unity"));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells.Length, Is.EqualTo(cells.Length));
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells[0].GetInstanceID(), Is.EqualTo(firstId));
            Assert.That(camera.transform.position, Is.EqualTo(position));
            Assert.That(camera.transform.rotation, Is.EqualTo(rotation));
        }

        [UnityTearDown]
        public IEnumerator Close()
        {
            var old = SceneManager.GetActiveScene();
            var clean = SceneManager.CreateScene("Stage1 Play Cleanup");
            SceneManager.SetActiveScene(clean);
            yield return SceneManager.UnloadSceneAsync(old);
        }
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\Rebuild\Tests\PlayMode\TestSceneFlowTests.cs === 
using System.Collections;
using System.Reflection;
using NUnit.Framework;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation;
using Team5.Rebuild.Presentation.LegacyUI;
using Team5.Rebuild.Session;
using Team5.Rebuild.Simulation;
using Team5.Rebuild.World;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;
using UnityEngine.UI;
using Object = UnityEngine.Object;

namespace Team5.Rebuild.Tests
{
    public sealed class TestSceneFlowTests
    {
        private static IEnumerator WaitForScene(string path)
        {
            for (var frame = 0; frame < 600 && SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path != path; frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(path));
            yield return null;
        }

        private static IEnumerator OpenTestPreparation()
        {
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.TestStartScene);
            yield return null;
            Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Button>().onClick.Invoke();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.TestUpdateScene);
        }

        [UnityTearDown]
        public IEnumerator Close()
        {
            foreach (var flow in Object.FindObjectsByType<BattleSceneFlow>(FindObjectsSortMode.None)) Object.Destroy(flow.gameObject);
            yield return null;
            yield return SceneManager.LoadSceneAsync(BattleSceneFlow.StartScene);
            yield return null;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator TestTitlePreparationAndEmptyLevelStaySeparateFromCampaign()
        {
            yield return OpenTestPreparation();
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsTestRun, Is.True);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.HasUnlimitedGold, Is.True);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleScenePath, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene));
            var preparation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            Assert.That(preparation.GoldLabel.text, Does.Contain("UNLIMITED"));
            var gold = BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance;
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.TrySpendGold(int.MaxValue), Is.True);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.TrySpendGold(int.MaxValue), Is.True);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(gold));
            var member = preparation.Preparation.Members[0];
            Assert.That(preparation.Preparation.TryAssign(member.MemberId, 4), Is.True);
            preparation.StartWave();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene);
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            var tools = session.GetComponent<TestLevelControls>();
            Assert.That(session.Grid.SceneCells, Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Route.Cells, Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Towers, Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation, Is.Null);
            Assert.That(tools.ConfigurationMessage, Does.Contain("Map not configured"));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CompletedLevelNumber, Is.Zero);
            BattleSceneFlow.ContinueToUpgrade();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.TestUpdateScene);
            Assert.That(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>().Preparation.MemberAtSlot(4), Is.EqualTo(member.MemberId));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.SceneForLevel(4), Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.LevelFourScene));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator TestPreparationUsesNewSummonOverlayAndUnlimitedWallet()
        {
            yield return OpenTestPreparation();
            var preparation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            var count = preparation.Preparation.Members.Count;
            var price = BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.NextPrice;
            preparation.OpenClaimCards();
            yield return null;
            var overlay = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyClaimCardOverlay>();
            Assert.That(overlay, Is.Not.Null);
            var choices = overlay.GetComponentsInChildren<Button>();
            Assert.That(System.Array.FindAll(choices, button => button.name.StartsWith("Summon Option ")).Length, Is.EqualTo(3));
            Assert.That(System.Array.Exists(overlay.GetComponentsInChildren<TMPro.TMP_Text>(), label => label.text.Contains("UNLIMITED")), Is.True);
            var selected = BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.BeginOffer()[0];
            System.Array.Find(choices, button => button.name == "Summon Option 0").onClick.Invoke();
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(preparation.Preparation.Members.Count, Is.EqualTo(count + 1));
            Assert.That(preparation.Preparation.Members[count].Unit, Is.SameAs(selected));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(int.MaxValue));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.NextPrice, Is.EqualTo(price + Resources.Load<GameEconomySettings>("RebuildEconomy").SummonCostIncreasePerPull));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.HighestClearedLevel, Is.Zero);
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator CampaignVictoriesKeepNewRewardsAndAdvanceSummonPool()
        {
            BattleSceneFlow.BeginRun();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            var settings = Resources.Load<GameEconomySettings>("RebuildEconomy");
            var expectedGold = settings.StartingGold;
            for (var level = 1; level <= 2; level++)
            {
                var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
                session.enabled = false;
                // Exercise the scene-flow victory handler without changing the combat system.
                typeof(BattleSceneFlow).GetMethod("Settle", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)
                    .Invoke(Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSceneFlow>(), new object[] { BattleOutcome.Victory });
                yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.SettlementScene);
                expectedGold += settings.GetStageClearGold(level);
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsTestRun, Is.False);
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.HasUnlimitedGold, Is.False);
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(expectedGold));
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardGold, Is.EqualTo(settings.GetStageClearGold(level)));
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.HighestClearedLevel, Is.EqualTo(level));
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleLevelNumber, Is.EqualTo(level + 1));
                BattleSceneFlow.ContinueToUpgrade();
                yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.UpgradeScene);
                Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.HighestStageCleared, Is.EqualTo(level));
                if (level == 2)
                {
                    Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CardShop.GetRarityWeights(), Is.EqualTo((50, 35, 15, 0)));
                    break;
                }
                var preparation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
                var member = preparation.Preparation.Members[0];
                Assert.That(preparation.Preparation.TryAssign(member.MemberId, 4), Is.True);
                preparation.StartWave();
                yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.LevelTwoScene);
            }
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator StartingCampaignAfterTestRestoresConfiguredFiniteGold()
        {
            yield return OpenTestPreparation();
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.HasUnlimitedGold, Is.True);
            BattleSceneFlow.BeginRun();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.BattleScene);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.IsTestRun, Is.False);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.HasUnlimitedGold, Is.False);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(Resources.Load<GameEconomySettings>("RebuildEconomy").StartingGold));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator CompletedTestDoesNotAwardCampaignRewardsOrLeaveTestLevel()
        {
            yield return OpenTestPreparation();
            var preparation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            var member = preparation.Preparation.Members[0];
            preparation.Preparation.TryAssign(member.MemberId, 4);
            preparation.StartWave();
            yield return WaitForScene(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene);
            var session = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<BattleSession>();
            session.enabled = false;
            var gold = BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance;
            var cells = new BattleCell[6, 3];
            // Temporary test fixtures only; the saved TestLevel remains completely empty.
            for (var x = 0; x < 6; x++)
            for (var z = 0; z < 3; z++)
            {
                var cell = new GameObject("Test Cell").AddComponent<BattleCell>();
                cell.transform.SetParent(session.Grid.transform, false);
                // BattleCell validates world coordinates; the user's saved rig may be offset.
                cell.transform.position = new Vector3(x, 0, z);
                typeof(BattleCell).GetField("kind", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)
                    .SetValue(cell, x < 3 ? CellKind.Spawn : CellKind.Goal);
                cells[x, z] = cell;
            }
            typeof(SceneRoute).GetField("cells", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance)
                .SetValue(session.Route, new System.Collections.Generic.List<BattleCell> { cells[1,1], cells[2,1], cells[3,1], cells[4,1] });
            Assert.That(session.ValidateConfiguration(), Is.Empty);
            Assert.That(session.GetComponent<TestLevelControls>().RestartTest(), Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units.Count, Is.Zero);
            for (var tick = 0; tick < 1000 && session.Simulation.Outcome == BattleOutcome.Running; tick++) session.Advance(.02f);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Victory));
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Units[0].MemberId, Is.EqualTo(member.MemberId));
            yield return null;
            yield return null;
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.GoldBalance, Is.EqualTo(gold));
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.LastRewardGold, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.HighestClearedLevel, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.CompletedLevelNumber, Is.Zero);
            Assert.That(session.AttemptsRemaining, Is.EqualTo(BattleSession.DefaultLivesPerLevel));
            Assert.That(session.GetComponent<TestLevelControls>().RestartTest(), Is.True);
            Assert.That(session.Simulation.Outcome, Is.EqualTo(BattleOutcome.Running));
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator PreviewTargetsTestMapWithoutStartingItsToolsOrSimulation()
        {
            yield return OpenTestPreparation();
            var preparation = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyUpgradeScene>();
            preparation.OpenNextLevelPreview();
            var preview = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<LegacyNextLevelPreview>();
            Assert.That(preview, Is.Not.Null);
            for (var frame = 0; frame < 600 && !preview.LoadedScene.isLoaded; frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(preview.LoadedScene.path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene));
            foreach (var root in preview.LoadedScene.GetRootGameObjects())
            {
                var session = root.GetComponentInChildren<BattleSession>(true);
                if (session == null) continue;
                Assert.That(session.Simulation, Is.Null);
                Assert.That(session.GetComponent<TestLevelControls>().ConfigurationMessage, Is.Null);
            }
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetActiveScene().path, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.TestUpdateScene));
            preview.Close();
            for (var frame = 0; frame < 600 && SceneManager.GetSceneByPath(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene).isLoaded; frame++) yield return null;
            Assert.That(SceneManager.GetSceneByPath(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene).isLoaded, Is.False);
            Assert.That(BattleSceneFlow.NextBattleScenePath, Is.EqualTo(BattleSceneFlow.TestLevelScene));
        }
    }
}

﻿using System;
using UnityEngine;

public class Readme : ScriptableObject
{
    public Texture2D icon;
    public string title;
    public Section[] sections;
    public bool loadedLayout;

    [Serializable]
    public class Section
    {
        public string heading, text, linkText, url;
    }
}

=== FILE: C:\Users\WINSTON\Documents\school\Game Design\Prototype\ReverseTD\Assets\TutorialInfo\Scripts\Editor\ReadmeEditor.cs === 
﻿using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEditor;
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Reflection;

[CustomEditor(typeof(Readme))]
[InitializeOnLoad]
public class ReadmeEditor : Editor
{
    static string s_ShowedReadmeSessionStateName = "ReadmeEditor.showedReadme";
    
    static string s_ReadmeSourceDirectory = "Assets/TutorialInfo";

    const float k_Space = 16f;

    static ReadmeEditor()
    {
        EditorApplication.delayCall += SelectReadmeAutomatically;
    }

    static void RemoveTutorial()
    {
        if (EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Remove Readme Assets",
            
            $"All contents under {s_ReadmeSourceDirectory} will be removed, are you sure you want to proceed?",
            "Proceed",
            "Cancel"))
        {
            if (Directory.Exists(s_ReadmeSourceDirectory))
            {
                FileUtil.DeleteFileOrDirectory(s_ReadmeSourceDirectory);
                FileUtil.DeleteFileOrDirectory(s_ReadmeSourceDirectory + ".meta");
            }
            else
            {
                Debug.Log($"Could not find the Readme folder at {s_ReadmeSourceDirectory}");
            }

            var readmeAsset = SelectReadme();
            if (readmeAsset != null)
            {
                var path = AssetDatabase.GetAssetPath(readmeAsset);
                FileUtil.DeleteFileOrDirectory(path + ".meta");
                FileUtil.DeleteFileOrDirectory(path);
            }

            AssetDatabase.Refresh();
        }
    }

    static void SelectReadmeAutomatically()
    {
        if (!SessionState.GetBool(s_ShowedReadmeSessionStateName, false))
        {
            var readme = SelectReadme();
            SessionState.SetBool(s_ShowedReadmeSessionStateName, true);

            if (readme && !readme.loadedLayout)
            {
                LoadLayout();
                readme.loadedLayout = true;
            }
        }
    }

    static void LoadLayout()
    {
        var assembly = typeof(EditorApplication).Assembly;
        var windowLayoutType = assembly.GetType("UnityEditor.WindowLayout", true);
        var method = windowLayoutType.GetMethod("LoadWindowLayout", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static);
        method.Invoke(null, new object[] { Path.Combine(Application.dataPath, "TutorialInfo/Layout.wlt"), false });
    }

    static Readme SelectReadme()
    {
        var ids = AssetDatabase.FindAssets("Readme t:Readme");
        if (ids.Length == 1)
        {
            var readmeObject = AssetDatabase.LoadMainAssetAtPath(AssetDatabase.GUIDToAssetPath(ids[0]));

            Selection.objects = new UnityEngine.Object[] { readmeObject };

            return (Readme)readmeObject;
        }
        else
        {
            Debug.Log("Couldn't find a readme");
            return null;
        }
    }

    protected override void OnHeaderGUI()
    {
        var readme = (Readme)target;
        Init();

        var iconWidth = Mathf.Min(EditorGUIUtility.currentViewWidth / 3f - 20f, 128f);

        GUILayout.BeginHorizontal("In BigTitle");
        {
            if (readme.icon != null)
            {
                GUILayout.Space(k_Space);
                GUILayout.Label(readme.icon, GUILayout.Width(iconWidth), GUILayout.Height(iconWidth));
            }
            GUILayout.Space(k_Space);
            GUILayout.BeginVertical();
            {

                GUILayout.FlexibleSpace();
                GUILayout.Label(readme.title, TitleStyle);
                GUILayout.FlexibleSpace();
            }
            GUILayout.EndVertical();
            GUILayout.FlexibleSpace();
        }
        GUILayout.EndHorizontal();
    }

    public override void OnInspectorGUI()
    {
        var readme = (Readme)target;
        Init();

        foreach (var section in readme.sections)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(section.heading))
            {
                GUILayout.Label(section.heading, HeadingStyle);
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(section.text))
            {
                GUILayout.Label(section.text, BodyStyle);
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(section.linkText))
            {
                if (LinkLabel(new GUIContent(section.linkText)))
                {
                    Application.OpenURL(section.url);
                }
            }

            GUILayout.Space(k_Space);
        }

        if (GUILayout.Button("Remove Readme Assets", ButtonStyle))
        {
            RemoveTutorial();
        }
    }

    bool m_Initialized;

    GUIStyle LinkStyle
    {
        get { return m_LinkStyle; }
    }

    [SerializeField]
    GUIStyle m_LinkStyle;

    GUIStyle TitleStyle
    {
        get { return m_TitleStyle; }
    }

    [SerializeField]
    GUIStyle m_TitleStyle;

    GUIStyle HeadingStyle
    {
        get { return m_HeadingStyle; }
    }

    [SerializeField]
    GUIStyle m_HeadingStyle;

    GUIStyle BodyStyle
    {
        get { return m_BodyStyle; }
    }

    [SerializeField]
    GUIStyle m_BodyStyle;

    GUIStyle ButtonStyle
    {
        get { return m_ButtonStyle; }
    }

    [SerializeField]
    GUIStyle m_ButtonStyle;

    void Init()
    {
        if (m_Initialized)
            return;
        m_BodyStyle = new GUIStyle(EditorStyles.label);
        m_BodyStyle.wordWrap = true;
        m_BodyStyle.fontSize = 14;
        m_BodyStyle.richText = true;

        m_TitleStyle = new GUIStyle(m_BodyStyle);
        m_TitleStyle.fontSize = 26;

        m_HeadingStyle = new GUIStyle(m_BodyStyle);
        m_HeadingStyle.fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold;
        m_HeadingStyle.fontSize = 18;

        m_LinkStyle = new GUIStyle(m_BodyStyle);
        m_LinkStyle.wordWrap = false;

        // Match selection color which works nicely for both light and dark skins
        m_LinkStyle.normal.textColor = new Color(0x00 / 255f, 0x78 / 255f, 0xDA / 255f, 1f);
        m_LinkStyle.stretchWidth = false;

        m_ButtonStyle = new GUIStyle(EditorStyles.miniButton);
        m_ButtonStyle.fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold;

        m_Initialized = true;
    }

    bool LinkLabel(GUIContent label, params GUILayoutOption[] options)
    {
        var position = GUILayoutUtility.GetRect(label, LinkStyle, options);

        Handles.BeginGUI();
        Handles.color = LinkStyle.normal.textColor;
        Handles.DrawLine(new Vector3(position.xMin, position.yMax), new Vector3(position.xMax, position.yMax));
        Handles.color = Color.white;
        Handles.EndGUI();

        EditorGUIUtility.AddCursorRect(position, MouseCursor.Link);

        return GUI.Button(position, label, LinkStyle);
    }
}
